Point Blank Shot<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: None.<br>Required for: Rapid Shot.<br>Specifics: A character with this feat negates the -4 penalty for using missile weapons in melee, and gains an additional +1 to attack and damage with ranged weapons when the target is within 15 feet. Warlocks gain a +1 bonus to attack with eldritch blasts.<br>Use: Automatic.

Shield Proficiency<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: None.<br>Specifics: A character with this feat has the training required to use a shield.

Wholeness of Body<br>
Type of feat: Class<br>Specifics: Restores a number of hit points equal to twice the number of the monk's class levels as well as providing 1 point of regeneration for 3 minutes if the character only has Monk levels. Wholeness of Body can be used 1 time for every 3 monks levels.<br>Use: Selected 

Defensive Roll<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Rogue level 10 or shadowdancer level 5.<br>Specifics: If the rogue is struck by a potentially lethal blow, they make a Reflex saving throw (DC = damage dealt). If succesful, they take only half damage from the blow.<br>Use: Automatic, but limited to ten uses per day. Flat-footed characters cannot make a defensive roll.

Inspiration<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bard.<br>Specifics: The very presence of a bard is a constant inspiration to his companions. Unlike bardic music, bardic inspirations are always active and do not have a limited number of uses, however, the bard may only use one inspiration at any given time. At 1st level, the bard can only inspire courage, but as he increases in level, he gains access to a variety of inspirations from which his companions (and himself) may benefit. There are seven types of inspirations:<br><br>* <b>Inspire Courage (1st level):</b> This gives a bonus to hit and damage. It starts at +1, and becomes +2 at 8th level, +3 at 14th level, +4 at 20th level, and +5 at 26th level.<br><br>* <b>Inspire Competence (2nd level):</b> This gives a skill bonus to all allies. It starts at +2 and becomes +4 at 11th level, +6 at 19th level, and +8 at 27th level.<br><br>* <b>Inspire Defense (5th level):</b> This gives a dodge AC bonus to all allies. It starts at +2 and becomes +3 at 10th level, +4 at 15th level, +5 at 20th level, +6 at 25th level, and +7 at 30th level.<br><br>* <b>Inspire Regeneration (7th level):</b> This heals all party members a certain number of hit points every round. This starts at 1 hit point per round, becomes 2 at 12th level, 3 at 17th level, 4 at 22nd level, and 5 at 27th level.<br><br>* <b>Inspire Toughness (8th level):</b> This gives a bonus to saving throws for all allies. It starts at +1, then +2 at 13th level, +3 at 18th level, +4 at 23rd level, and +5 at 28th level.<br><br>* <b>Inspire Slowing (11th level):</b> All enemies that come within 20 feet of the bard have to make a Will save (DC 13 + ½ Bard level + CHA modifier) otherwise they will be slowed down. Initially they are slowed down 15% but at 16th level they are slowed by 30%. There are no further penalties besides movement, unlike the <i>slow</i> spell.<br><br>* <b>Inspire Jarring (14th level):</b> All enemies within 20' of the bard suffer a -4 penalty to all Discipline and Concentration checks and -2 to all Will saves.<br><br>Use: Select the particular form of Inspiration to turn that power on (and turn off any Inspiration already in effect).

Barbarian Rage<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian.<br>Specifics: The barbarian may rage and the effects are determined by which style of rage they took at 1st level, standard or whirlwind frenzy.<br>Use: Selected. The barbarian may use this ability once per day at first level, +1 use per day at level 4, and every 4 levels thereafter.  The feat has a cooldown timer that lasts as long as the length of the rage and fatigue (if any). This is an instantly activated feat.

Elemental Shape<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid level 16.<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an elemental (a huge elemental at 16th level, and an elder elemental at 20th level). The ability may be used once per day at level 16, twice per day at level 18, three times per day at level 20, and one additional use for each additional two levels (to 8/day at 30th). The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose one of four elemental types (earth, air, fire or water) to become.

Wild Shape<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an animal at will. It may be used once per day at fifth level, twice per day at sixth level, three times per day at seventh level, four times per day at 10th level, five times per day at 14th level, and six times per day at 18th level. The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level. Available shapes include bear, wolf, boar and badger. At 12th level, the druid may assume the dire form of any of these animals.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose an animal form to take.<br>WARNING: Exporting a character, saving a game, and a persistent world saving your character will cause spell loss and remove all non-AC gear-based bonuses that transferred when you shifted. You will need to unshift-reshift to fix this problem. It is not a problem that the community can fix.

Strength Domain Powers<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Strength domain are able to boost their Strength with divine energy, and gain access to spells that make them stronger and more resilient.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Divine Strength: Once per day, the cleric may gain a bonus to Strength equal to 2 + 1 per 3 class levels. This effect has a duration of 5 rounds + the cleric's Charisma modifier.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>bull's strength (1), divine power (3), stoneskin (6)</i>.

Protection Domain Powers<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Protection domain are able to shield themselves from harm using their special abilities and spells.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Divine Protection: Once per day, the cleric is able to cast an improved <i>sanctuary</i> spell-like ability that sets the save DC at 10 + Charisma modifier + cleric level. The effect has a duration of 1 round per caster level + the cleric's Charisma modifier.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>lesser globe of invulnerability (4), energy immunity (5), Stoneskin (6), Mind Blank (8), Prismatic Spray (9).</i><br>

Death Domain Powers<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Death domain can summon an enhanced shadow and gains spells related to death and dying.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Negative Plane Avatar: Once per day, the cleric is able to summon a shadow that gains power as the cleric advances.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>death ward (3), phantasmal killer (4), wail of the banshee (9).</i><br><br>

Sun Domain Powers<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Sun domain are better able to turn undead than their brethren, and gain spells that are harmful to undead.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Exceptional Turning: The cleric has a greater chance of turning more powerful undead creatures, as well as being able to turn a larger number of undead at once.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>searing light (2), elemental shield (4), sunbeam (7), and sunburst (8).</i><br><br>

Barbarian Rage<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian.<br>Specifics: The barbarian may rage and the effects are determined by which style of rage they took at 1st level, standard or whirlwind frenzy.<br>Use: Selected. The barbarian may use this ability once per day at first level, +1 use per day at level 4, and every 4 levels thereafter.  The feat has a cooldown timer that lasts as long as the length of the rage and fatigue (if any). This is an instantly activated feat.

Greater Rage<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian.<br>Specifics: The barbarian may rage and the effects are determined by which style of rage they took at 1st level, standard or whirlwind frenzy.<br>Use: Selected. The barbarian may use this ability once per day at first level, +1 use per day at level 4, and every 4 levels thereafter.  The feat has a cooldown timer that lasts as long as the length of the rage and fatigue (if any). This is an instantly activated feat.

Mighty Rage<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian.<br>Specifics: The barbarian may rage and the effects are determined by which style of rage they took at 1st level, standard or whirlwind frenzy.<br>Use: Selected. The barbarian may use this ability once per day at first level, +1 use per day at level 4, and every 4 levels thereafter.  The feat has a cooldown timer that lasts as long as the length of the rage and fatigue (if any). This is an instantly activated feat.

Elemental Shape (2x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid level 16.<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an elemental (a huge elemental at 16th level, and an elder elemental at 20th level). The ability may be used once per day at level 16, twice per day at level 18, three times per day at level 20, and one additional use for each additional two levels (to 8/day at 30th). The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose one of four elemental types (earth, air, fire or water) to become.

Elemental Shape (3x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid level 16.<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an elemental (a huge elemental at 16th level, and an elder elemental at 20th level). The ability may be used once per day at level 16, twice per day at level 18, three times per day at level 20, and one additional use for each additional two levels (to 8/day at 30th). The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose one of four elemental types (earth, air, fire or water) to become.

Elemental Shape (4x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid level 16.<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an elemental (a huge elemental at 16th level, and an elder elemental at 20th level). The ability may be used once per day at level 16, twice per day at level 18, three times per day at level 20, and one additional use for each additional two levels (to 8/day at 30th). The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose one of four elemental types (earth, air, fire or water) to become.

Low-light Vision<br>
Type of Feat: Race or Class.<br>Prerequisite: Deep Imaskari, Elf, half-elf, or gnome; spirit shaman level 20; or Heartwarder 10.<br>Specifics: Low-light vision grants the ability to see in the dark, but not as far as darkvision.<br>Use: Selected.

Artist<br>
Type of Feat: Background Trait<br>Prerequisite: Can only take this feat at 1st-level.<br>Specifics: Character gains a +2 bonus on Perform and Diplomacy checks. They also gain +3 uses of a bard's song per day if they have bardic levels (up to 20/day). These are real uses of bardic song and as such qualify for feats requiring a specific number of songs. These extra songs are awarded AFTER each level up.<br>Use: Automatic.

Circle Kick<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Base attack bonus +3, Dex 15+, Improved Unarmed Strike.<br>Specifics: If the character succeeds in hitting an opponent with an unarmed attack, they get an additional free attack against another, nearby enemy. There is a maximum of one free attack per round.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>WARNING: This extra attack often switches your target to the new enemy which may not be desired.

Zen Archery<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Base attack bonus +1, Wis 13+.<br>Specifics: Wisdom guides the character's ranged attacks, letting them use their Wisdom modifier, if it is higher, instead of their Dexterity when firing ranged weapons.<br>Use: Automatic.

Shadow Daze<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Shadowdancer 3.<br>Specifics: Three times per day the shadowdancer may inflict an illusory daze upon a target. This daze lasts for 5 rounds.<br>Use: Selected.

Shadow Evade<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Shadowdancer level 4.<br>Specifics: Three times per day the shadowdancer can call upon the shadows in the area to help conceal her. She gains a 5% concealment bonus, damage reduction of 5/magic and a +1 dodge AC. This improves to 10% concealment, 5/alchemical silver damage reduction, and +2 dodge AC bonus at level 6. At 8th level it becomes 15% concealment, 10/alchemical silver damage reduction and +3 dodge AC. At 10th level, Shadow Evade grants 20% concealment, 10/adamantine damage reduction and a +4 dodge AC bonus. The effects last for 2 rounds per level of shadowdancer.<br>Use: Selected.

Epic Rage<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Str 25, Mighty Rage, base attack bonus +25.<br>Specifics: While raging, the character's Str and Con bonuses increase to +10 and their Will save bonus increases to +8.<br>Use: Automatic while in rage.

Dragon Shape<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Wis 27, Wild Shape 8/day.<br>Specifics: The character can spend a Wild Shape use to transform into one of: Black Dragon, Red Dragon, Blue Dragon, Bronze Dragon, and Silver Dragon.<br>Use: Selected.

Undead Graft I<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Pale Master level 6.<br>Specifics: Two times per day a Pale Master may use their undead arm to paralyze foes. At level 8 this may be used three times per day. The DC is 10 + Palemaster level + Cha modifier and the paralysis lasts d4 + 1 rounds.<br>Use: Selected.

Undead Graft II<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Pale Master level 6.<br>Specifics: Two times per day a Pale Master may use their undead arm to paralyze foes. At level 8 this may be used three times per day. The DC is 10 + Palemaster level + Cha modifier and the paralysis lasts d4 + 1 rounds.<br>Use: Selected.

Deathless Master's Touch<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Pale Master level 10.<br>Specifics: At 10th level a Pale Master's touch becomes deadly, instantly killing unless a DC (10 + Palemaster level + Cha modifier) fortitude save is made. This ability may be used three times per day.<br>Use: Selected.

Use Poison<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Specifics: The  character is trained in the use of poison and is never at risk of poisoning himself when applying poison to a weapon.<br>Use: Automatic.

Natural Armor Increase<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Disciple.<br>Specifics: A dragon disciple becomes more draconic in appearance. His skin develops tiny iridescent scales, nearly invisible. This provides increasing natural armor bonuses to his base Armor Class (+1 at 1st, +2 at 4th, +3 at 7th  and +4 at 10th level).<br>Use: Automatic

Dragon Abilities<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Disciple.<br>Specifics: As the dragon disciple gains levels in this prestige class, his ability scores increase by the following table:<br> Level<br> 2 :  +2 to Strength<br> 4 :  +2 to Strength<br> 6 :  +2 to Con<br> 8 :  +2 to Int<br> 10: +4 to Strength, +2 to Charisma<br>Use: Automatic

Immunity to Paralysis<br>
Type of Feat: Race<br>Prerequisite: Duergar or Dragon Disciple level 10 or Dragon Shaman 4th.<br>Specifics: This feat grants immunity to paralysis.<br>Use: Automatic.

Dragon Breath<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Disciple level 3.<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, the dragon disciple gains the ability to breate a cone of their dragon's element that deals 2d10 points of damage to all creatures within its area. This damage increases to 4d10 at 7th level and 6d10 at 10th level. Targets may make a Reflex save (DC 10 + 1/2 the red dragon disciple's class level + the disciple's Charisma bonus).<br>Use: Selected. This ability may be used once per day.<br>Black, Green, and Copper dragon disciples deal Acid damage. Blue and Bronze dragon disciples deal Lightning damage. White and Silver dragon disciples deal Cold damage. Red, Brass, and Gold dragon disciples deal Fire damage.

Expert Tactician<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Dex 13, base attack bonus +2<br>Specifics: You gain a +1 bonus on Attack rolls (subject to the +20 AB cap).<br>Use: Automatic.

Spellcasting Prodigy<br>
Type of Feat: Background Trait<br>Prerequisite: Only available at 1st level.<br>Specifics: For the purpose of determining bonus spells and the saving throw DCs of spells you cast, treat your primary spellcasting ability score (Charisma for bards, sorcerers, and warlocks; Wisdom for divine spellcasters; Intelligence for wizards) as 2 points higher than its actual value. If you have more than one spellcasting class, the bonus applies to all of those classes. Warlocks only gain the bonus to DC and do not gain any extra invocations. Hexblades gain an additional spell per day of each spell level that they qualify for.<br>Use: Automatic.

Greater Resiliency<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian damage reduction 1/- (or better) OR Unbroken Flesh (Anointed Knight 5) OR Scarred Flesh (Warrior of Darkness 5) or Dragonslayer damage reduction 1/- (or better)<br>Specifics: Your damage reduction increases by 1. If it would normally rise thereafter with level, it does so at its previous rate, adding the +1 normally. For example, a 13th-level barbarian has damage reduction 3/-. By taking this feat, he raises it to 4/-. When he reaches 16th level, his damage reduction becomes 5/- and at 19th level, it becomes 6/-.<br>Use: Automatic.

Extend Rage<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Rage, frenzy, or feral stance ability.<br>Specifics: Each use of rage, frenzy, or feral stance lasts 5 rounds longer. Extend Rage II through IV each add an additional 5 rounds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Improved Shifting<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Ability to use wild shape.<br>Specifics: You use your wild shape ability two more times per day than you otherwise could. If you are able to use wild shape to become an elemental, you also gain one additional elemental wild shape use per day. <br>Use: Automatic.

Oaken Resilience<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>Prerequisite: Plant Shape.<br>Specifics: You can spend a wild shape to gain immunity to critical hits, poison, sleep, paralysis, and stunning for 10 minutes.<br>Use: Selected.

Improved Uncanny Dodge<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Rogue level 8, barbarian 5, assassin level 5, dwarven defender 6, shadow thief level 5 or shadowdancer level 5<br>Specifics: A character with this feat cannot be the victim of a sneak attack unless the attacker performing the sneak attack has 4 or more levels in classes that give sneak attack bonuses (such as rogue or assassin) than character levels of the defender with this feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

Eldritch Blast (1d6)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Warlock level 1.<br>Specifics: A warlock attacks his foes with eldritch power, using baleful magical energy to deal damage and sometimes impart other debilitating effects. It is a ranged touch attack that affects a single target, allowing no saving throw.  An <i>eldritch blast</i> is subject to spell resistance, although the Spell Penetration feat and other effects that improve caster level checks to overcome spell resistance also apply to <i>eldritch blast.</i> Metamagic feats cannot improve a warlock's <i>eldritch blast</i> (because it is a spell-like ability, not a spell). Eldritch blast can sneak attack if the eldritch spear shape or no shape at all is used and sneak attack for spells has been turned on.<br>Use: Selected. An <i>eldritch blast</i> deals 1d6 points of damage at 1st level and increases in power as the warlock rises in level. An <i>eldritch blast</i> is the equivalent of a spell whose level is equal to highest of the blast (1), the shape, and the essence.<br><br>* Beshadowed Blast: 4th<br>* Bewitching Blast: 4th<br>* Binding Blast: 7th<br>* Brimstone Blast: 3rd<br>* Caster's Lament: 8th<br>* Caustic Web: 4th<br>* Draining Blast: 2nd<br>* Eldritch Chain: 4th<br>* Eldritch Cone: 5th<br>* Eldritch Doom: 8th<br>* Eldritch Spear: 2nd<br>* Frightful Blast: 2nd<br>* Frightful Presence: 3rd<br>* Hellrime Blast: 4th<br>* Hideous Blow: 1st<br>* Hindering Blast: 4th<br>* Ignore the Pyre: 4th<br>* Instill Vulnerability: 7th<br>* Noxious Blast: 6th<br>* Repelling Blast: 6th<br>* Tempest Blast: 4th<br>* Undead Bane Blast: 3rd<br>* Utterdark Blast: 8th<br>* Vitriolic Blast: 6th<br><br>

Eldritch Blast (5d6)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Warlock level 1.<br>Specifics: A warlock attacks his foes with eldritch power, using baleful magical energy to deal damage and sometimes impart other debilitating effects. It is a ranged touch attack that affects a single target, allowing no saving throw.  An <i>eldritch blast</i> is subject to spell resistance, although the Spell Penetration feat and other effects that improve caster level checks to overcome spell resistance also apply to <i>eldritch blast.</i> Metamagic feats cannot improve a warlock's <i>eldritch blast</i> (because it is a spell-like ability, not a spell). Eldritch blast can sneak attack if the eldritch spear shape or no shape at all is used and sneak attack for spells has been turned on.<br>Use: Selected. An <i>eldritch blast</i> deals 1d6 points of damage at 1st level and increases in power as the warlock rises in level. An <i>eldritch blast</i> is the equivalent of a spell whose level is equal to highest of the blast (1), the shape, and the essence.<br><br>* Beshadowed Blast: 4th<br>* Bewitching Blast: 4th<br>* Binding Blast: 7th<br>* Brimstone Blast: 3rd<br>* Caster's Lament: 8th<br>* Caustic Web: 4th<br>* Draining Blast: 2nd<br>* Eldritch Chain: 4th<br>* Eldritch Cone: 5th<br>* Eldritch Doom: 8th<br>* Eldritch Spear: 2nd<br>* Frightful Blast: 2nd<br>* Frightful Presence: 3rd<br>* Hellrime Blast: 4th<br>* Hideous Blow: 1st<br>* Hindering Blast: 4th<br>* Ignore the Pyre: 4th<br>* Instill Vulnerability: 7th<br>* Noxious Blast: 6th<br>* Repelling Blast: 6th<br>* Tempest Blast: 4th<br>* Undead Bane Blast: 3rd<br>* Utterdark Blast: 8th<br>* Vitriolic Blast: 6th<br><br>

Eldritch Blast (6d6)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Warlock level 1.<br>Specifics: A warlock attacks his foes with eldritch power, using baleful magical energy to deal damage and sometimes impart other debilitating effects. It is a ranged touch attack that affects a single target, allowing no saving throw.  An <i>eldritch blast</i> is subject to spell resistance, although the Spell Penetration feat and other effects that improve caster level checks to overcome spell resistance also apply to <i>eldritch blast.</i> Metamagic feats cannot improve a warlock's <i>eldritch blast</i> (because it is a spell-like ability, not a spell). Eldritch blast can sneak attack if the eldritch spear shape or no shape at all is used and sneak attack for spells has been turned on.<br>Use: Selected. An <i>eldritch blast</i> deals 1d6 points of damage at 1st level and increases in power as the warlock rises in level. An <i>eldritch blast</i> is the equivalent of a spell whose level is equal to highest of the blast (1), the shape, and the essence.<br><br>* Beshadowed Blast: 4th<br>* Bewitching Blast: 4th<br>* Binding Blast: 7th<br>* Brimstone Blast: 3rd<br>* Caster's Lament: 8th<br>* Caustic Web: 4th<br>* Draining Blast: 2nd<br>* Eldritch Chain: 4th<br>* Eldritch Cone: 5th<br>* Eldritch Doom: 8th<br>* Eldritch Spear: 2nd<br>* Frightful Blast: 2nd<br>* Frightful Presence: 3rd<br>* Hellrime Blast: 4th<br>* Hideous Blow: 1st<br>* Hindering Blast: 4th<br>* Ignore the Pyre: 4th<br>* Instill Vulnerability: 7th<br>* Noxious Blast: 6th<br>* Repelling Blast: 6th<br>* Tempest Blast: 4th<br>* Undead Bane Blast: 3rd<br>* Utterdark Blast: 8th<br>* Vitriolic Blast: 6th<br><br>

Frenzy 1<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Frenzied Berserker level 1<br>Specifics: - A 1st-level frenzied berserker gains the ability to frenzy. Frenzy adds a +6 bonus to Strength (no Constitution bonus), imposes a -4 penalty to AC,  and gives the frenzied berserker a single extra attack at his highest Base Attack Bonus (this does not stack with Haste or other effects that add an additional attack). Each round of the frenzy, the frenzied berserker takes 6 points of damage (2 if he has any barbarian levels). The effects last 3 + Constitution modifier rounds, after which the frenzied berserker is fatigued for 5 rounds, suffering -2 Strength, -2 Dexterity, and -10% movement penalties. Level 10 Frenzied Berserkers are no longer fatigued by using Frenzy. Initially, this ability can be used once per day and at 3rd level and every odd level after that, the frenzied berserker gains an additional use per day. Bonuses and penalties (including fatigue effects) from Frenzy and Barbarian Rage stack with each other. The Tireless feat also prevents this fatigue. <br>Use: Selected. This ability cannot be re-used until the fatigue effects have expired.

Inspire Frenzy 1<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Frenzied berserker level 6.<br>Specifics: At 6th level, the frenzied berserker can inspire Frenzy in all allies. This ability has no effect on the frenzied berserker, only the berserker's allies. The Frenzy effect adds a +6 bonus to Strength (no Constitution bonus, +10 Strength at 8th level), imposes a -4 penalty to AC,  and gives the subjects a single extra attack at their highest Base Attack Bonus (this does not stack with Haste or other effects that add an additional attack). Each round of the frenzy, the subjects takes 6 points of damage (2 if they have any barbarian levels). The effects last 3 + Con modifier rounds, after which the subjects are fatigued for 5 rounds, suffering -2 Strength, -2 Dexterity, and -10% movement penalties. The frenzied berserker can use this ability once per day at 6th level, twice at 8th level, and three times at 10th level. <br>Special: Bonuses from Inspire Frenzy and Barbarian Rage do not stack, though penalties do. If both abilities are used, only the highest modifiers are applied and all penalties take effect.<br>Use: Selected. The frenzied berserker must wait at least four rounds between uses of this ability.

Inspire Frenzy 2<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Frenzied berserker level 6.<br>Specifics: At 6th level, the frenzied berserker can inspire Frenzy in all allies. This ability has no effect on the frenzied berserker, only the berserker's allies. If both abilities are used, only the highest modifiers are applied and all penalties take effect.<br>Use: Selected. Fascinate<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bard level 1, Perform 3 ranks<br>Specifics: Every hostile creature within 90 feet must make a Will save (against a DC of 10 + your Perform skill). This is an enchantment (compulsion) mind-affecting ability. If the target fails this save, then they are dazed for as long as the song is playing and the bard is within 90 feet of them. If a creature is attacked, or is within 10 feet of a creature being attacked, the effect ends for that creature, though anyone who fails their saving throw will be dazed for at least one round. There is a cool down of 10 rounds before this ability can be used again.<br>Fascinate effects up to one enemy per level of the bard.<br>Use: Selected

Cloud Mind<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bard level 6, Perform 9 ranks<br>This is a more potent, single-target version of the Fascinate bard song. The saving throw DC is increased to 10 + your Perform skill, and enemies being attacked nearby don't break the effect. There is a 5-round cool down before this ability can be used again.<br>Use: Selected

Guild Thief Sneak Attack 1<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Guild Thief level 1.<br>Specifics: Starting at 1st level, the guild thief gets the sneak attack ability at +1d6 damage. This is the same as the rogue ability of the same name, and its effects stack with that ability. This ability increases by +1d6 at 3rd and 5th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Guild Thief Sneak Attack 2<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Guild Thief level 1.<br>Specifics: Starting at 1st level, the guild thief gets the sneak attack ability at +1d6 damage. This is the same as the rogue ability of the same name, and its effects stack with that ability. This ability increases by +1d6 at 3rd and 5th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Guild Thief Sneak Attack 3<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Guild Thief level 1.<br>Specifics: Starting at 1st level, the guild thief gets the sneak attack ability at +1d6 damage. This is the same as the rogue ability of the same name, and its effects stack with that ability. This ability increases by +1d6 at 3rd and 5th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Guild Thief Reputation<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Guild Thief level 3.<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, the guild thief gets a 10% discount from all merchants and an additional +2 bonus on all Bluff, Intimidate, and Diplomacy checks because of his reputation.<br>Use: Automatic.

Harper Spellcasting (Cleric)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Harper agent<br>Specifics: When a Harper agent gains a level, he gains new spells per day  (and spells known, if applicable) as if he had also gained a level in a spellcasting class he belonged to before adding the prestige class. He does not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained (hit points, bonus feats, etc.), except for an increased level of effective spellcasting level. This spellcasting class is chosen from any spellcasting classes you have when you first become a Harper agent, and all Harper agent levels only improve spells per day progression in that one class. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Harper Spellcasting (Druid)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Harper agent<br>Specifics: When a Harper agent gains a level, he gains new spells per day  (and spells known, if applicable) as if he had also gained a level in a spellcasting class he belonged to before adding the prestige class. He does not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained (hit points, bonus feats, etc.), except for an increased level of effective spellcasting level. This spellcasting class is chosen from any spellcasting classes you have when you first become a Harper agent, and all Harper agent levels only improve spells per day progression in that one class. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Harper Spellcasting (Sorcerer)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Harper agent<br>Specifics: When a Harper agent gains a level, he gains new spells per day  (and spells known, if applicable) as if he had also gained a level in a spellcasting class he belonged to before adding the prestige class. He does not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained (hit points, bonus feats, etc.), except for an increased level of effective spellcasting level. This spellcasting class is chosen from any spellcasting classes you have when you first become a Harper agent, and all Harper agent levels only improve spells per day progression in that one class. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

<color=Gold>Dragonslayer</color><br>
You have defeated one of the most fearsome monsters of the Sword Coast: a dragon! If you have levels in the dragonslayer prestige class, you gain a +5 bonus against dragons.

Flourish<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Duelist level 6.<br>Specifics: Periodically in combat, the duelist can prepare a special attack. If this Flourish attack connects with an opponent, it does an additional 2d6 piercing damage. This ability has a cooldown of 12 seconds.<br>Use: Selected. This ability modifies your next attack. It does not add an additional attack.

Elaborate Parry<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Duelist level 7.<br>Specifics: Starting at 7th level, the duelist adds her class level to her parry skill.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><br>The class may gain an AC bonus equal to class level (or fraction of) based on cmi_options. Defaults to AC equal to 1/3 Duelist level while in parry or combat expertise modes.

Pale Master Spellcasting (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Pale Master.<br>Specifics: Beginning at 2nd level and at each new level, the pale master gains new spells per day as if he had also gained a level in an arcane spellcasting class he belonged to before adding the prestige class. He does not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained (hit points, bonus feats, etc.), except for an increased level of effective spellcasting level. This spellcasting class is chosen from any arcane spellcasting classes that give you access to 3rd-level spells when you first become a pale master, and all pale master levels only improve spells per day progression in that one class.<br>Use: Automatic.

Protective Aura<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Neverwinter Nine level 1<br>Specifics: At 1st level, the Nine gain an aura that grants +2 to all saving throws, +1 dodge bonus to armor class for all allies within 20 feet. This bonus does NOT apply to the Nine, only to his allies). At 4th level, this bonus increases to +3 saving throws, +2 dodge bonus, and the protection extends to allies within 30 feet.<br>Use: Automatic, this aura is always in effect and automatically improves at 4th level.

Warpriest Spell (Haste)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Warpriest level 7<br>Specifics: Upon reaching 7th level, a warpriest may cast <i>haste</i> three times per day as a spell-like ability. The warpriest's caster level is used to determine the duration of this spell.<br>Use: Selected, usable three times per day.

Air Domain<br>
<br>Clerics of the Air domain move with the subtlety of the breeze and gain the ability to cast electrical damage spells.<br><br><b>Domain Special Abilities and Bonus Spells: </b><br>- Uncanny Dodge: The cleric retains his Dexterity bonus to AC, even when flat-footed or struck by an invisible creature. <br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>ghostly visage (1), call lightning (3), chain lightning (6), elemental swarm (9)</i>.<br>

Animal Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Animal domain are able to call a faithful animal companion and cast spells that give them bestial agility and perception.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Animal Companion: The cleric can summon an animal companion, like a druid or ranger.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>cat's grace (2), dominate animal (3), true seeing (4).</i><br><br>

Death Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Death domain can summon an enhanced shadow and gains spells related to death and dying.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Negative Plane Avatar: Once per day, the cleric is able to summon a shadow that gains power as the cleric advances.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>death ward (3), phantasmal killer (4), wail of the banshee (9).</i><br><br>

Destruction Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Destruction domain are able to smite the unfaithful and are granted spells that allow them to weaken and destroy their enemies.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Smite Infidel: Once per day, the cleric can smite a creature of a different alignment than himself, adding his Charisma bonus to the attack roll and his cleric level to damage.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>rage (3), acid fog (6), disintegrate (7)</i>.<br>

Earth Domain<br>
<br>Clerics of the Earth domain are as tough as old stones and are granted spells that protect against melee and energy damage.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Toughness: The cleric gains the Toughness feat, gaining an additional hit point for every character level he has, and an additional hit point each time he gains a level.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>spike growth (3), stoneskin (4), energy immunity (5), iron body (8), elemental swarm (9).</i><br>

Fire Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Fire domain are resistant to the searing heat of the flame, and are granted spells that call down fire against their foes.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Fire Resistance: The cleric gains fire resistance 5.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>burning hands (1), fireball (3), wall of fire (4), elemental shield (5), firestorm (7), incendary cloud (8), and elemental swarm (9)</i>.<br>

Strength Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Strength domain are able to boost their Strength with divine energy, and gain access to spells that make them stronger and more resilient.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Divine Strength: Once per day, the cleric may gain a bonus to Strength equal to 2 + 1 per 3 class levels. This effect has a duration of 5 rounds + the cleric's Charisma modifier.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>bull's strength (1), divine power (3), stoneskin (6)</i>.

Sun Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Sun domain are better able to turn undead than their brethren, and gain spells that are harmful to undead.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Exceptional Turning: The cleric has a greater chance of turning more powerful undead creatures, as well as being able to turn a larger number of undead at once.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>searing light (2), elemental shield (4), sunbeam (7), and sunburst (8).</i><br><br>

Water Domain<br>
<br>Clerics who take the Water domain flow like water to avoid danger and are granted spells that weaken or kill opponents.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Evasion: Whenever the cleric makes a successful Reflex save to take half damage from a spell or effect, he instead takes no damage.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>ghostly visage (1), poison (3), ice storm (5), cone of cold (6), acid fog (7), horrid withering (8), and elemental swarm (9)</i>.

Pilfer Magic<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Arcane trickster level 1.<br>Specifics: At 1st level, once per day, the arcane trickster can dispel all magical effects on a target and receive statistical bonuses from doing so.  The ability is a touch attack "greater" dispel. Level 10 Arcane Tricksters have an uncapped dispel like Disjunction.  If successful, the effects vanish from the target and the arcane trickster receives +2 bonus to attack rolls and all saving throws for the next ten rounds plus your arcane trickster level.  He can use this ability twice per day at 5th level and three times per day at 9th level.<br>Use: Selected.

Practical Metamagic (Empower)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You can apply a selected metamagic feat to your spells more easily.<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 8, Empower Spell, Level 3 spells.<br>Benefit: When applying the chosen metamagic feat to a spell, the spell uses a spell slot one level lower than normal for the applied metamagic feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

Practical Metamagic (Maximize)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You can apply a selected metamagic feat to your spells more easily.<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 8, Maximize Spell, Level 3 spells.<br>Benefit: When applying the chosen metamagic feat to a spell, the spell uses a spell slot one level lower than normal for the applied metamagic feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

Practical Metamagic (Quicken)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You can apply a selected metamagic feat to your spells more easily.<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 8, Quicken Spell, Level 3 spells.<br>Benefit: When applying the chosen metamagic feat to a spell, the spell uses a spell slot one level lower than normal for the applied metamagic feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Fiendish Resilience<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Fiendish Resilience 5.<br>Specifics: The Fiendish Resilience ability now grants fast healing 25 for 5 minutes.<br>Use: Automatic.

Eldritch Blast (10d6)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Warlock level 1.<br>Specifics: A warlock attacks his foes with eldritch power, using baleful magical energy to deal damage and sometimes impart other debilitating effects. It is a ranged touch attack that affects a single target, allowing no saving throw.  An <i>eldritch blast</i> is subject to spell resistance, although the Spell Penetration feat and other effects that improve caster level checks to overcome spell resistance also apply to <i>eldritch blast.</i> Metamagic feats cannot improve a warlock's <i>eldritch blast</i> (because it is a spell-like ability, not a spell). Eldritch blast can sneak attack if the eldritch spear shape or no shape at all is used and sneak attack for spells has been turned on.<br>Use: Selected. An <i>eldritch blast</i> deals 1d6 points of damage at 1st level and increases in power as the warlock rises in level. An <i>eldritch blast</i> is the equivalent of a spell whose level is equal to highest of the blast (1), the shape, and the essence.<br><br>* Beshadowed Blast: 4th<br>* Bewitching Blast: 4th<br>* Binding Blast: 7th<br>* Brimstone Blast: 3rd<br>* Caster's Lament: 8th<br>* Caustic Web: 4th<br>* Draining Blast: 2nd<br>* Eldritch Chain: 4th<br>* Eldritch Cone: 5th<br>* Eldritch Doom: 8th<br>* Eldritch Spear: 2nd<br>* Frightful Blast: 2nd<br>* Frightful Presence: 3rd<br>* Hellrime Blast: 4th<br>* Hideous Blow: 1st<br>* Hindering Blast: 4th<br>* Ignore the Pyre: 4th<br>* Instill Vulnerability: 7th<br>* Noxious Blast: 6th<br>* Repelling Blast: 6th<br>* Tempest Blast: 4th<br>* Undead Bane Blast: 3rd<br>* Utterdark Blast: 8th<br>* Vitriolic Blast: 6th<br><br>

Eldritch Master<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Eldritch Blast 8d6, Spellcraft 24.<br>Specifics: The character's eldritch blasts deal +50% damage and have +2 to attack rolls. In addition, the area of effect is doubled for Eldritch Chain, Eldritch Doom, and Eldritch Cone. Eldritch Doom affects all targets within 40', for eldritch chain the distance between targets increases to 60', and for eldritch cone the length expands to 60'.<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Animal Companion<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Animal or Telthor Companion.<br>Specifics: The character's animal companion's abilities are determined as if the character possessed three additional druid levels. For example, a 21st level druid with this feat would have the animal companion of a 24th level druid without this feat. (Note that a ranger's effective druid level is level - 3 for determining their animal companion's abilities. So a 21st level ranger with this feat would have the companion of a 21st level druid.)<br>Use: Automatic.

Expose Weakness<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Evasion, Epic Prowess.<br>Specifics: As a full attack action, the character makes a single normal attack against an opponent (regardless of the number of attacks per round they normally receive). If the attack hits, the target's weakness is exposed: every round for 5 rounds the target takes damage equal to the character's Dex bonus (minimum of 1). This damage ignores all damage reductions and immunities.  During this time, the target's AC is also reduced by 3.  This ability has a cooldown of 30 seconds. This ability does NOT work with ranged or thrown weapons.<br>Use: Selected.

Song of Requiem<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: Bard level 21, Perform 24.<br>Specifics: This song damages all enemies within 20 feet for 5 rounds. The total sonic damage caused is equal to 2*Perform skill; the minimum damage caused per target is Perform/3. For example, with Perform 30, a total of 60 points of damage is inflicted each round. If six (or more) enemies are affected, they would each take 10 sonic damage per round. This ability has a cooldown of 20 rounds. This ability breaks stealth and does not cancel other inspirations.<br>Use: Selected.

Hymn of Requiem<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 20 Bard Songs/day, Song of Requiem, Perform 30.<br>Specifics: The character's Song of Requiem now also heals all party members. The amount healed is the same as the damage caused by the Hymn and is divided among all party members; the minimum amount healed per ally is Perform/3. For example, if the total damage dealt is 60 and four characters are in the party, each is healed 15 hit points per round.<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Spell: Epic Gate<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 27, the ability to cast 9th level spells (6th for Bards).<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Epic Gate.<br> <br>Classes: Bard, Cleric, Druid, Spirit Shaman, Wizard, Sorcerer, Warlock, Arcane Scholar of Candlekeep, Arcane Trickster, Eldrich Knight, Pale Master, Red Wizard, Stormlord, Harper Agent, Sacred Fist, Warpriest<br>Spellcraft Required: 27<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Conjuration<br>Descriptor(s): <br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Personal<br>Area of Effect / Target: Point<br>Duration: 40 rounds<br>Save: None<br>Spell Resistance: No<br><br>This spell opens a portal to the another plane and calls forth a creature to assail your foes. If it is slain, a second one is immediately summoned in its place.  The strength of this conjuration is such that the creatures are bound to your will, and you need not have cast Protection from Evil, or any similar spell, to prevent them from attacking you. Evil casters summon a balor, good casters summon a solar, and neutral casters summon an earth elemental monolith (all summons have identical stats).<br><br>Use: Selected.

Epic Spell: Damnation<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 32, 8d6 Eldritch Blast.<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Damnation.<br><br>Spellcraft Required: 32<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Enchantment<br>Descriptor(s): Teleportation<br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Touch<br>Area of Effect / Target: Creature touched<br>Duration: Instant<br>Save: Will negates (DC +5)<br>Spell Resistance: Yes<br><br>You banish a single foe to the Hells, with no possibility of return. You must succeed at a melee touch attack, and the target must fail at a Will saving throw (DC +5). If the target fails the saving throw, it is dragged screaming into the Hells, to be tormented and ultimately devoured by fiends.<br><br>Creatures that succeed at their saving throw are nonetheless exhausted from resisting so powerful an enchantment, and they are Dazed for 1d6+1 rounds.<br><br>Use: Selected.

Epic Spell: Entropic Husk<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 31, 8d6 Eldritch Blast.<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Entropic Husk.<br><br>Classes: Druid, Spirit Shaman, Wizard, Sorcerer, Warlock, Arcane Scholar of Candlekeep, Arcane Trickster, Eldrich Knight, Pale Master, Red Wizard, Stormlord<br>Spellcraft Required: 31<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Conjuration<br>Descriptor(s): Chaos<br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Touch<br>Area of Effect / Target: Creature Touched<br>Duration: 20 rounds<br>Save: Will negates (DC +5)<br>Spell Resistance: No<br><br>You transform a single enemy into a vessel of pure chaos which randomly attacks all nearby creatures. <br><br>You must succeed at a melee touch attack, and the target must fail at a Will saving throw (DC +5). If the target fails the saving throw, its soul is instantly annihilated, and its body is animated by primal entropy. For the duration of the spell, the creature becomes a juggernaut of destruction, gaining a +8 bonus to Strength and Constitution, and randomly attacking former allies and enemies alike. After 20 rounds, the entropic force animating the creature's body burns itself out, and the creature collapses into dust.<br><br>Use: Selected.

Epic Spell: Epic Gate<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 27, 8d6 Eldritch Blast.<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Epic Gate.<br> <br>Spellcraft Required: 27<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Conjuration<br>Descriptor(s): <br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Personal<br>Area of Effect / Target: Point<br>Duration: 40 rounds<br>Save: None<br>Spell Resistance: No<br><br>This spell opens a portal to the another plane and calls forth a creature to assail your foes. If it is slain, a second one is immediately summoned in its place.  The strength of this conjuration is such that the creatures are bound to your will, and you need not have cast Protection from Evil, or any similar spell, to prevent them from attacking you. Evil casters summon a balor, good casters summon a solar, and neutral casters summon an earth elemental monolith (all summons have identical stats).<br><br>Use: Selected.

Epic Spell: Hellball<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 30, 8d6 Eldritch Blast.<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Hellball.<br><br>Spellcraft Required: 30<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Evocation<br>Descriptor(s): Fire, Acid, Electrical, Sonic<br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Long<br>Area of Effect / Target: Huge<br>Duration: Instant<br>Save: Reflex ½ (DC +5)<br>Spell Resistance: Yes<br><br>You unleash a massive blast of energy that detonates upon all in the area of effect, dealing 10d6 fire damage, 10d6 acid damage, 10d6 electrical damage, and 10d6 sonic damage.  The Hellball ignores Evasion and Improved Evasion.<br><br>Use: Selected.

Epic Spell: Vampiric Feast<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisite: 21st level, Spellcraft 24, 8d6 Eldritch Blast.<br><br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Vampiric Feast.<br> <br>Spellcraft Required: 24<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Necromancy<br>Descriptor(s): Evil<br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Personal<br>Area of Effect / Target: All hostile creatures within 20 ft. of caster.<br>Duration: Instant<br>Save: Fortitude ½ (DC +5)<br>Spell Resistance: Yes<br> <br>When this spell is cast, you drink in the life force of all enemies in the area of effect. Creatures who succeed at a Fortitude save (DC +5) lose only half their remaining hit points, while those who fail their saving throw lose all their remaining hit points and are instantly slain. The total amount of damage done by this spell is then added to the caster's hit point total. Moreover, the life-force of slain creatures coalesces as a Greater Shadow, which will attack any surviving enemies.<br><br>You are only able to absorb sufficient hit points to return you to full health. Any remaining life force dissipates into the fabric of the Weave.<br> <br>Use: Selected.

Harper Spellcasting (Spirit Shaman)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Harper agent<br>Specifics: When a Harper agent gains a level, he gains new spells per day  (and spells known, if applicable) as if he had also gained a level in a spellcasting class he belonged to before adding the prestige class. He does not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained (hit points, bonus feats, etc.), except for an increased level of effective spellcasting level. This spellcasting class is chosen from any spellcasting classes you have when you first become a Harper agent, and all Harper agent levels only improve spells per day progression in that one class. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Chastise Spirits<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Shaman Level 2.<br>Specifics: Beginning at 2nd level, a spirit shaman can deal 1d6 damage/shaman level to all spirits within 30 feet. The affected spirits get a Will save (DC 10 + shaman level + Cha modifier) for half damage.  This ability can be used a number of times per day equal to 3 + the shaman's Cha modifier.<br>Use: Selected.

Spirit Form (1x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Shaman Level 9.<br>Specifics: A spirit shaman learns how to temporarily transform herself into a spirit.  As a standard action, she can grant herself a 50% concealment bonus, a Deflection AC bonus equal to her Charisma modifer, and Spell Resistance equal to 11 + her character level for 10 rounds. This ability is usable 1 time per day at 9th level, 2 times per day at 15th level, and 1 additional time per day every 5 levels thereafter.<br>Use: Selected.

Spirit Form (2x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Shaman Level 9.<br>Specifics: A spirit shaman learns how to temporarily transform herself into a spirit.  As a standard action, she can grant herself a 50% concealment bonus, a Deflection AC bonus equal to her Charisma modifer, and Spell Resistance equal to 11 + her character level for 10 rounds. This ability is usable 1 time per day at 9th level, 2 times per day at 15th level, and 1 additional time per day every 5 levels thereafter.<br>Use: Selected.

Shocking Burst and Sonic Weapon<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Stormlord Level 8.<br>Specifics: At 8th level, a stormlord may enchant any equipped thrown weapon or spear to deal an additional 1d8 points of electricity damage, an extra 1d8 points of sonic damage, and an extra 2d8 points of weapon damage on a critical hit.  This effect lasts 20 rounds.<br>Use: Selected.

Shocking Burst Weapon<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Stormlord Level 5.<br>Specifics: At 5th level, a stormlord may enchant any equipped thrown weapon or spear to deal an additional 1d8 points of electricity damage and an extra 2d8 points of weapon damage on a critical hit.  This effect lasts 20 rounds.<br>Use: Selected.

Harper Spellcasting (Favored Soul)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Harper agent<br>Specifics: When a Harper agent gains a level, he gains new spells per day  (and spells known, if applicable) as if he had also gained a level in a spellcasting class he belonged to before adding the prestige class. He does not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained (hit points, bonus feats, etc.), except for an increased level of effective spellcasting level. This spellcasting class is chosen from any spellcasting classes you have when you first become a Harper agent, and all Harper agent levels only improve spells per day progression in that one class. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Haste (3x/day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Favored Soul Level 17<br>Specifics: Upon reaching 17th level, a favored soul may cast <i>haste</i> three times per day on himself as a spell-like ability. The favored soul's caster level is used to determine the duration of this spell.<br>Use: Selected, usable three times per day.

Darkness Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Darkness domain have heightened senses and are granted spells that master darkness and blind others.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Blind-fight: The cleric receives an extra chance to overcome an invisible opponent's concealment.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>ghostly visage (1), blindness and deafness (2), shadow conjuration (5), power word blind (7), and power word kill (9)</i>.

Cold Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Cold domain are resistent to even magical sources of cold.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Resist Cold Energy: The cleric gains 5/- resistance against cold attacks.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>creeping cold (2), hypothermia (3), ice storm (4), cone of cold (6), polar ray (9).</i>

Dream Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Dream domain are immune to sleep effects and are masters of sleep and dreams.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Immunity to sleep: The cleric is immune to sleep effects, much as elves are.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>sleep (1), deep slumber (3), phantasmal killer (4), hiss of sleep (7), and weird (9)</i>.

Time Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Time domain are quick to act.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Improved initiative: The cleric receives a bonus to initiative rolls.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>true strike (1), haste (4), legend lore (6), and premonition (7).</i>

Undeath Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Undeath domain have mastery over undead.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Extra turning: The cleric receives additional turn undead attempts.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>animate dead (2), circle of death (6), create greater undead (7), and energy drain (8).</i>

War Domain<br>
Clerics who take the War domain spend considerable time training for combat.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Weapon focus: The cleric receives the weapon focus feat for their deity's favored weapon. They are also proficient with that weapon even if clerics normally are not. If their deity's favored weapon is unarmed strike, they gain the improved unarmed strike feat.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>flame strike (4), power word stun (8), and power word kill (9).</i>

<color=Gold>Ice Troll Berserker</color><br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian's Rage ability.<br>Specifics: After passing several initiation rites, you became an honorary member of the Ice Troll Berserker Lodge. Their warriors have trained you in their unique fighting style, providing new insight into the untapped potential of your inner fury. While raging, your character now gets a bonus +2 natural armor bonus to AC. This bonus increases to +4 if the player has Greater Rage, +6 with Mighty Rage, and +8 with Epic Rage. This natural AC does not stack with other sources of natural AC.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>

Fey Power<br>
Type of Feat: Heritage<br>Prerequisite: Fey Heritage. Nonlawful Alignment<br>Specifics: Your fey heritage augments the power of certain types of magic. Your caster level and save DCs for enchantment spells and warlock invocations increase by 1. For warlocks this caster level increase may increase their eldritch blast damage (if their new caster level would gain an increase in blast dice, such as a level 2 with this feat) and their caster level can exceed their hit dice.<br>Use: Automatic.

Fiendish Power<br>
Type of Feat: Heritage<br>Prerequisite: Fiendish Heritage, Non-good alignment.<br>Specifics: Your fiendish heritage augments the power of certain types of magic. Your caster level and save DCs for evocation spells and warlock invocations increase by 1. For warlocks this caster level increase may increase their eldritch blast damage (if their new caster level would gain an increase in blast dice, such as a level 2 with this feat) and their caster level can exceed their hit dice.<br>Use: Automatic.

Leadership<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Minimum Level 6<br>Specifics: You are the sort of person others want to follow.  You can have up to six party members. Bards gain a +1 bonus with Inspire Courage for having this feat.<br><br>[NOTE: Leadership only affects player created characters in Storm of Zehir. This feat does nothing when given to cohorts, or to characters in other campaigns except for bards (mentioned above).]<br><br>Use: Automatic.

Sacred Purification<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Turn Undead, Extra Turning<br>Specifics: You can expend a turn undead attempt to create a pulse of divine energy. All living creatures within 60 feet of you heal an amount of damage equal to 1d8 for every 2 points of Charisma modifier plus your Charisma modifier (if any). All undead creatures in this area take damage equal to 1d8 for every 2 points of Charisma modifier plus your Charisma modifier (if any).

Lay on Hands (Harm Undead)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Paladin level 2, Divine Champion level 1, Hospitaler level 1.<br>Specifics: With this feat, characters can deal damage equal to their class level multiplied by their Charisma modifier to undead.<br>Use: Selected. When used against undead creatures it is treated as a touch attack spell that delivers damage.

Spell Specialization (Melee Touch)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisite: Weapon Focus (Melee Touch Attack), caster level 4th.<br>Specifics: Damage-dealing spells that require a melee touch attack roll gain a +2 bonus on the damage they deal.  This extra damage applies only to the first successful attack of spells that create multiple rays or missiles, or the first round of damage for spells that deal damage over multiple rounds on a single successful attack.  Only spells that deal hit point damage can be affected by this feat.<br>Use: Automatic

Spell Specialization (Ranged Touch)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisite: Weapon Focus (Ranged Touch Attack), caster level 4th.<br>Specifics: Damage-dealing spells that require a ranged touch attack roll gain a +2 bonus on the damage they deal.  This extra damage applies only to the first successful attack of spells that create multiple rays or missiles, or the first round of damage for spells that deal damage over multiple rounds on a single successful attack.  Only spells that deal hit point damage can be affected by this feat.<br>Use: Automatic

Hospitaler Spellcasting Class (Spirit Shaman)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Hospitaler level 1.<br>Specifics: Every level except 1st, 5th and 9th level, the hospitaler progresses in one selected divine spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which he could cast divine spells before he became a hospitaler, the player must decide which class to assign each level of hospitaler for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.
