Blackguard's Spellbook, Level 1<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A blackguard has several level 1 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>bull's strength</i>, <i>magic weapon</i>, <i>doom</i>, and <i>cure light wounds</i>.<br>Use: Selected.

Blackguard's Spellbook, Level 2<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A blackguard has several level 2 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>inflict serious wounds</i>, <i>veil of shadow</i>, <i>cure moderate wounds</i>, <i>eagle's splendor</i>, and <i>death knell</i>.<br><br>Veil of Shadow is a 1 minute per caster level buff that provides 20% concealment.<br>Use: Selected.

Blackguard's Spellbook, Level 3<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A blackguard has several level 3 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>contagion</i>, <i>cure serious wounds</i>, <i>protection from energy</i>, and <i>summon creature III</i>.<br>Use: Selected.

Blackguard's Spellbook, Level 4<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A blackguard has several level 4 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>cure critical wounds</i>, <i>manifest death</i>, <i>freedom of movement</i>, and <i>poison</i>.<br>Use: Selected.<br><br>Manifest Death has the following description:<br>Range: Touch<br>Target: Undead touched.<br>Saving Throw: None<br>Spell Resistance: Yes<br><br><i>Your touch siphons some of the life energy from the undead, leaving a soul-numbing ball of darkness in your grasp.</i><br><br>You must succeed on a melee touch attack against an undead creature. If you do so, your touch deals 1d6 points of negative damage per two caster levels (maximum 15d6). <br><br>You regain one use of turn undead on a successful attack.

Assassin's Spellbook, Level 1<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An assassin or avenger has several level 1 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>ghostly visage</i>, <i>sleep</i>, and <i>true strike</i>.<br>Use: Selected.

Assassin's Spellbook, Level 2<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An assassin or avenger has several level 2 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>cat's grace</i>, <i>fox's cunning</i>, and <i>veil of shadow</i>.<br><br>Veil of Shadow is a 1 minute per caster level buff that provides 20% concealment.<br>Use: Selected.

Assassin's Spellbook, Level 3<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An assassin or avenger has several level 3 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>invisibility</i>, <i>deep slumber</i>, and <i>false life</i>.<br>Use: Selected.

Assassin's Spellbook, Level 4<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An assassin or avenger has several level 4 spells they can cast.  These are: <i>improved invisibility</i>, <i>freedom of movement</i>, <i>poison</i>, and <i>clairaudience and clairvoyance</i>.<br>Use: Selected.

Widen Aura Of Despair<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisite: Cha 25, Aura of Despair.<br>Benefit: Your aura of despair extends to all enemies within 100 feet of you.<br>Use: Automatic

Improved Aura Of Despair<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisite: Cha 21, Aura of Despair, Blackguard level 8.<br>Benefit: Your aura of despair causes a -4 morale penalty on all saving throws.<br>Use: Automatic

Tempest Whirlwind<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Tempest level 5.<br>Specifics: The Tempest's skill with two weapons has improved to the point her whirlwind attack is a deadly dance of blades.  This is an improved Whirlwind Attack that deals an additional 2d6 slashing damage on each attack made.<br>Use: Selected.

Tempest Defense<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Tempest level 1.<br>Specifics: When wielding two weapons and wearing light or no armor, the Tempest gains the ability to defend herself with more skill and to seek out the gaps in an opponents defense.  The bonuses for a given level replace those of a previous level.<br>Level 1: +1 Dodge AC Bonus<br>Level 2: +1 Dodge AC Bonus, +1 Attack Bonus<br>Level 3: +2 Dodge AC Bonus, +1 Attack Bonus<br>Level 4: +2 Dodge AC Bonus, +2 Attack Bonus<br>Level 5: +3 Dodge AC Bonus, +2 Attack Bonus

Sacred Flame<br>
Beginning at 7th level, at will, a black flame zealot can command the melee weapons in his possession to burn with a black, deadly fire.  The weapons gain the flaming ability, dealing 1d6 points of fire damage per successful attack in addition to its normal weapon damage.

Zealous Heart<br>
A black flame zealot is immune to fear, magical or otherwise.  Unlike a paladin's aura of courage, this confers no special benefits on his companions.  A Black Flame Zealot gains this ability at first level.

Celerity Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Celerity domain move with speed and grace, and are granted spells that increase movement.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Dash: The cleric gains the ability to move faster at all times.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Expeditious Retreat (1), Cat's Grace (2), Invisibility (3), Haste (4), Cat's Grace, Mass(7)</i>.

Dwarf Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Dwarf domain seek to better understand Dwarves, and are granted spells that favor their kind.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Great Fortitude: The cleric becomes hardier than most.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Protection from Spells (8), Elemental Swarm(9)</i>.

Elf Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Elf domain seek to better understand Elves, and are granted spells that favor their kind.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Point Blank Shot: The cleric is more profience with a bow in close combat.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>True Strike (1), Cat's Grace (2), Liveoak (7), Sunburst(8)</i>.

Fate Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Fate domain have a deeper understanding of mortality, and are granted spells to prolong their inevitable demise.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Uncanny Dodge: The cleric gains the ability to react to danger before his senses would normally allow.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>True Strike (1), Mark of Justice (5), Legend Lore(7), Mind Blank (8), Premonition (9)</i>.

Hatred Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Hatred domain tap the angst of dark hatred for power, and are granted spells to inspire fear and hatred in others.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Inspire Hatred: The cleric gains the ability inspire hatred towards the selected foe among his allies once per day.  The subject suffers a -2 penalty to their AC, Attack Bonus, and Saving Throws for 1 minute.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Scare (2), Rage (4), Blasphemy (7), Wail of the Banshee(9)</i>.

Mysticism Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Mysticism domain seek a better understand of their deity, and are granted spells that favor becoming closer to the divine.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Mystic Protection:  The cleric is shielded by divine will from harm once per day.  This protection grants the cleric her Cha modifier as a bonus to all saving throws for a number of rounds equal to her caster level.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Lesser Visage of the Deity (2), Righteous Might (4), Holy Word(7)</i>.

Pestilence Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Pestilence domain seek to spread death and decay, and are granted spells that further such dolorous desires.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Divine Health: The cleric is immune to the diseases they seek to spread among the world.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Contagion (2), Poison (3), Scourge (7), Horrid Wilting(8)</i>.

Storm Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Storm domain embody the essence of the tempest, and are granted spells that reflect their turbulent nature.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Resist Energy: Electricity: The cleric is resistant to the fury of the thunderous sky.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Gust of Wind (2), Call Lightning (3), Ice Storm (5), Chain Lightning(7)</i>.

Suffering Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Suffering domain seek to bring pain and torture to the masses, and are granted spells that reflect their cruel nature.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Pain Touch: The cleric is has the ability to infuse the essence of pain into a single touch once per day.  With a melee touch attack, the cleric deals 2 strength and dexterity damage to the target (4 on a critical).<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Enervation (4), Feeblemind (5), Destruction (6), Horrid Wilting(9)</i>.

Tyranny Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Tyranny domain seek absolute power, and are granted spells to oppress those they seek to rule.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Spell Focus: Enchantment: The cleric is greater able to project her will with her magic.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Fear (4), Bigby's Grasping Hand (7), Mass Charm Monster (8), Dominate Monster(9)</i>.

Repose Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Repose domain seek the grace of a good death, and are granted spells to combat unnecessary death.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Death Touch: The cleric can strike dead with a touch those who defy their will once per day.  With a melee touch attack, the cleric rolls d6 per caster level (double on a critical) and slays the target if their HP is less than this value.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Death Ward (3), Phantasmal Killer (4), Destruction (6), Wail of the Banshee(9)</i>.

Hatred Domain, Inspire Hatred<br>
Clerics who take the Hatred domain tap the angst of dark hatred for power, and are granted spells to inspire fear and hatred in others.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Inspire Hatred: The cleric gains the ability inspire hatred towards the selected foe among his allies once per day.  The subject suffers a -2 penalty to their AC, Attack Bonus, and Saving Throws for 1 minute.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Scare (2), Rage (4), Blasphemy (7), Wail of the Banshee(9)</i>.

Mysticism Domain, Mystic Protection<br>
Clerics who take the Mysticism domain seek a better understand of their deity, and are granted spells that favor becoming closer to the divine.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Mystic Protection:  The cleric is shielded by divine will from harm once per day.  This protection grants the cleric her Cha modifier as a bonus to all saving throws for a number of rounds equal to her caster level.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Lesser Visage of the Deity (2), Righteous Might (4), Holy Word(7)</i>.

Suffering Domain, Pain Touch<br>
Clerics who take the Suffering domain seek to bring pain and torture to the masses, and are granted spells that reflect their cruel nature.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Pain Touch: The cleric is has the ability to infuse the essence of pain into a single touch once per day.  With a melee touch attack, the cleric deals 2 strength and dexterity damage to the target (4 on a critical).<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Enervation (4), Feeblemind (5), Destruction (6), Horrid Wilting(9)</i>.

Repose Domain, Death Touch<br>
Clerics who take the Repose domain seek the grace of a good death, and are granted spells to combat unnecessary death.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Death Touch: The cleric can strike dead with a touch those who defy their will once per day.  With a melee touch attack, the cleric rolls d6 per caster level (double on a critical) and slays the target if their HP is less than this value.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Death Ward (3), Phantasmal Killer (4), Destruction (6), Wail of the Banshee(9)</i>.

Shock Blade<br>
Twice per day as a swift action, a shining blade can cause a melee weapon he is holding to become a shock weapon, dealing an extra 1d6 points of electricity damage on a successful hit.  The weapon is enchanced this way for up to a number of rounds equal to the shining blade's level plus his Charisma modifier (if any), but only so long as the shining blade is holding the weapon. This increases to 3 times/day at 3rd level, 4 times/day at 5th, 5 times/day at 7th, and 6 times/day at 9th.<br><br>At 9th level the Shocking Blade and Holy Blade abilities will also grant a +2 attack bonus. If both abilities are used on the same weapon the bonus will stack.

Holy Blade<br>
At 5th level, the shining blade can cause a melee weapon he is holding to become a holy weapon, dealing an extra 2d6 points of divine damage against evil on a successful hit.  The weapon is enchanced this way for up to a number of rounds equal to the shining blade's level plus his Charisma modifier (if any).  In a given combat a shining blade can spend one standard action to make his weapon holy and another to make it a shock weapon with Shocking Blade.<br><br>At 9th level the Shocking Blade and Holy Blade abilities will also grant a +2 attack bonus. If both abilities are used on the same weapon the bonus will stack.

Sacred Stealth<br>
The divine seeker can call upon her deity's power to shield her from prying eyes and ears. As a standard action, she can grant herself a +10 sacred bonus (or profane bonus, if her patron deity is evil) on Hide and Move Silently checks. This benefit lasts for a number of minutes equal to her Charisma modifier (minimum 1 minute). This ability is usable once per day.

Sacred Defense<br>
The divine seeker gains a +1 bonus on all saving throws. This bonus increases to +2 at 4th level.

Divine Perserverance<br>
If a 4th-level or higher divine seeker is brought to -1 hit points or lower, she automatically heals a number of hit points equal to 3d6 + her Charisma bonus (if any). This ability is usable once per day.

Nightsong Infiltrator Teamwork<br>
Nightsong Infiltrator Teamwork grants an aura that improves with each level.<br><br>Level 1:<br>Teamwork Trap Sense +1: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a +1 bonus on Reflex saves made to avoid traps.<br><br>Level 2:<br>Teamwork Infiltration +2: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a +2 competence bonus the following skills: Disable Device, Hide, Move Silently, Open Lock, Search, and Tumble. <br><br>Level 4:<br>Teamwork Trap Sense +2: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a +2 bonus on Reflex saves made to avoid traps. <br>Teamwork Sneak Attack: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a 1d6 sneak attack bonus when flanking an opponent or any time the target would be denied its Dexterity bonus. <br><br>Level 7:<br>Teamwork Trap Sense +3: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a +3 bonus on Reflex saves made to avoid traps. <br><br>Level 8:<br>Teamwork Infiltration +4: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a +4 competence bonus the following skills: Disable Device, Hide, Move Silently, Open Lock, Search, and Tumble. <br>Teamwork Sneak Attack: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a 2d6 sneak attack bonus when flanking an opponent or any time the target would be denied its Dexterity bonus. <br><br>Level 10:<br>Teamwork Trap Sense +4: The nightsong infiltrator grants herself and her allies (within 30 feet) a +4 bonus on Reflex saves made to avoid traps. 

Adrenaline Boost<br>
You can exhort your allies to discover a reservoir of energy they didn't think they had. Once per day, as a standard action, you can grant temporary hit points equal to 5 plus your nightsong infiltrator level to all of your allies within 30 feet (but not to yourself). If the ally's current hit point total is no more than half his full normal hit points, the number of temporary hit points granted to that character is doubled. The temporary hit points last for up to 1 minute per nightsong infiltrator level. At 5th level you can use this ability twice per day and three times per day at 9th level.

Nightsong Enforcer Teamwork<br>
All allies within 30 feet of the nightsong enforcer gain a +2 competence bonus on Hide, Listen, Move Silently, and Spot checks. At 7th level, this bonus increases to +4.<br><br>

Agility Training<br>
The nightsong enforcer gains a +2 bonus to Hide, Move Silently, and Tumble when wearing light armor.

Trackless Step (allies)<br>
The nightsong infiltrator can grant the trackless step ability to her allies. This benefit lasts for 24 hours.

Acidic Splatter<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel magical energy into orbs of acid.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 2nd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have an acid spell of 2nd level or higher available to cast, you can throw an orb of acid as a ranged touch attack. The attack has a range of 65 feet and deals 1d3+3 points of damage per level of your highest-level acid spell memorized.<br><br>This spell gains damage from Sneak Attack and Death Attack when appropriate.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Acid Fog, Greater Shadow Conjuration, Melfs Acid Arrow, Storm of Vengeance, Mestils Acid Breath, Vitriolic Sphere, Lesser Energized Shield, Energized Shield, Weapon of Energy, Orb of Acid<br>

Clap of Thunder<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can deliver a thunderous roar with a touch.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a sonic spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can deliver a melee touch attack as a standard action. This attack deals 1d3+3 points of sonic damage per level of the highest-level sonic spell you have available to cast. Additionally, the subject must succeed on a Fortitude save or be deafened for 1 round.<br><br>This spell gains damage from Sneak Attack and Death Attack when appropriate.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Cacophonic Burst, Shout, Greater Shout, Castigate, Energized Shield, Sonic Shield, Lion's Roar, Resonating Bolt, Sound Blast, Orb of Sound, Song of Discord, Warcry, Dirge, Crumble, Leonal's Roar, and Wounding Whispers

Fiery Burst<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You channel your magical talent into a blast of fire.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 2nd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a fire spell of 2nd level or higher available to cast, you can spend a standard action to create a 20-foot-radius burst of fire at a range of 65 feet. This burst deals 1d3+3 points of fire damage per level of the highest-level fire spell you have available to cast. A successful Reflex save halves the damage. If you have levels of Mystic Reservist, there is no Reflex save. This ability does not affect allies.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Deadly Lahar, Delayed Blast Fireball, Elemental Shield, Fire Storm, Fireball, Flame Arrow, Flame Strike, Incendiary Cloud, Meteor Swarm, Shades, Wall of Fire, Firebrand, Inferno, Combust, Flame Weapon, Fireburst, Greater Fireburst, Shroud of Flame, Body of the Sun, Scorching Ray, Lesser Energized Shield, Energized Shield, Flame of Faith, Weapon of Energy, Orb of Fire, Heartfire<br>

Hurricane Breath<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>The power of elemental air you hold in your mind allows you to exhale the wind.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 2nd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a air spell of 2nd level or higher available to cast, you can attempt to knock a single creature within 30 feet back with a blast of wind. This requires a standard action and functions like a knockdown; roll 1d20 + the level of the highest-level air spell you have available to cast opposed by your opponent's Strength check. If you succeed, you knock the creature down. You gain an additional 2 points of DC from your highest mental ability.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Flaywind Burst, Gust of Wind, Elemental Swarm

Invisible Needle<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You create tiny darts of force.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a force spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can use a standard action to hurl a tiny needle-shaped projectile created from pure force. This attack requires a successful ranged touch attack roll, and the dart has a range of 65 feet. The needle deals 1d3+3 points of damage per level of the highest-level force spell you have available.<br><br>This spell gains damage from Sneak Attack and Death Attack when appropriate.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Mordenkainens Sword, Isaacs Lesser Missile Storm, Isaacs Greater Missile Storm, Bigbys Interposing Hand, Bigbys Forceful Hand, Bigbys Grasping Hand, Bigbys Clenched Fist, Bigbys Crushing Hand, Orb of Force

Minor Shapeshift<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>Your mastery of shapeshifting magic allows you to reshape your flesh in small but significant ways.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 4th-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a polymorph spell of 4th level or higher available to cast, you can instantly activate this feat to grant yourself one of the following benefits:<br><br>Might: +2 bonus on melee damage rolls.<br>Speed: +5-foot enhancement bonus to movement.<br>Vigor: Temporary hit points equal to your HD.<br><br>The chosen benefit lasts for a number of rounds equal to three times the level of the highest-level polymorph spell you have available to cast. If you activate this feat a second time while a previous feat is still in effect, the first benefit ends immediately.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Poly Self, Shapechange, Tensers Transformation<br>Having at least one use left of Wildshape allows Minor Shapeshift to be used as if a level 9 spell were memorized.

Storm Bolt<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>The electrical energy contained within your magic rages inside you, begging to be released.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a electricity spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can fire a 30-foot line of electricity as a standard action. This bolt deals 1d3+3 points of electricity damage per level of the highest-level electricity spell you have available to cast. Stormsingers gain a bonus number of dice equal to their class level as well as a boost to DC equal to half their class level. A successful Reflex save halves damage. Having levels of Mystic Reservist removes the Reflex save. This ability does not affect allies.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Arc of Lightning, Call Lightning, Chain Lightning, Lightning Bolt, Storm of Vengeance, Scintillating Sphere, Call Lightning Storm, Energized Shield, Weapon of Energy, Orb of Electricty, Lightning Blast

Sickening Grasp<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You wreak havoc with the inner organs of a target, causing it to grow ill.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a necromancy spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can make a melee touch attack against living creatures which becomes sickened for a number of rounds equal to the level of the highest-level necromancy spell you have available. The subject can reduce this duration to 1 round with a successful Fortitude save.<br><br>A sickened creature takes a -1 penalty on attack rolls, weapon damage rolls, saving throws, and skill checks for every 2 levels of the activated spell (minimum -2).<br><br>Trigger Spells: Animate Dead, Circle of Death, Mass Inflict Light Wounds, Cloudkill, Contagion, Control Undead, Create Greater Undead, Create Undead, Death Ward, Energy Drain, Enervation, Fear, Finger of Death, Harm, Poison, Slay Living, Vampiric Touch, Wail of the Banshee, Destruction, Horrid Wilting, Inflict Serious Wounds, Inflicts Critical Wounds, Infestation of Maggots, Undeath to Death, Mass Inflict Moderate Wounds, Mass Inflict Serious Wounds, Mass Inflict Critical Wounds, Mass Contagion, Mass Death Ward, Avasculate, Mass Curse of Blades, Blood of the Martyr, Scourge

Summon Elemental<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel the summoning power you hold to briefly bring forth an elemental servant.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 4th-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a summoning spell of 4th level or higher available to cast, you can summon a Small elemental (fire, earth, air, or water; your choice) within a range of 30 feet. The duration of the summoning is equal to 1 round per level of the highest-level conjuration (summoning) spell you have available to cast.<br><br>If you have a summoning spell of 6th level or higher available to cast, you summon a Medium elemental instead. If you have a summoning spell of 8th level or higher available to cast, you summon a Large elemental.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Elemental Swarm, Gate, Greater Shadow Conjuration, Shades, Shadow Conjuration, Summon Creature IV, Summon Creature IX, Summon Creature V, Summon Creature VI, Summon Creature VII, Summon Creature VIII

Winter's Blast<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>The frozen magic within you can burst forth in a hail of frost.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 2nd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a cold spell of 2nd level or higher available to cast, you can create a 30-foot cone-shaped burst of cold. This cone deals 1d3+3 points of cold damage per level of the highest-level cold spell you have available to cast. A successful Fortitude save halves the damage. Having levels of Mystic Reservist removes the Fortitude save. This ability does not affect allies.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Cone of Cold, Elemental Shield, Frost Breath, Shades, Ice Storm, Polar Ray, Hypothermia, Creeping Cold, Greater Creeping Cold, Glacial Wrath, Lesser Energized Shield, Energized Shield, Weapon of Energy, Orb Cold, Arc of Frost, and Call Frost.

Holy Warrior<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>Your zeal on the battlefield allows you to deal extra damage in combat.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 4th-level spells, War domain<br>Benefit: As long as you have a War domain spell of 4th level or higher available to cast, you gain a bonus on your weapon damage rolls equal to the level of the highest-level War spell you have available to cast (once activated). This bonus is controlled by a cap in the cmi_options and may be lower than the maximum. <br>Use: Selected.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Power Word Kill, Power Word Stun, Blade Barrier, Divine Power, Flame Strike, Power Word Blind

Touch of Healing<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel divine energy into healing with a touch.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 2nd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a conjuration(healing) spell of 2nd level or higher available to cast, you can spend a standard action to touch a target creature and heal 3 points of damage per level of the highest-level conjuration(healing) spell you have available to cast. This ability has no effect on creatures that can't be healed by cure spells. Sorcerers, Warlocks, and Wizards should NOT take this feat unless they have another casting class that has access to the listed trigger spells.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Cure Critical Wounds, Mass Cure Critical Wounds, Mass Cure Light Wounds, Cure Moderate Wounds, Mass Cure Moderate Wounds, Cure Serious Wounds, Mass Cure Serious Wounds, Heal , Mass Heal, Heal Animal Companion, Neutralize Poison, Raise Dead, Regenerate, Rejuvenation Cocoon, Remove Blindness/Deafness, Remove Disease, Remove Paralysis, Restoration, Greater Restoration, Lesser Restoration, Resurrection, Vigor, Mass Lesser Vigor, Vigorous Circle, Healing Sting<br><br>This feat is affected by several cmi_options entries.  The feat will only heal injured characters with 50% or less of their total health by default.

Umbral Shroud<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You control darkness and shadows.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a darkness spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, shadows and darkness appear to obey your will. As a standard action, you can direct these shadows to obscure the vision of one foe within 30 feet. If your target fails a Will save, its attacks have a miss chance equal to 5% per level of the highest-level darkness spell you have available to cast. This effect lasts one round plus an additional round for every 2 levels of the activating spell. If your target succeeds on a Will save, they still suffer a 5% miss chance for the duration.<br><br>Creatures that do not rely on sight are unaffected by this effect, and those with Blind-fight or similar abilities can fight as they normally would.<br><br>Trigger Spells: Mass Blindness and Deafness, Shadow Conjuration, Shadow Shield, Greater Shadow Conjuration

Team Initiative Bonus<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You are skilled at keeping a team organized and ready for danger. All allies within 30 feet gain Improved Initiative. This bonus improves to Superior Initiative at 5th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Armored Ease<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You learn to adapt your movements to the restrictive nature of armor. Beginning at 2nd level, you gain a +2 bonus to Hide, Move Silently, and Tumble. At 4th level, this bonus increases to 4 points.<br>Use: Automatic.

Darkling Weapon<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A warrior of darkness concocts a magic oil with which to coat his favorite weapon. When applied, the oil grants a new supernatural ability chosen from the list below and it lasts 4 rounds per warrior of darkness level. This ability can be used ten times per day.<br><br>List of Choices:<br>Flaming (1d6 Fire)<br>Frost (1d6 Cold)<br>Vampiric (+2)<br>Shock (1d6 Electricity)<br>Massive Criticals (2d10)<br>Use: Automatic.

Scarred Flesh<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Through ritual scarification, a 5th level warrior of darkness gains damage reduction 3/-.<br>Note: Only Greater Resiliency and the Epic Damage Reduction feats stack with this DR.<br>Use: Automatic.

Repellant Flesh<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Through ritual, obscene tattooing, an 8th-level warrior of darkness gains spell resistance equal to 10 + character level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Bladesinger Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bladesinger level 1.<br>Specifics: On every odd level, the Bladesinger progresses in one selected arcane spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which he could cast arcane spells before he became a Bladesinger, the player must decide which class to assign each level of Bladesinger for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.

Bladesinger Spellcasting Class (Sorcerer)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bladesinger level 1.<br>Specifics: On every odd level, the Bladesinger progresses in one selected arcane spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which he could cast arcane spells before he became a Bladesinger, the player must decide which class to assign each level of Bladesinger for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.

Bladesinger Spellcasting Class (Wizard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bladesinger level 1.<br>Specifics: On every odd level, the Bladesinger progresses in one selected arcane spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which he could cast arcane spells before he became a Bladesinger, the player must decide which class to assign each level of Bladesinger for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.

Bladesong Style<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When wielding a longsword or rapier in one hand (and nothing in the other), a bladesinger gains a dodge bonus to Armor Class equal to her class level, up to a maximum of her Intelligence bonus. If the bladesinger wears medium or heavy armor, she loses all benefits of the bladesong style. This bonus will not stack with the AC bonus granted by Invisible Blade or Duelist.<br>Use: Automatic.

Song of Celerity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Once per day, a bladesinger of 4th level or higher may grant her party the benefits of the haste spell for a number of rounds equal to three times her class level.  At 8th level this increases to two uses per day.<br>Use: Selected.

Song of Fury<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a 10th-level bladesinger makes a full attack with a longsword or rapier in one hand (and nothing in the other), she can make one extra attack in a round at her highest base attack bonus, but this attack and each other attack made in that round take a -2 penalty.<br>Use: Automatic.

Armored Caster (Bladesinger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bladesinger level 6<br>Specifics: At 6th level, bladesingers do not suffer spell failure penalties while wearing light armor. However, they suffer the full arcane spell failure penalties from casting spells while wearing medium or heavy armor, or while equipped with shields.<br>Use: Automatic.

Stormsinger Spellcasting Class (Spirit Shaman)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Stormsinger level 1.<br>Specifics: On every level, the Stormsinger progresses in one selected arcane, invocation, or divine spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which she could cast spells before she became a Stormsinger, the player must decide which class to assign each level of Stormsinger for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Crossbow Sniper<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Benefit: When using a crossbow for which you have the Weapon Focus feat, you gain a bonus on damage rolls equal to 1/2 your Dexterity bonus.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><br>Note: There is a cmi_options.2da entry that allows the full dex bonus to be added as a damage bonus.

Sacred Vow<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have willingly given yourself to the service of a deity or cause, denying yourself an ordinary life to better serve your highest ideals.<br>Benefit: You gain a +2 perfection bonus on Diplomacy checks.

Exalted Natural Attack<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>You can focus holy or unholy power into your natural attacks.<br>Prerequisites: BAB +5<br>Benefit: Each time you deal damage with a natural attack, you deal 1 extra point of divine damage, or 1d4 points of magic damage to outsiders and undead.

Exalted Wild Shape<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>When you use your wild shape ability you take the form of a good-aligned or evil-aligned magical beast (depending on your alignment).<br>Prerequisites: Wild Shape, +6 BAB<br>Benefit: When you use your wild shape ability you gain either the celestial (if good or neutral) or fiendish (if evil) template of the animal you can normally transform into.<br>Both Fiendish and Celestial Animals gain:<br>Spell Resistance equal to their hit dice + 5<br>Damage Reduction 10/Magic if level 12 or higher, 5/Magic otherwise.<br>Darkvision<br>Fiendish creatures gain Damage Resistance (Cold and Fire) 10 if level 8 or higher, 5 otherwise.<br>Celestial creatures gain Damage Resistance (Cold, Electricity, and Acid) 10 if level 8 or higher, 5 otherwise.

Exalted Companion<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>Instead of an animal companion, you have a celestial animal of good alignment or a fiendish animal of evil alignment.<br>Prerequisites: Animal Companion.<br>Benefit: Your animal companion gains the Celestial template if you are good or neutral and the Fiendish template if you are evil.<br>Both Fiendish and Celestial Animals gain:<br>Spell Resistance equal to their hit dice + 5<br>Damage Reduction 10/Magic if level 12 or higher, 5/Magic otherwise.<br>Attack Bonus of +4<br>Darkvision<br>Fiendish creatures gain Damage Resistance (Cold and Fire) 10 if level 8 or higher, 5 otherwise.<br>Celestial creatures gain Damage Resistance (Cold, Electricity, and Acid) 10 if level 8 or higher, 5 otherwise.

Weapon Mastery (Ranged)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have mastered a wide range of weapons.  Your training with one specific weapon now extends to other weapons of a similar sort.<br>Prerequisites: BAB +8, Weapon Specialization in one of the following weapons: Short Bow, Long Bow, Light Crossbow, Heavy Crossbow, or Sling.<br>Benefit: When using any ranged weapon, you gain a +2 bonus on attacks and a +2 bonus on damage.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Weapon Mastery (Ranged) as one of his fighter bonus feats.

Stormsong (Gust of Wind)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>The stormsinger can use her bardic music ability to create various storm-related effects in addition to the normal uses of bardic music.<br>A stormsinger of 1st level or higher with 9 or more ranks in Perform can use bardic music to generate a gust of wind, as the spell of the same name. Her caster level is equal to her ranks in Perform, with a maximum caster level of 30th.<br>Use: Selected.

Stormsong (Thunderstrike)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>The stormsinger can use her bardic music ability to create various storm-related effects in addition to the normal uses of bardic music.<br>At 3rd level, a stormsinger with 11 or more ranks in Perform can use bardic music to unleash a deadly thunderbolt. The bolt can be targeted at any one creature within 60 feet, and the stormsinger must make a successful ranged touch attack to hit the target. If she hits, the stormsinger then makes a Perform check; the result indicates how much electricity damage the thunderbolt deals (double on a critical hit). A perform check is d20 + your Perform skill (including all bonuses to Perform). A Reflex save (DC10 + stormsinger's class level + Cha modifier) halves the damage. If the creature fails its Reflex save, it must make a Fortitude save (same DC) or be deafened for a number of rounds equal to the damage dealt.<br>Use: Selected.

Stormsong (Call Lightning)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>The stormsinger can use her bardic music ability to create various storm-related effects in addition to the normal uses of bardic music.<br>A stormsinger of 5th level or higher with 13 or more ranks in Perform can use bardic music to generate a Call Lightning effect, as the spell of the same name. Her caster level is equal to her ranks in Perform, with a maximum caster level of 30th.<br>Use: Selected.

Stormsong (Winter's Ballad)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>The stormsinger can use her bardic music ability to create various storm-related effects in addition to the normal uses of bardic music.<br>A stormsinger of 7th level or higher with 15 or more ranks in Perform can use bardic music to generate a Greater Call Lightning effect, as the spell of the same name. Her caster level is equal to her ranks in Perform, with a maximum caster level of 30th.<br>Use: Selected.

Stormsong (Great Thunderstrike)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>The stormsinger can use her bardic music ability to create various storm-related effects in addition to the normal uses of bardic music.<br>At 9th level, a stormsinger with 17 or more ranks in Perform can use bardic music to unleash a terrible stroke of lightning, bollowed by a deafening clap of thunder. The great thunderstrike affects a line 60 feet long from the stormsinger. The stormsinger makes a Perform check; the results indicate how much electricity damage the great thunderstrike deals (double on a critical). A Reflex save (DC10 + stormsinger's class level + Cha modifier) halves the damage. If the creature fails its Reflex save, it must make a Fortitude save (same DC) or take an additional 2d6 points of sonic damage and be permanently deafened.<br><br>The great thunderstrike is very strenuous, and uses up two of the stormsinger's bardic music uses for the day.<br>Use: Selected.

Stormsong (Storm of Vengeance)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>The stormsinger can use her bardic music ability to create various storm-related effects in addition to the normal uses of bardic music.<br>At 10th level, a stormsinger with 18 or more ranks in Perform can use bardic music to cause a storm of vengeance, as the spell of the same name (DC 10 + stormsinger's class level + Cha modifier). Her caster level is equal to her ranks in Perform, with a maximum caster level of 35th. This potent ability is quite exhausting to use; each time it is activated, the stormsinger uses four of her bardic music uses for the day.<br>Use: Selected.

Bardic Music (Stormsinger)<br>
A stormsinger's class level stacks with any levels of bard she has for purposes of determining the number of times per day she may use bardic music. Many of the stormsinger's high-level abilities require her bardic music as well. When a stormsinger uses her bardic music ability to create a stormsong effect (see below), it counts as one (or more) uses of her bardic music for the day.

Stormpower<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 2nd level, a stormsinger gains a +2 bonus on Perform checks to use her stormsong powers and adds a +2 bonus to her caster level with stormsong powers.<br>Use: Automatic.

Resistance to Electricity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 4th level, a stormsinger gains resistance to electricity 5. This increases to resistance to electricity 10 at 6th, and resistance to electricity 15 at 8th.<br>Use: Automatic.

Anointed Weapon<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An anointed knight concocts a magic oil with which to coat her ancestral weapon. Once the oil is applied, the ancestral weapon gains a new supernatural ability chosen from the list below for 4 rounds per level. This ability can be used ten times per day.<br><br>List of Choices:<br>Flaming (1d6 Fire)<br>Frost (1d6 Cold)<br>Vampiric (+2)<br>Shock (1d6 Electricity)<br>Massive Criticals (2d10)<br>Use: Automatic.

Unbroken Flesh<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: The anointed knight uses a special concoction of magical oils to apply to her flesh at 5th-level granting permanent damage reduction of 3/-.<br>Note: Only Greater Resiliency and the Epic Damage Reduction feats stack with this DR.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sacred Flesh<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Through sacred rituals and special magical oils, an 8th-level anointed knight gains spell resistance equal to 10 + character level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Lion of Talisid level 1.<br>Specifics: At every level, the Lion of Talisid progresses in one selected divine spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which she could cast spells before she became a Lion of Talsid, the player must decide which class to assign each level of Lion of Talsid for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.

Lion's Companion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>A Lion of Talisid can add his class levels to his effective druid level (his actual druid level or adjusted ranger level) to determine the capabilities of his animal companion.<br>Use: Automatic.

Lion's Courage<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>A Lion of Talisid is immune to fear and gains a +4 sacred bonus on Will saves against other mind-affecting spells and effects.<br>Use: Automatic.

Lion's Swiftness<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>When he reaches 7th level, a Lion of Talisid can act as if under the effects of a haste spell for a total of 1 round per class level. At 7th level he has 7 uses/day and gains another use for each additional Lion of Talisid level (10 uses/day at 10th level). This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Leonal's Roar<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Three times a day, a 10th-level Lion of Talisid can issue a roar that duplicates the effects of the leonal's roar spell (see below). The saving throw DC is 20 + the lion of Talisid's Charisma modifier.<br>Use: Selected.<br><br>Range: 40'<br>Area of Effect / Target: Enemies within 40'<br>Duration: Varies (see details)<br>Save: Fortitude partial (see details)<br>Spell Resistance: Yes<br><br>Any nongood creature within the area of a holy word spell suffers the following ill effects:<br><br>2d6 Sonic damage. A successful Fortitude saving throw negates the sonic damage, but not the other effects of the spell.<br><br>The effects of the holy word are:<br><br>Equal to caster level: Dazed <br>Up to caster level -1: Weakened, dazed <br>Up to caster level -5: Paralyzed, weakened, dazed <br>Up to caster level -10: Killed, paralyzed, weakened, dazed <br><br>The effects are cumulative and concurrent. <br>No saving throw is allowed against these effects. <br><br>Dazed<br>The creature can take no actions for 1 round, though it defends itself normally. <br><br>Weakened<br>The creature’s Strength score decreases by 2d6 points for 2d4 rounds. <br><br>Paralyzed<br>The creature is paralyzed and helpless for 1d10 minutes. <br><br>Killed<br>Living creatures die. Undead creatures are destroyed. <br><br>Furthermore, if you are on your home plane when you cast this spell, nongood extraplanar creatures within the area are instantly banished back to their home planes. Creatures so banished cannot return for at least 24 hours. This effect takes place regardless of whether the creatures hear the holy word. The banishment effect allows a Will save (at a -4 penalty) to negate. <br><br>Creatures whose Hit Dice exceed your caster level are unaffected by holy word. <br>

Swiftblade Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Swiftblade level 1.<br>Specifics: At levels 2,3,5,6,8, and 9 you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. If you had more than one arcane spellcasting class before becoming a swiftblade, you must decide which class gains the increased casting ability.<br>Use: Automatic.

Swiftblade Spellcasting Class (Sorcerer)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Swiftblade level 1.<br>Specifics: At levels 2,3,5,6,8, and 9 you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. If you had more than one arcane spellcasting class before becoming a swiftblade, you must decide which class gains the increased casting ability.<br>Use: Automatic.

Swiftblade Spellcasting Class (Wizard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Swiftblade level 1.<br>Specifics: At levels 2,3,5,6,8, and 9 you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. If you had more than one arcane spellcasting class before becoming a swiftblade, you must decide which class gains the increased casting ability.<br>Use: Automatic.

Swift Surge<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Your body is augmented with the residual energy of previous haste castings. You gain a +1 bonus on attack rolls, a +1 dodge bonus to AC, and a +1 bonus to Reflex saves. At 7th level, these bonuses increase to +2 and you deal an extra 1d6 points of damage.  At 10th level you gain 2d6 points of bonus damage. These bonuses stack with the bonuses gained from the haste spell.<br>Use: Automatic.

Blurred Alacrity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 2nd level, your understanding of the haste spell makes you difficult to target with melee and ranged attacks. While under the effect of a haste spell that you cast yourself, you gain concealment (20% miss chance). This miss chance increases by 10% at 3rd, 4th, and 5th level. The effect of this ability does not stack with blur, displacement, or similar spell effects.<br>Use: Automatic.

Residual Celerity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 3rd level, your body retains more of the energy imbued by the haste spell allowing it to last longer. When casting the haste spell on yourself the duration is increased by 3 rounds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Evasive Celerity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 5th level, your knowledge of the haste spell makes you difficult to target with spells. While under the effect of a haste spell that you cast yourself, you gain a Spell Resistance equal to 10 + character level. The effect of this ability does not stack with other effects that grant spell resistance.<br>Use: Automatic.

Fortified Hustle<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 6th level, you learn how to intuitively absorb the haste spell into your body. While under the effect of a haste spell that you cast yourself, the effect becomes extraordinary rather than a normal spell effect, and therefore cannot be dispelled by any means.<br>Use: Automatic.

Diligent Rapidity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 8th level, you automatically overcome magic and mundane obstacles with the haste spell. While under the effect of a haste spell that you cast yourself, you can move and attack normally, even under the influence of magic that usually impedes movement, as the freedom of movement spell.<br>Use: Automatic.

Perpetual Celerity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 9th level, the haste spell lingers even longer in your body before it dissipates. When casting the haste spell on yourself the duration is increased by 6 rounds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Innervated Speed<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 10th level, your mastery of the haste spell seems to bring the world around you to a standstill. This effect is comparable to the slow spell with a 100' radius effect and lasting 10 rounds. This burst of speed is available for use once every five minutes. The DC of the ability is 19 + the higher of your Int or Cha modifier.<br>Use: Selected.

Shock Blade<br>
Twice per day as a swift action, a shining blade can cause a melee weapon he is holding to become a shock weapon, dealing an extra 1d6 points of electricity damage on a successful hit.  The weapon is enchanced this way for up to a number of rounds equal to the shining blade's level plus his Charisma modifier (if any), but only so long as the shining blade is holding the weapon. This increases to 3 times/day at 3rd level, 4 times/day at 5th, 5 times/day at 7th, and 6 times/day at 9th.<br><br>At 9th level the Shocking Blade and Holy Blade abilities will also grant a +2 attack bonus. If both abilities are used on the same weapon the bonus will stack.

Divine Armor<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Turn Undead, Minimum caster level 5.<br>Specifics: The character may spend one of his turn undead attempts to gain DR 5/- for a number of rounds equal to the Charisma bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Divine Fortune<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Turn Undead, Minimum caster level 5.<br>Specifics: The character may spend one of his turn undead attempts to gain +4 Saving Throws for a number of rounds equal to the Charisma bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Divine Cleansing<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Turn Undead.<br>Specifics: The character may spend one of his turn undead attempts to grant all allies (including yourself) within a 60-foot burst a +2 Sacred bonus on Fortitude saving throws for a number of turns equal to the Charisma bonus.<br>Use: Selected.<br><i>You can channel energy to improve your allies' ability to resist attacks against their vitality and health.</i>

Divine Vigor<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Turn Undead.<br>Specifics: The character may spend one of his turn undead attempts to gain a 33% movement speed buff as well as gain +2 temporary hit points per character level. These effects last a number of minutes equal to your Charisma bonus.<br>Use: Selected.<br><i>You can channel energy to increase your speed and durability.<i>

Master of Radiance Spellcasting Class (Spirit Shaman)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Master of Radiance level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, the Master of Radiance progresses in one selected divine spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which she could cast spells before she became a Master of Radiance, the player must decide which class to progress for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.

If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which she could cast spells before she became a Master of Radiance, the player must decide which class to progress for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known.<br>Use: Automatic.

Radiant Aura<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>A master of radiance can emanate an aura of brilliant light that weakens undead creatures. The aura provides bright illumination in a 30-foot radius around the character, and shadowy illumination for an additional 30 feet beyond that. Undead creatures (and those sensitive to light) take a -2 penalty on attack rolls, armor class, and saving throws. Each use lasts 1 minute. It also dispells any darkness spell of 5th level or lower in the area.<br>Use: Selected.<br>A master of radiance has 1 use/day at 1st level, 2 uses/day at 3rd level, and 3 uses/day at 5th level.

Searing Light<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Beginning at 2nd level, a master of radiance can use <i>searing light</i> once per round as long as her radiant aura is active. Treat the character's caster level for this effect as the equal to her highest divine caster level + 2.<br>Use: Selected.

Beam of Sunlight<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Beam of Sunlight: A 5th-level master of radiance can evoke a dazzling beam of intense light (the equivalent of a beam from the sunbeam spell) once per round as long as her radiant aura is active. Treat the character's caster level for this effect as the equal to her highest divine caster level + 2.<br>Use: Selected.

Battle Caster (Bladesinger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Bladesinger level 8<br>Specifics: At 8th level, bladesingers do not suffer spell failure penalties from wearing medium armor. However, they suffer the full arcane spell failure penalties from casting spells while wearing heavy armor or while equipped with shields.<br>Use: Automatic.

Illusion Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Illusion domain seek to weave illusions and confound the enemy, and are granted spells that reflect their deceitful nature.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Spell Focus Illusion: The cleric is better able to deceive enemies.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Blur (2), Invisibility Sphere (3), Phantasmal Killer (4), Weird(9)</i>.

Pride Domain<br>
Clerics who take the Pride domain seek to improve their self image, and are granted spells that reflect their ambitious nature.<br><br><b>Domain Special Ability and Bonus Spells</b><br>- Epic Resilience: The character no longer automatically fails saving throws on a roll of 1. The character will still fail the save if their result fails to equal or beat the DC.<br><br>- The cleric gains access to the following spells at the specified spell level: <i>Heroism (3), Greater Heroism (7), Mass Charm Monster (9)</i>.

Fast Healing I<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: Con 25<br>Specifics: You gain 3 points of regeneration.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><i>You heal your wounds very quickly.</i>

Fast Healing II<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: Con 25, Fast Healing I<br>Specifics: You gain 6 points of regeneration.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><i>You heal your wounds very quickly.</i>

Greater Two-Weapon Defense<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: Dex 19, Improved Two-Weapon Fighting, Improved Two-Weapon Defense, BAB +11<br>Benefit: When wielding two weapons, you gain a +3 shield bonus to your AC.<br>When you are fighting defensively this shield bonus increases to +6.<br>Special: A fighter may select Greater Two-Weapon Defense as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br><i>When fighting with two weapons, your defenses are extraordinarily strong.</i>

Beckon the Frozen<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Creatures you summon are infused with cold energy and have the cold subtype.<br>Prerequisites: Augment Summoning, Spell Focus (Conjuration)<br>Benefit: When you summon a creature using any summon spell, the summoned creature gains the cold subtype (100% cold immunity, 50% fire vulnerability, and its natural attacks deal an additional 1d6 points of cold damage.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>Affected Spells: Animate Dead, Create Undead, Epic Gate, Gate, Glass Doppleganger, Greater Create Undead, Greater Planar Binding, Lesser Planar Binding, Mordenkainen’s Sword, Palemaster's Animate Dead, Palemaster's Summon Undead, Planar Ally, Planar Binding, Shadow Simulacrum, Summon Creature I-IX, Summon Fiendish Servant, The Dead Walk

Toxic Gift<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>Prerequisites: Plant Shape<br>Benefit: You can expend one daily use of wild shape to gain +1d4 bonus acid damage on all attacks for 2 rounds per caster level you possess. This is an instantly activated feat.<br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Essence<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>Prerequisites: Wild Shape<br>Benefit: You can expend one daily use of wild shape to surround your limbs and weapons with an aura of the chosen energy. While this effect is active, each attack you make deals an extra 1d6 points of the appropriate type of energy damage. The energy damage types available are acid, cold, electricity, and fire. Additionally, you gain resistance 5 against that type of energy. These effects last for 20 rounds. This is an instantly activated feat.<br>Use: Selected.

Armor Specialization (Medium)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: BAB +12, Medium Armor Proficiency<br>Benefit: When wearing medium armor you gain damage reduction 3/-. This DR does not stack with any other source of DR.<br>Special: A fighter may select Armor Specialization (Medium) as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><i>Through long wear and hours of combat, you have trained your body to believe in its armor. Where others flinch, you confront. When the sword falls, your instincts, born of bruises and rent flesh, present your cuirass, cuisse, helm, or gorget to meet the blade at the perfect angle, causing it to skitter off harmlessly.</i>

Armor Specialization (Heavy)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: BAB +12, Heavy Armor Proficiency<br>Benefit: When wearing heavy armor you gain damage reduction 5/-. This DR does not stack with any other source of DR.<br>Special: A fighter may select Armor Specialization (Heavy) as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><i>Through long wear and hours of combat, you have trained your body to believe in its armor. Where others flinch, you confront. When the sword falls, your instincts, born of bruises and rent flesh, present your cuirass, cuisse, helm, or gorget to meet the blade at the perfect angle, causing it to skitter off harmlessly.</i>

Epic Inspiration<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: Bardic music, Charisma 25, Perform 30.<br>Benefit: You gain a +2 bonus to your bardic music inspiration abilities. This bonus stacks with the bonus from Song of the Heart.<br><i>Your bardic music provides greater inspiration than normally possible.<i>

Song of the Heart<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: Bardic music, Inspire Competence, Perform 6.<br>Benefit: When you use a bard song, any bonus granted by your music increases by +1. Thus, at 15th level bard with this feat grants his allies a +4 bonus on attack rolls, damage rolls, and saving throws. Cloud Mind, Fascinate, Song of Haven, and Inspire Slowing have their DC increased by 1. Countersong has it's SR bonus increased by 1.<br>Affected Songs: inspire courage, inspire competence, inspire defense, inspire regeneration, inspire toughness, inspire heroics, inspire slowing, clound mind, song of haven, countersong, and fascinate.<br><i>Your bardic music reaches the depths of its listener's hearts.<i>

Racial Spell (Faerie Fire)<br>
Type of Feat: Race<br>Prerequisite: Drow.<br>Specifics: This feat grants the ability to cast Faerie Fire as per the spell once per day (caster level equal to character level).<br>Use: Selected.

Daylight Endurance<br>
You have gained the Daylight Adaption feat from Zehir.

Oversized Two-Weapon Fighting<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: Str 13, Two-Weapon Fighting.<br>Benefit: When wielding a one-handed weapon in your off hand, you gain a +2 Attack Bonus if the weapon is larger than a light weapon. This feat will not work for Halflings or Gnomes at this time.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Oversized Two-Weapon Fighting as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br><i>You are adept at wielding larger than normal weapons in your off hand.</i><br>Valid weapons are Bastard Sword, Battle Axe, Club, Dwarven Waraxe, Falchion, Great Axe, Great Club, Great Sword, Halberd, Katana, Light Flail, Longsword, Magic Staff, Morningstar, Quarterstaff, Rapier, Scimitar, Scythe, Spear, Universal Sword (Silver Sword), Warhammer, and War Mace

Penetrating Shot<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You send a powerful shot cleaving through your enemies.<br>Prerequisites: Str 15, Point Blank Shot, BAB +10<br>Benefit: You can choose to make a single, mighty attack that blasts through multiple opponents with a ranged weapon. This attack requires a standard action, and your shot takes the form of a 60-foot line. Make a seperate attack roll against each creature in the line. If struck, creatures along this line take damage from your shot, though any extra damage (such as from a sneak attack or a flaming weapon) is applied only against the first creature struck.<br>Special: A fighter can select Penetrating Shot as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br>Use: Selected.

Battle Dancer<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: BAB +2, Bard 5 or Swashbuckler 5<br>Benefit: You gain a +2 morale bonus on your attack rolls.<br>Special: A fighter can select Battle Dancer as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br><i>You strike at your foes in rime with the music you sing or in cadence with an oration you deliver. The magical power of your bardic performance drives you forward and improves your fighting ability.</i>

Fiery Fist<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: BAB +8, Dex 13, Wis 13, Improved Unarmed Strike, Stunning Fist<br>Benefit: You can expend one of your uses of the Stunning Fist feat to surround your fists and feet in flame. You gain an extra 1d6 points of fire damage on your unarmed strikes for a number of rounds equal to 1/2 your Monk level + your Wisdom modifier (minimum 1).<br>Special: A fighter can choose Fiery Fist as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br><i>By channeling your ki energy, you sheathe your limbs in magical fire. Your unarmed strikes deal extra fire damage.</i>

Fiery Ki Defense<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: BAB +8, Dex 13, Wis 13, Fiery Fist, Improved Unarmed Strike, Stunning Fist<br>Benefit: You can expend one of your uses of the Stunning Fist feat to cloak yourself in flame. Any creature that strikes you with a melee attack takes 1d6 points of fire damage. This benefit lasts for a number of rounds equal to 1/2 your monk level + your Wisdom modifier (minimum 1).<br>Special: A fighter can choose Fiery Ki Defense as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br><i>You channel your ki energy into a cloak of flame that injures all who attempt to strike you.</i>

Weapon Mastery (Unarmed)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have mastered combat with hand to hand and natural weapons.<br>Prerequisites: BAB +8, Weapon Specialization in Unarmed Strike.<br>Passive Benefit: When using natural or no weapons, you gain a +2 bonus on attacks and a +2 bonus on damage.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Weapon Mastery (Unarmed) as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br>You may not hold anything in your offhand for this ability to work. <br><br>Polymorph effects suffer a scaling penalty that is based on the number of monk levels they have. The AB penalty is equal to 2 plus whatever AB they would have gotten from the new monk feats (weapon focus unarmed, greater weapon focus unarmed, epic weapon focus unarmed, and unarmed combat mastery) which leads to a net 2 AB penalty. The damage penalty is equal to whatever damage bonus they would have gotten from the new feats (weapon specialization unarmed, greater weapon specialization unarmed, epic weapon specialization unarmed, and unarmed combat mastery).

Forest Master Spellcasting Class (Cleric)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Forest Master level 1.<br>Specifics: At levels 1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 9, and 10 you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in a divine spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. If you had more than one divine spellcasting class before becoming a forest master, you must decide which class gains the increased casting ability.<br>Use: Automatic.

Tree Brother<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: The forest master may assume tree shape once per day granting the equivalent of the sanctuary spell.<br>Use: Selected.

Forest Dominion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 2nd level, the forest master gains the ability to damage all plants within 30 ft. His effective level for this ability is his class level (plus any cleric levels if you have the Plant domain), and he may use it eight times per day. The damage done is equal to 1d6 per effective level with a Will save for half (DC is 10 + effective level + Cha modifier).<br>Use: Selected.

Forest's Hammer<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Beginning at 2nd level, a forest master begins to awaken magical abilities within the wood and metal that make up his warmace. Any warmace used by a forest master in his right hand is treated as if it were a +2 weapon with either the frost or shock property (the forest master can change which of these elements infuse the weapon at will).<br><br>Beginning at 6th-level, the forest master's warmace acts as a +2 icy burst or shocking burst weapon (as the forest master's chooses).<br><br>At 9th level, the forest master's warmace acts as a +3 mighty cleaving weapon in addition to the elemental properties. The mighty cleaving property grants the Great Cleave feat temporarily.<br>Use: Automatic.

Oaken Skin<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, the forest master's skin changes into a tough, bark-like hide, granting him natural armor +3. This bonus increases to +5 at 5th level and +7 at 7th level. This is an enhance bonus and does not stack with any other natural AC bonus.<br>Use: Automatic.

Spruce Growth<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 5th level, once per day the forest master can temporarily increase his size and strength as if using a <i>righteous might</i> spell.<br>Use: Selected.

Oakheart<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Upon reaching 7th level, the forest master's body becomes a thing of wood and leaf rather than meat and bone. His type changes to plant. As such, he is immune to mind-affecting effects, poison, sleep, paralysis, stunning, and hostile polymorphing. He is no longer subject to critical hits or sneak attacks. However, the forest master becomes vulnerable to fire, and suffers double damage from fire attacks if he fails a Reflex saving throw, or half damage if he succeeds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Deep Roots<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Beginning at 8th level, once per day the forest master may sink roots into the ground. While rooted, the forest master gains fast healing 5, but has an effective Dexterity score of 1 and may not move from the spot in which he stands or the effect ends. Deep roots will last for 48 hours or until you move one foot or more from where you used the ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Forest Might<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level, the primordial forest infuses the forest master with power. He gains a +2 bonus to Strength (thus +4 total), +2 bonus to Constitution, and he takes only half damage from piercing weapons. He also gains 1 point of regeneration.  These stat increases are through bonus feats (Great Strength III and IV, Great Constituation I and II).<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Spell: Epic Gate<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 27, the ability to cast 9th level spells (6th for Bards).<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Epic Gate.<br> <br>Classes: Bard, Cleric, Druid, Spirit Shaman, Wizard, Sorcerer, Warlock, Arcane Scholar of Candlekeep, Arcane Trickster, Eldrich Knight, Pale Master, Red Wizard, Stormlord, Harper Agent, Sacred Fist, Warpriest<br>Spellcraft Required: 27<br>Caster Level: Epic<br>Innate Level: Epic<br>School: Conjuration<br>Descriptor(s): <br>Components: Verbal, Somatic<br>Range: Personal<br>Area of Effect / Target: Point<br>Duration: 40 rounds<br>Save: None<br>Spell Resistance: No<br><br>This spell opens a portal to the another plane and calls forth a creature to assail your foes. If it is slain, a second one is immediately summoned in its place.  The strength of this conjuration is such that the creatures are bound to your will, and you need not have cast Protection from Evil, or any similar spell, to prevent them from attacking you. Evil casters summon a balor, good casters summon a solar, and neutral casters summon an earth elemental monolith (all summons have identical stats).<br><br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Shot<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At first level, the elemental archer channels their chosen plane through their hands, causing their shots to strike with elemental damage. For each level of Elemental Archer, the player gains 1 point of elemental damage on each shot made. Genasi gain a +2 elemental damage bonus if they choose the same element as their heritage (stacking with the bonus from elemental shot).<br>Use: Automatic.

Accurate Shot<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At level 2 their accuracy at channeling the elements improves, granting them a +1 attack bonus.  This bonus improves to +2 at level 4.<br>Use: Automatic.

Elemental Shield<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, the elemental archer is capable of directing their chosen elemental energy into a protective shield about them once every 5 minutes.  They gain a damage shield of their element that also adds a shield AC bonus. The shield AC bonus is equal to half their class level (+3 at level 5) and the damage shield's damage is d4 + half their class level (d4 + 3 at 5th).  It lasts for a number of rounds equal to their con bonus plus their class level. The elemental archer is able to use this shield more often once they achieve 5th level (every 2 minutes, 30 seconds).<br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Storm<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Beginning at 5th level, an elemental archer can tap the raw fury of their native plane and direct it against a group of foes.  You unleash a hail of attacks imbued with elemental damage in a 40' circle, dealing your weapon damage + 2d6 of your chosen element if you succeed with a ranged touch attack. You can affect up to 5 targets.<br>Use: Selected.

Improved Elemental Shield<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: The elemental shield adds an AC bonus equal to class level instead of half of their class level.  The damage dealt improves to d4 + class level. Duration is doubled.<br>Use: Selected.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Improved Elemental Shield as one of his fighter bonus feats.

Improved Elemental Storm<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: The elemental storm becomes a raging conflagration of energy. The damage bonus increases to 4d6 and the number of targets increases to 10.<br>Use: Selected.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Improved Elemental Storm as one of his fighter bonus feats.

Elemental Path of Air<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You have chosen to follow the path of Air, channeling the power of the elemental plane. Your attacks become infused with electricity and with enough practice you will be able to shield yourself with electricity as well.

Elemental Path of Earth<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You have chosen to follow the path of Earth, channeling the power of the elemental plane. Your attacks become infused with acid and with enough practice you will be able to shield yourself with acid as well.

Elemental Path of Fire<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You have chosen to follow the path of Fire, channeling the power of the elemental plane. Your attacks become infused with fire and with enough practice you will be able to shield yourself with fire as well.

Elemental Path of Water<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You have chosen to follow the path of Water, channeling the power of the elemental plane. Your attacks become infused with cold and with enough practice you will be able to shield yourself with cold as well.

Deadly Defense<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Combat Expertise<br>Specifics: Benefit: When using Combat Expertise (or Improved Combat Expertise), you deal an extra 1d6 points of damage with any light weapon or with any weapon to which the Weapon Finesse feat applies (such as Rapier). This benefit only applies when you are unarmored or wearing light armor and not using a shield.<br>Special: A fighter can select Deadly Defense as one of his bonus feats.<br>Use: Automatic<br><i>You are at your most dangerous when forced to protect yourself.</i>

Dragon Disciple<br>
Your draconic ancestor's blood beckons irresistibly and you seek to use your magical power as a catalyst to ignite your dragon blood in order to realize its fullest potential. You draw on the potent blood of the dragon coursing in your veins.

Draconic Immunity<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Disciple level 10 or Dragon Shaman level 9.<br>Specifics: The character gains immunity to their dragon's element. Black, Green, and Copper followers gain Acid Immunity. Blue and Bronze followers gain Lightning Immunity. White and Silver followers gain Cold Immunity. Red, Brass, and Gold followers gain Fire Immunity.<br>Use: Automatic

Rage (Standard)<br>
Specifics: The barbarian may rage, gaining a +4 morale bonus to Strength and Constitution and +2 to all Will saving throws, in exchange for a -2 penalty to Armor Class. Rage lasts for 3 + (Con modifier after rage bonus) rounds. After the rage effect expires, the barbarian is fatigued and suffers a -2 Strength, -2 Dexterity and a -10% movement penalty for 5 rounds (at level 17, the barbarian gets Tireless Rage and no longer deals with this penalty). At 11th level, the rage ability becomes greater rage, giving the barbarian +6 to Strength and Consitution and +3 to Will saves (the -2 penalty to Armor Class still applies). At 20th level, the rage ability becomes mighty rage. The Strength and Constitution bonuses increase to +8, and the Will save bonus to +4. The AC penalty remains -2.<br>Use: Selected. The barbarian may use this ability once per day at first level, +1 use per day at level 4, and every 4 levels thereafter.  The feat has a cooldown timer that lasts as long as the length of the rage and fatigue (if any).

Rage (Whirlwind Frenzy)<br>
Specifics:  A barbarian with this variant form of rage doesn't gain the normal bonuses when he enters a rage. Instead, when a barbarian with whirling frenzy enters a rage, he temporarily gains a +4 bonus to Strength and a +2 dodge bonus to Armor Class and on Reflex saves. While in a whirling frenzy, the barbarian may make one extra attack in a round at his highest base attack bonus, but this attack takes a -2 penalty, as does each other attack made that round. This extra attack does not stack with Haste and uses the haste improvement instead (no penalty applied to all attacks).<br><br>Whirling frenzy is otherwise identical to the standard barbarian rage in all other ways. At 11th level (when a standard barbarian gains greater rage), the Strength bonus increases to +6, and the dodge bonus to Armor Class and on Reflex saves increases to +3. At 20th level (when a standard barbarian gains mighty rage), the Strength bonus increases to +8, and the dodge bonus to Armor Class and on Reflex saves increases to +4. This bonus increases to +10 Strength and +5 AC/Reflex saves with the Epic Rage feat.<br><br>A barbarian using this variant doesn't gain indomitable will at 14th level. Instead, he gains evasion, but only while in a whirling frenzy. <br><br>Use: Selected. The barbarian may use this ability once per day at first level, +1 use per day at level 4, and every 4 levels thereafter.  The feat has a cooldown timer that lasts as long as the length of the rage and fatigue (if any).

Nature's Warrior Spellcasting Class (Druid)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Nature's Warrior level 1.<br>Specifics: At levels 2 and 4 you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in a both druid and ranger.<br>Use: Automatic.

Nature's Warrior Spellcasting Class (Ranger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Nature's Warrior level 1.<br>Specifics: At levels 2 and 4 you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in a both druid and ranger.<br>Use: Automatic.

Wilding<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Nature's warrior class levels stack with druid levels (along with Lion of Talisid and Daggerspell Shaper) to determine wild shape abilities.

Armor of the Crocodile<br>
The nature's warrior's natural armor bonus is improved by +1 per class level in all forms. <b>Note: This "stacking" bonus only applies when you change shapes. It does not affect your natural AC if it later changes due to items or spells.</b>

Blaze of Power<br>
While in fire elemental form, the nature's warrior is covered in a blaze of power, which functions as a warm fire shield at a caster level equal to her druid level (if any) plus her ranger level (if any) plus her nature's warrior level.

Claws of the Grizzly<br>
The nature's warrior gains a +3 bonus on damage when using her natural weapons in all forms.

Earth's Resilience<br>
The nature's warrior gains DR 3/- in all forms.

Robe of Clouds<br>
While in air elemental form, her body is wreathed in mist and clouds which is equivalent to concealment (20%).

Wild Growth<br>
The nature's warrior gains fast healing 1 in all forms.

Citadel Training<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 1st level, you gain a +2 insight bonus for the Diplomacy and Search skills.<br>Use: Automatic.

Heartwarder Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Heartwarder level 1.<br>Specifics: At every level you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane, invocation, or divine-based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a heartwarder, you must decide which class gains the increased casting ability. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.
