Heart of Passion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 2nd level, a heartwarder displays such passionate belief in whatever she does or says that she can sway the thoughts of the most rigid critic. This ability translates into a +2 bonus on all Charisma-based skill checks (Bluff, Diplomacy, Intimidate, Perform, Use Magic Device).<br>Use: Automatic.

Fey Metamorphosis<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level, a heartwarder is so attuned to the forces of natural beauty that she transcends mortal definitions of beauty. She gains the low-light vision ability and becomes immune to charm and dominate.<br>Use: Automatic.

Lips of Rapture<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 4th level, a heartwarder's kiss confers a state of bliss upon its recipient, conferring a +2 morale bonus on attacks, weapon damage, checks, and saves. This ability lasts 5 rounds, and can be used five times per day. The one downside to a heartwarder's kiss is that it also dazes the subject for one round.<br>Use: Selected.

Armor of Frost<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th level, a frost mage's body becomes more like the ice he venerates. His skin turns whiter and colder to the touch as the permanent layer of frost grows deeper. This provides an increase to the character's existing natural armor starting at +1 and increasing by +1 each time to +4 at 10th. This is an enhance bonus and does not stack with other Natural AC bonuses.<br>Use: Automatic.

Piercing Cold<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 6th level, the frost mage gains the Piercing Cold ability.  This allows a frost mage to ignore any cold resistance or immunity with his cold spells.  <br>Affected Spells: Burst of Glacial Wrath, Cone of Cold, Creeping Cold, Greater Creeping Cold, Hypothermia, Ice Storm, Lesser Orb of Cold, Orb of Cold, Polar Ray, Ray of Frost, Lesser Aura of Cold, Aura of Cold, and Frost Breath.<br>Use: Automatic.

One with the Cold<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level, the frost mage's body has become perfectly adapted to cold energy. He gains the cold subtype, granting him immunity to cold. His oneness with cold, however, makes him more susceptible to flame. He gains vulnerability to fire, which means he takes half again as much (+50%) damage as normal from fire.<br>Use: Automatic.

Hunter's Immunities<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: As she grows in power, a skullclan hunter develops immunities to special attacks that are common among undead creatures. At 3rd level, she gains immunity to all fear. At 4th level, she gains immunity to disease. At 7th level she gains immunity to paralysis. At 8th level she gains immunity to ability drain or damage. At 10th level she gains immunity to energy drain.<br>Use: Automatic.

Protection from Evil<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 4th level, a skullclan hunter benefits from a permanent <i>protection from evil</i> effect upon herself.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sword of Light<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Any weapon wielded by a skullclan hunter of 5th level or higher gains a 1d6 divine bonus damage against undead.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sword of Darkness<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Any weapon wielded by a skullclan hunter of 9th level or higher gains a 2d6 divine bonus damage against undead (replacing Sword of Light).<br>Use: Automatic.

Elegant Strike<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Upon reaching 2nd level, you become able to place your attacks where they deal greater damage. You apply your Dexterity bonus as a bonus on damage rolls (in addition to any Strength bonus you may have) with any of the following weapons: longsword, rapier, scimitar, or universal sword.<br>Use: Automatic. (This bonus is calculated and applied when you equip/unequip items)

Superior Defense<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, you learn special techniques for making the best use of your natural agility even while wearing restrictive armor. You gain a +1 Dodge AC bonus at 3rd level. At 6th level the bonus improves to +2 and at 9th level it increases by +3.<br>Use: Automatic.

Wrath of the Wild<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 8th level, you gain the ability to briefly channel the divine power of a wild deity, enveloping one melee weapon you wield in a veil of incandescent light. You gain a +2 sacred bonus on attack rolls and deal an extra 2d6 points of damage with each melee attack you make for a number of rounds equal to 4 + your Cha modifier.<br>Use: Selected.

Extra Wrath of the Wild I<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 8th level, you gain the ability to briefly channel the divine power of a wild deity, enveloping one melee weapon you wield in a veil of incandescent light. You gain a +2 sacred bonus on attack rolls and deal an extra 2d6 points of damage with each melee attack you make for a number of rounds equal to 4 + your Cha modifier.<br>Use: Selected.

Extra Wrath of the Wild II<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 8th level, you gain the ability to briefly channel the divine power of a wild deity, enveloping one melee weapon you wield in a veil of incandescent light. You gain a +2 sacred bonus on attack rolls and deal an extra 2d6 points of damage with each melee attack you make for a number of rounds equal to 4 + your Cha modifier.<br>Use: Selected.

Dragon Breath (15')<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Shaman level 4.<br>Specifics: At 4th level, you gain a breath weapon corresponding to your totem dragon. All breath weapons deal 2d6 points of damage, plus an extra 1d6 points of damage for every two addtional class levels (3d6 at 6th level, 4d6 at 8th level, and so forth). A successful Reflex save halves the damage dealth; the save DC is equal to 10 + 1/2 your Dragon Shaman level + your Con modifier. This ability has a 15 second cooldown before it can be used again.<br><br>The breath weapon is a cone shaped blast of energy dependent on your dragon choice (see below) that initially extends out to 15 feet. This increases to 30 feet at 12th level and 60 feet at 20th level. Black, Green, and Copper dragon shamans deal Acid damage. Blue and Bronze dragon shamans deal Lightning damage. White and Silver dragon shamans deal Cold damage. Red, Brass, and Gold dragon shamans deal Fire damage.

Dragon Breath (30')<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Shaman level 4.<br>Specifics: At 4th level, you gain a breath weapon corresponding to your totem dragon. All breath weapons deal 2d6 points of damage, plus an extra 1d6 points of damage for every two addtional class levels (3d6 at 6th level, 4d6 at 8th level, and so forth). A successful Reflex save halves the damage dealth; the save DC is equal to 10 + 1/2 your Dragon Shaman level + your Con modifier. This ability has a 15 second cooldown before it can be used again.<br><br>The breath weapon is a cone shaped blast of energy dependent on your dragon choice (see below) that initially extends out to 15 feet. This increases to 30 feet at 12th level and 60 feet at 20th level. Black, Green, and Copper dragon shamans deal Acid damage. Blue and Bronze dragon shamans deal Lightning damage. White and Silver dragon shamans deal Cold damage. Red, Brass, and Gold dragon shamans deal Fire damage.

Dragon Breath (60')<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragon Shaman level 4.<br>Specifics: At 4th level, you gain a breath weapon corresponding to your totem dragon. All breath weapons deal 2d6 points of damage, plus an extra 1d6 points of damage for every two addtional class levels (3d6 at 6th level, 4d6 at 8th level, and so forth). This increases to 30 feet at 12th level and 60 feet at 20th level. Black, Green, and Copper dragon shamans deal Acid damage. Blue and Bronze dragon shamans deal Lightning damage. White and Silver dragon shamans deal Cold damage. Red, Brass, and Gold dragon shamans deal Fire damage.

Totem Dragon<br>
At first level, you choose which type of true dragon you venerate above all others. Your breath weapon will be based upon the dragon you select. Black, Green, and Copper dragon shamans deal Acid damage. Blue and Bronze dragon shamans deal Lightning damage. White and Silver dragon shamans deal Cold damage. Red, Brass, and Gold dragon shamans deal Fire damage.

Draconic Adaption<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 4th level, you gain immunity to paralysis, fear, and sleep effects.<br>Use: Automatic.

Natural Armor<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 7th level, your skin thickens, developing faint scales. Your natural armor bonus improves by 1. At 12th level, this improvement increases to +2, and at 17th level to +3.<br>Use: Automatic.

Touch of Vitality<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 6th level, you can heal the wounds of living creatures (your own or others) by touch. Each day you can heal a number of points of damage equal to twice your class level * your Charisma bonus. For example, a 7th-level dragon shaman with a Charisma score of 14 (+2 bonus) can heal 28 points of damage. It has no effect on undead. This ability can be used repeatedly until you run out of points (so you can heal a player for 20 points and then another for 8 points using the previous example).<br><br>Beginning at 11th level your touch of vitality can remove status effects from your target. Damage is healed first, followed by curses, then poison, and finally negative levels, blindness, deafness, and disease.<br><br>    * Curse: 20 points to remove.<br>    * Poison: 10 points to remove.<br>    * Negative Levels: 20 points to remove.<br>    * Blindness: 20 points to remove.<br>    * Deafness: 20 points to remove.<br>    * Disease: 20 points to remove.<br>

Aura (Energy Shield)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Any creature striking you or your ally is dealt 2 points of energy damage for each point of your aura bonus. The energy type is that of your totem dragon's damage dealing breath weapon.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Insight)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This aura grants you a bonus on Lore and Spellcraft checks.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Power)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Bonus on melee damage rolls equal to your aura bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Presence)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Bonus on Bluff, Diplomacy, and Intimidate checks equal to your aura bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Resistance)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Resistance to your totem dragon's energy type equal to 5 x your aura bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Resolve)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This aura grants you a bonus on Concentration checks and on saves against fear, paralysis, and sleep effects.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Senses)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Bonus on Listen and Spot checks equal to your aura bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Stamina)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This aura grants you a bonus on Fortitude saves.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Toughness)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: DR 1/Magic for each point of your aura bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Aura (Vigor)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You and your party heal 1 point for each point of your aura bonus every 2 rounds.<br>Use: Selected.

Death Touch<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Avengers and Black Flame Zealots can study their enemies and strike a deadly blow.  With a melee touch attack, the player rolls d6 per effective level (double on a critical) and slays the target if their HP is less than this value. The effective level is the total of all Avenger, Assassin, and Black Flame Zealot levels. This ability will only succeed if the target is not immune to sneak attacks and a sneak attack would be valid when the touch is made. This ability takes two rounds to cast.<br>Use: Automatic.

Touch of the Wild<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Champion of the Wild 1.<br>Specifics: With this feat, characters can heal damage equal to their class level multiplied by their Charisma modifier (stacks with all classes that provide Lay on Hands). This ability can only heal a target.<br>Use: Selected.

Daring Outlaw<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Grace +1, Sneak Attack +2d6.<br>Benefit: Your rogue, ninja, and swashbuckler levels also stack for the purpose of determining your sneak attack bonus damage, grace, and swashbuckler dodge. For example, a 7th-level rogue/4th-level swashbuckler would deal an extra 6d6 points of damage with her sneak attacks, as if she were an 11th-level rogue.<br><br><i>You combine grace and stealth to deadly effect.<i>

Lyric Thaumaturge Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Lyric Thaumaturge level 1.<br>Specifics: At every level you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in the bard class.<br>Use: Automatic.

Melodic Casting<br>
Type: General<br>Prerequisite: Perform 4, Spellcraft 4, bardic music 1/day<br>Benefit: You gain a bonus to Concentration checks (equal to the difference) if your Perform is greater than your Concentration skill. Only the base ranks in the skill are compared.<br><i>You can weave your music and magic together into a single perfect voice.</i><br>Use: Automatic.

Sonic Might<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Beginning at 5th level, you can expend a use of bardic music to add 1d6 points of sonic damage per spell level to any spell with the sonic descriptor for a number of rounds equal to your lyric thaumaturge level.<br>Use: Selected.<br>Affected Spells: Cacophonic Burst, Greater Shout, Lesser Orb of Sound, Orb of Sound, Resonating Bolt, Shout, Sound Burst<br>Affected Feats: Clap of Thunder

Mystic Knight Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Mystic Knight level 1.<br>Specifics: At every level you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane, divine, or invocation spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level except for the Sorcerer and Wizard. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a Mystic Knight, you must decide which class gains the increased casting ability. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Shadowbane Stalker Spellcasting Class (Cleric)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Shadowbane Stalker level 1.<br>Specifics: A shadowbane stalker continues training in divine spellcasting as well as learning. At each level gained in the shadowbane stalker class except for 4th and 9th, she gains new spells per day (and spells known) as if she had gained a level in a divine spellcasting class she belonged to before adding the prestige class level. If she had more than one divine spellcasting class before becoming a shadowbane stalker, she must decide which class to progress in.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sacred Stealth<br>
A shadowbane stalker can channel some of her divine power to become stealthier. She expends one use of Turn Undead and gains a +4 sacred bonus to Hide and Move Silently checks for a number of minutes equal to her Charisma bonus. At 7th level, the bonus increases to +8.

Discover Subterfuge<br>
A shadowbane stalker trains diligently to detect subterfuge and the misdirection of others. At 2nd level and higher, she gains a +2 competence bonus on Search checks. This bonus increases to +4 at 5th level and +6 at 8th level.

Practiced Spellcaster (Assassin)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 4 ranks.<br>Specifics: Your caster level for the chosen spellcasting class increases by +4. This can't increase your caster level beyond your HD. However, even if you can't benefit from the full bonus immediately, if you later gain noncaster-level HD you may be able to apply the rest of the bonus. For example, a human 5th-level cleric/3rd-level fighter who selects this feat would increase his cleric caster level from 5th to 8th (since he has 8 HD). If he later gained a fighter level, he would gain the remainder of the bonus and his cleric caster level would become 9th (since he now has 9 HD). A character with two or more spellcasting classes (such as a bard/sorcerer or a ranger/druid) must choose which class gains the feat's effect. This does not affect your spells per day or spells known. It only increases your caster level, which would help you penetrate SR and increase the duration and other effects of your spells. You may select this feat multiple times. Each time you choose it, you must apply it to a different spellcasting class. For instance, a 4th-level cleric/5th-level wizard who had selected this feat twice would cast cleric spells as an 8th-level caster and wizard spells as a 9th-level caster.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>

Practiced Spellcaster (Blackguard)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 4 ranks.<br>Specifics: Your caster level for the chosen spellcasting class increases by +4. This can't increase your caster level beyond your HD. However, even if you can't benefit from the full bonus immediately, if you later gain noncaster-level HD you may be able to apply the rest of the bonus. For example, a human 5th-level cleric/3rd-level fighter who selects this feat would increase his cleric caster level from 5th to 8th (since he has 8 HD). This does not affect your spells per day or spells known. It only increases your caster level, which would help you penetrate SR and increase the duration and other effects of your spells. You may select this feat multiple times. Each time you choose it, you must apply it to a different spellcasting class. For instance, a 4th-level cleric/5th-level wizard who had selected this feat twice would cast cleric spells as an 8th-level caster and wizard spells as a 9th-level caster.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>

Divine Champion Spellcasting<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Divine Champion level 1.<br>Specifics: At every odd level you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known) as if you had also gained a level in the Paladin and Ranger spellcasting classes.<br>Use: Automatic.

Intuitive Attack<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: Base attack bonus +1.<br>Benefit: With a simple or natural weapon, you may use your Wisdom modifier instead of your Strength modifier on attack rolls. This bonus is applied when you equip/unequip a weapon or change shapes. This includes unarmed fighting.<br>Special: A fighter may select Intuitive Attack as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br>Use: Automatic. (This bonus is calculated and applied when you equip/unequip items)<br>If you have Weapon Finesse you will get the difference between your Wisdom and the higher of (Str or Dex). A 14 Str, 20 Dex, 30 Wis character with weapon finesse will get a +5 (30 Wis - 20 Dex) AB bonus. Without weapon finesse the bonus for the same character would be +8 (30 Wis - 14 Str). This ability does not work with Zen Archery.<br>Valid Weapons: Club, Dagger, Dart, Light Mace, Sickle, Spear, Morning Star, Light Crossbow, Heavy Crossbow, Quarterstaff, Sling, Silver Sword of Gith, and Unarmed attacks<br><i>You fight by faith more than brute strength.</i>

Damage Bonus (vs Dragons)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>A dragonslayer gains a bonus on weapon damage rolls against dragons equal to his class level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Energy Resistance<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 5th level, a dragonslayer gains resistance to acid 5, cold 5, electricity 5, fire 5, and sonic 5. These resistances improve to 10 at 10th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Damage Reduction<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 3rd level, a dragonslayer gains damage reduction 1/-. This improves to damage reduction 2/- at 6th level, and to damage reduction 3/- at 9th level. This damage reduction only stacks with the DR granted by Greater Resiliency and the epic damage reduction feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

True Strike 1/day<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 2nd level, a dragonslayer may use true strike once per day. At 10th level, a dragonslayer may use true strike twice per day.<br>Use: Automatic.

True Strike 2/day<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>At 2nd level, a dragonslayer may use true strike once per day. At 10th level, a dragonslayer may use true strike twice per day.<br>Use: Automatic.

Dragonslayer Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dragonslayer level 1.<br>Specifics: At each odd level (1, 3, 5, 7, 9), you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane, divine, or invocation based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a dragonslayer, you must decide which class to progress in. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Gift (Damage Reduction)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This ability is granted at level 1. You gain temporary damage reduction (overcome by cold iron) equal to 1/2 your class level (minimum 1). This damage reduction stacks with the damage reduction granted by the warlock class (along with the epic damage reduction feats, the greater resilience feat, and the fey skin damage reduction). It does not stack with any other source of damage reduction. This effect lasts for a number of rounds equal to 3 + your Cha modifier (minimum 1 round).<br>Use: Automatic.

Gift (Healing Blast)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This ability is granted at level 7. You gain the ability to change your eldritch blast in a wave of healing energy (20' radius, centered on you). The blast heals living creatures instead of dealing damage to them (the damage healed is equal to the damage normally dealt).<br>Use: Automatic.

Gift (Fiendish Resistance)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This ability is granted at level 4. You gain resistance to acid and fire equal to 10 + your class level. This resistance stacks with the energy resistance granted by the warlock class. This effect lasts for a number of rounds equal to 3 + your Cha modifier (minimum 1 round).<br>Use: Automatic.

Gift (Wild Frenzy)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level you gain the ability to enter a wild frenzy. You gain a +2 bonus on attack rolls with weapons and eldritch blasts and a +2 bonus on damage rolls for weapons, along with temporary hit points equal to twice your class level. This effect lasts for a number of rounds equal to 3 + your Cha modifier (minimum 1 round).<br>Use: Automatic.

Eldritch Disciple Spellcasting Class (Cleric)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Eldritch Disciple level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in a divine or invocation based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a eldritch disciple, you must decide which class to progress in. Eldritch blast dice are advanced as though taking warlock levels regardless of which spellcasting progression is chosen. Warlocks who choose divine progression do not gain any bonuses on beating spell resistance with their blasts (only blast dice).<br>Use: Automatic.

Elemental Affinity (Air)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An elemental warrior is attuned to one of the Elemental Planes, which imbues her with certain related abilities. At 1st level, she selects one of the four elements (air, earth, fire, or water) as the one for which she has an affinity. She gains resistance 10 to a particular energy type based on her chosen element (electricity for air, acid for earth, fire for fire, and cold for water). This resistance becomes 20 points at level 5.<br>Use: Automatic.

Elemental Affinity (Earth)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An elemental warrior is attuned to one of the Elemental Planes, which imbues her with certain related abilities. At 1st level, she selects one of the four elements (air, earth, fire, or water) as the one for which she has an affinity. She gains resistance 10 to a particular energy type based on her chosen element (electricity for air, acid for earth, fire for fire, and cold for water). Elemental Affinity (Water)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An elemental warrior is attuned to one of the Elemental Planes, which imbues her with certain related abilities. At 1st level, she selects one of the four elements (air, earth, fire, or water) as the one for which she has an affinity. She gains resistance 10 to a particular energy type based on her chosen element (electricity for air, acid for earth, fire for fire, and cold for water). This resistance becomes 20 points at level 5.<br>Use: Automatic.

Elemental Manifestation<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An elemental warrior of 2nd level or higher can cause her body to manifest aspects of her chosen element. The effect lasts for 1 minute and may be used a number of times per day equal to her class level.<br><br>Air: All ranged attacks against her have a 20% miss chance.<br>Earth: The elemental warrior's skin is partially sheathed in rocky hide. Her natural armor bonus increased by 3 (this is an enhancement bonus).<br>Fire: A sheath of flame envelops the elemental warrior. Any opponent who lands a successful melee attack against her takes 1d6 points of fire damage.<br>Water: The elemental warrior becomes coated in water. She gains damage reduction 3/piercing.<br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Weapon<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level and higher, an elemental warrior can imbue her melee weapons with the energy of her chosen element. The imbued weapons deals an extra 2d6 points of energy damage on each hit. The effect lasts for 1 minute and can be used a number of times per day equal to her class level.<br><br>When an elemental warrior reaches 5th level, this ability improves. The extra damage remains the same, but on a successful critical hit, the weapons release a burst of energy that deals an additional 4d6 damage (Massive Criticals: 4d6).<br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Sanctuary<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 4th level, elemental warriors of all kinds also gain the ability to plane shift once per day for 1 minute (equivalent to the ethereal jaunt spell).<br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Strike<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Once per day, a 5th-level elemental warrior can channel her chosen element to deal a devastating melee touch attack. No save is allowed against the damage dealt by the attack, but a successful Fortitude save negates the secondary effect. The save DC for these effects is 15 + the elemental warrior's Con modifier.<br><br>Air, Earth: Target takes 10d6 points of blunt damage from the crushing touch attack and is knocked down (Fort save negates knockdown).<br>Fire: Target takes 10d6 points of fire damage and catches fire for 1d4 rounds (Fort save negates catching on fire). The target takes 1d6 fire damage for each round they are on fire unless they make a Reflex save, in which case the flames end.<br>Water: Target takes 10d6 points of magic damage as moisture is drawn from its body and becomes nauseated for 1 round (Fort save negates nausea).<br>Use: Selected.

Extra Elemental Strike<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Level 5 Elemental Warrior.<br>Specifics: Three times per day, a 5th-level elemental warrior can channel her chosen element to deal a devastating melee touch attack. No save is allowed against the damage dealt by the attack, but a successful Fortitude save negates the secondary effect. The save DC for these effects is 15 + the elemental warrior's Con modifier.<br><br>Air, Earth: Target takes 10d6 points of blunt damage from the crushing touch attack and is knocked down (Fort save negates knockdown).<br>Fire: Target takes 10d6 points of fire damage and catches fire for 1d4 rounds (Fort save negates catching on fire). The target takes 1d6 fire damage for each round they are on fire unless they make a Reflex save, in which case the flames end.<br>Water: Target takes 10d6 points of magic damage as moisture is drawn from its body and becomes nauseated for 1 round (Fort save negates nausea).<br>Use: Selected.

Critical Sense<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 4th level the Whirling Dervish improves her understanding of how best to attack to her foe. She gains a +1 bonus to attack rolls which improves to +2 at 10th.<br>Use: Automatic.

Expert Tumbling<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 5th level the Whirling Dervish gains the ability to use her tumbling skills to better avoid being hit in combat. She gains a +1 Dodge AC bonus.<br>Use: Automatic.

Minor Teleportation<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At tenth level, the Whirling Dervish gains the supernatural ability to dissapear from sight and travel a short distance without the enemy finding her (equivalent to the ethereal jaunt spell that lasts 1 minute). The Whirling Dervish may do this three times per day.<br>Use: Selected.

Elemental Shape (Ember Guard)<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>Prerequisite: Elemental Shape, Druid 25<br>Specifics: You gain the ability to shift into a more powerful elemental form, the Ember Guard.<br>Use: Selected.<br><br>Ember Guards have the following statistics: Str 30, Dex 15, Con 26, AC 35 (10 Base, 22 Natural AC, -1 Size, +4 Dex), Fast Healing 5, DR 15/-, Immune to Fire, Immune to Poison, Resist Acid 10, Resist Cold 10, Vulnerability to Cold, Immunity to Disease, Immunity to Critical Hits, Immunity to Paralysis, Immunity to Sneak Attacks, Spell Resistance (24), and Darkvision. It deals d8 blunt plus d6 fire damage in melee and has a 1d6 fire damage shield.

Augment Elemental<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Your knowledge of elemental magic allows you to imbue your summoned elementals with extraordinary combat prowess and durability.<br>Prerequisite: Lore 2<br>Benefit: Elementals you conjure gain a +2 enhancement bonus for their attacks and temporary hit points equal to twice its Hit Dice.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><br>Affected spells: Elemental Swarm, Greater Planar Binding, Lesser Planar Binding, Summon Creature VII, Summon Creature VIII, and Summon Creature IX

Ashbound<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You have been trained in a druidic tradition focusing on becoming one of nature's avengers. You consider the use of arcane magic to be a vile and unnatural act.<br>Prerequisite: Animal Companion or Telthor Companion<br>Benefit: The duration of your summon creature spells is doubled. Creatures summoned by those spells receive a +3 bonus on their attack rolls.<br>Use: Automatic.<br><br>Affected spells: Elemental Swarm, Greater Planar Binding, Lesser Planar Binding, Planar Binding, and Summon Creature I-IX

Epic of the Lost King<br>
Type of Feat: Bardic Music<br>You can channel the power of your bardic music to reinvigorate your allies.<br>Prerequisite: Bardic Music, Perform 6.<br>Benefit: As an instant action, you can expend one daily use of your bardic music ability to remove fatigue or exhaustion from yourself and allies within 30 feet.<br>This removes the negative effects of frenzy, inspire frenzy, hypothermia, rage, and touch of fatigue.<br>Use: Selected.

Shared Fury<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Your fearsome rage spurs your animal companion to greater heights.<br>Prerequisite: Survival 4, Animal Companion, Rage or Frenzy<br>Benefit: When you rage or frenzy, your animal companion gains the same benefits and penalties from your rage/frenzy that you do.<br>Use: Automatic.

Weapon Supremacy<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You are a grandmaster in the use of weaponry and no foe can stand against you.<br>Prerequisite: Fighter level 18, any Melee Weapon Mastery feat<br>Benefit: You gain a +1 AB bonus and a +1 Dodge AC bonus.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Weapon Supremacy as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Melee Weapon Mastery (Bludgeoning)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have mastered a wide range of weapons.<br>Prerequisite: Fighter level 12.<br>Benefit: When using any melee weapon that has the damage type you selected, you gain a +2 bonus on attack rolls and a +2 bonus on damage rolls. Weapons that deal more than one type of damage will NOT gain any benefit from having a melee weapon mastery in each damage type.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Melee Weapon Mastery as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Melee Weapon Mastery (Piercing)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have mastered a wide range of weapons.<br>Prerequisite: Fighter level 12.<br>Benefit: When using any melee weapon that has the damage type you selected, you gain a +2 bonus on attack rolls and a +2 bonus on damage rolls. Weapons that deal more than one type of damage will NOT gain any benefit from having a melee weapon mastery in each damage type.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Melee Weapon Mastery as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Melee Weapon Mastery (Slashing)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have mastered a wide range of weapons.<br>Prerequisite: Fighter level 12.<br>Benefit: When using any melee weapon that has the damage type you selected, you gain a +2 bonus on attack rolls and a +2 bonus on damage rolls. Weapons that deal more than one type of damage will NOT gain any benefit from having a melee weapon mastery in each damage type.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Melee Weapon Mastery as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Grim Survival<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>By following an ancient druidic ritual, your ability to track while in wildshape is heightened.<br>Prerequisite: Base Fortitude +4, Wild Shape.<br>Benefit: You gain a Survival bonus while wildshaped equal to half your level, +10 max.<br>Use: Automatic.

Unstoppable Ferocity<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>By following an ancient druidic ritual, your companion's life force is suffused with healing power.<br>Prerequisite: Base Fortitude +4, Animal Companion<br>Benefit: Your companion gains 1 point of regeneration for every 3 HD it posseses, rounded up (1 point minimum). This ability has a maximum regen bonus of +5<br>Use: Automatic.

Dragonsong<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Your song or poetics echo the power of the dragonsong, an ancient style of vocal performance created by dragons in the distant past.<br>Prerequisite: Cha 13, Lore 4, Perform 6, Bard Songs<br>Benefit: You gain a +2 bonus with the Perform skill as well as +2 to the DC of any bardic music effects.<br>Affected songs: Cloud Mind, Fascinate, Haven Song, and Inspire Slowing.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ability Focus (Bard Songs)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Your chosen ability becomes more potent than normal.<br>Prerequisite: Bard Songs<br>Benefit: Add +2 to the DC for all saving throws against the chosen ability.<br>Affected songs: Cloud Mind, Fascinate, Haven Song, and Inspire Slowing.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ability Focus (Eldritch Blast)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Your chosen ability becomes more potent than normal.<br>Prerequisite: Warlock level 1<br>Benefit: Add +2 to the DC for all saving throws against the chosen ability.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ability Focus (Invocations)<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Your chosen ability becomes more potent than normal.<br>Prerequisite: Warlock level 1<br>Benefit: Add +2 to the DC for all saving throws against the chosen ability. This does not affect eldritch blast.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sanctify Strikes<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Sacred power suffuses your attacks.<br>Prerequisite: Paladin level 8, Cha 15<br>Benefit: You deal 2 extra points of divine damage with your attacks.<br>Use: Automatic.

Heavy Armor Optimization<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have trained extensively in heavy armor, and you have learned to take advantage of the protection it offers.<br>Prerequisite: Armor Proficiency (Heavy), Base Attack Bonus +4<br>Benefit: When you are wearing heavy armor, lessen the armor check penalty by 1 and increase the armor bonus by 1. This is done as a +skill bonus to (Hide, Move Silently, Parry, Set Trap, Sleight of Hand, Tumble) and a +1 Dodge AC bonus.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Heavy Armor Optimization as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Greater Heavy Armor Optimization<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>You have mastered the use of heavy armor, maximizing its protective qualities while moving more easily in it.<br>Prerequisite: Heavy Armor Optimization, Base Attack Bonus +8<br>Benefit: When you are wearing heavy armor, lessen the armor check penalty by 2 and increase the armor bonus by 2. This is done as a +skill bonus to (Hide, Move Silently, Parry, Set Trap, Sleight of Hand, Tumble) and a +2 Dodge AC bonus. This bonus replaces the one from Heavy Armor Optimization.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Greater Heavy Armor Optimization as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Practical Metamagic (Extend)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You can apply a selected metamagic feat to your spells more easily.<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 8, Extend Spell, Level 3 spells.<br>Benefit: When applying the chosen metamagic feat to a spell, the spell uses a spell slot one level lower than normal for the applied metamagic feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

Practical Metamagic (Silent)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You can apply a selected metamagic feat to your spells more easily.<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 8, Silent Spell, Level 3 spells.<br>Benefit: When applying the chosen metamagic feat to a spell, the spell uses a spell slot one level lower than normal for the applied metamagic feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

Practical Metamagic (Still)<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>You can apply a selected metamagic feat to your spells more easily.<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 8, Still Spell, Level 3 spells.<br>Benefit: When applying the chosen metamagic feat to a spell, the spell uses a spell slot one level lower than normal for the applied metamagic feat.<br>Use: Automatic.

<color=Gold><b>Aasimar</b></color><br><br>Carrying the blood of a celestial, an aasimar typically fights against evil in the world. Some have a minor physical trait suggesting their heritage such as silver hair, golden eyes, or an unnaturally intense stare. Those descended from a celestial minion often carry a birthmark in the shape of the deity's holy symbol or some other mark significant to that faith. <br><br><color=Gold><b>Racial Traits:</b></color><br><br>- <b>Ability Adjustments:</b> +2 Wisdom, +2 Charisma. <br><br>- <b>Darkvision:</b> Aasimar can see in the dark up to 60 feet. <br><br>- <b>Celestial Resistance:</b> Acid, cold, and electricity resistance 5.<br><br>- <b>Spell-like abilities:</b><br><br>     1/day - <i>Light</i> as sorcerer of equal level.<br><br>- <b>Keen Senses:</b> +2 racial bonus to Listen and Spot checks.<br><br>- <b>Favored Class:</b> Paladin.  A multiclass aasimar's paladin class does not count when determining whether she takes an experience point penalty for multiclassing.<br>
<color=Gold><b>Tiefling</b></color><br><br>Because they are descended from evil outsiders, those who know their ancestry immediately consider most tieflings evil and untrustworthy. Not all tieflings are evil or untrustworthy, but enough are that the prejudice tends to cling. Some tieflings have a minor physical trait suggesting their heritage, such as pointed teeth, red eyes, small horns, the odor of brimstone, cloven feet, or just an unnatural aura of wrongness. Those descended from an infernal minion often carry a birthmark of the deity's holy symbol or another trait related to that evil faith.<br><br><color=Gold><b>Racial Traits:</b></color><br><br>- <b>Ability Adjustments:</b> +2 Dexterity, +2 Intelligence, -2 Charisma. <br><br>- <b>Darkvision:</b> Tieflings can see in the dark up to 60 feet. <br><br>- <b>Infernal Resistance:</b> Cold, fire, and electricity resistance 5.<br><br>- <b>Spell-like abilities:</b><br><br>     1/day - <i>Darkness</i> as a sorcerer of equal level.<br><br>- <b>Skill Affinities:</b> +2 racial bonus to Bluff and Hide checks.<br><br>- <b>Favored class:</b> Rogue. A multiclass tiefling's rogue class does not count when determining whether she takes an experience point penalty for multiclassing.<br>

AC Bonus<br>
AC Bonus: While unarmored, you gain a bonus to your dodge Armor Class equal to your Constitution bonus (if any). Only your base Constitution is used for this bonus (spell/item enhancements are not used). This bonus will not work while wildshaped.

Unarmed Damage Increase<br>
Your unarmed attacks deal more damage than usual. You deal +1d6 blunt damage when not wielding any weapons.

Feral Trance 1/day<br>
Once per day, you can enter a feral battle trance. While you are in this state, the raw power of the beast flows through you. For the duration of the trance, you gain a +4 bonus to Dexterity and a +2 bonus on attack rolls. A feral trance lasts for a number of rounds equal to 3 + your Con modifier. At 3rd level, you can use this ability twice per day. The Extend Rage feat increases the duration by 5 rounds. This is an instantly activated feat.

Feral Trance 2/day<br>
Once per day, you can enter a feral battle trance. While you are in this state, the raw power of the beast flows through you. For the duration of the trance, you gain a +4 bonus to Dexterity and a +2 bonus on attack rolls. A feral trance lasts for a number of rounds equal to 3 + your Con modifier. At 3rd level, you can use this ability twice per day. The Extend Rage feat increases the duration by 5 rounds. This is an instantly activated feat.

Stormsinger Spellcasting Class (Warlock)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Stormsinger level 1.<br>Specifics: On every level, the Stormsinger progresses in one selected arcane, invocation, or divine spellcasting class. If a character had more than one spellcasting class in which she could cast spells before she became a Stormsinger, the player must decide which class to assign each level of Stormsinger for the purpose of determining spells per day and spells known. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Champion of the Silver Flame Spellcasting Class (Paladin)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Champion of the Silver Flame level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, a champion of the Silver Flame gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Paladin level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Blessing of the Champion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A champion of the Silver Flame dispenses the fury of the Silver Flame with her weapons. The champion gains a +1 bonus on Attack rolls and a +1 divine damage bonus.<br>Use: Automatic.

Resist Compulsion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Beginning at 3rd level, a champion of the Silver Flame receives a +2 sacred bonus on saving throws against all mind-affecting spells and effects.<br>Use: Automatic.

Weapons of Flame<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 6th level, a champion of the Silver Flame deals an extra 1d6 points of fire damage on a successful hit.<br>Use: Automatic.

Weapons of Sacred Flame<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 9th level, a champion of the Silver Flame deals an extra 1d6 points of sacred (divine) damage on a successful hit in addition to the bonus granted by weapons of flame.<br>Use: Automatic.

Warding Flame<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level, a champion of the Silver Flame can bathe herself in silver flame. As long as the warding flame covers her, she gains a +2 sacred bonus to dodge Armor Class and saving throws. In addition, she gains spell resistance 12 + character level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Snowflake Wardance<br>
Type of Feat: Bardic Music<br>You have mastered the snowflake wardance, a mystical style of fighting with slashing weapons that allows you to leap and almost seem to float haphazardly across the battlefield like a whirling, razor-edged snowflake.<br>Prerequisite: Bardic Music, Perform 6.<br>Benefit: By expending one of your daily uses of bardic music, you may perform a deadly style of combat known as the snowflake wardance. Once activated, you add your Charisma modifier to your attack rolls with any slashing melee weapon you are wielding in one hand. This bonus to hit stacks with any bonuses you get from a high Strength score (or Dexterity score, if you are using Weapon Finesse).<br>You cannot use this feat if you are carrying a shield, wearing medium or heavy armor, or carrying a medium or heavy load. If dual wielding, both weapons must be slashing. This does not work with two-handed weapons.<br>A snowflake wardance lasts for a number of rounds equal to 1 + your ranks in Perform. Performing a snowflake wardance is physically tiresome - when the dance ends, you become fatigued for the next 10 minutes. Fatigue causes a -2 penalty to Strength and Dexterity as well as a 10% movement speed reduction. It can only be removed by resting and the bardic song <i>Epic of the Lost King</i><br>Use: Selected.

Protective Ward<br>
Type of Feat: Wild<br>Your fervor in protecting the wild has afforded you divine protection from danger which shields you from attacks.<br>Prerequisite: Ranger 4, Ability to cast level 1 Ranger spells.<br>Benefit: You gain a +1 Dodge AC bonus. This bonus increases to +2 at 24th level as a Ranger. <br>If you do not have levels in any other class except for Ranger, you instead gain the following benefits: At level 4 you gain a +1 Dodge AC bonus and a +1 Attack bonus. This bonus becomes +2 Dodge AC and a +1 Attack bonus at 8th level. This bonus becomes +3 Dodge AC and +1 Attack bonus at 11th level. This bonus becomes +4 Dodge AC and a +1 Attack bonus at 14th level. This bonus finally becomes +4 Dodge AC and +2 Attack bonus at 24th level.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>Note: If you have levels in Knight of the Wild, then Paladin levels and Knight of the Wild levels count as Ranger. If you have the feat Wildfire Blades, then Invisible Blade levels count as Ranger.

Sword Dancer Spellcasting Class (Cleric)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Sword Dancer level 1.<br>Specifics: Spellcasting Progression: At each sword dancer level, the character gains new divine spells per day as if she had also gained a level in a divine spellcasting class to which she belonged before adding the prestige class level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sword Dancer Spellcasting Class (Druid)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Sword Dancer level 1.<br>Specifics: Spellcasting Progression: At each sword dancer level, the character gains new divine spells per day as if she had also gained a level in a divine spellcasting class to which she belonged before adding the prestige class level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sword Dancer Spellcasting Class (Paladin)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Sword Dancer level 1.<br>Specifics: Spellcasting Progression: At each sword dancer level, the character gains new divine spells per day as if she had also gained a level in a divine spellcasting class to which she belonged before adding the prestige class level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sword Dancer Spellcasting Class (Favored Soul)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Sword Dancer level 1.<br>Specifics: Spellcasting Progression: At each sword dancer level, the character gains new divine spells per day as if she had also gained a level in a divine spellcasting class to which she belonged before adding the prestige class level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Sword Song<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Once a day, a sword dancer wielding a sword in her right hand causes the sword to sing heroic songs, granting her a +2 bonus on attack and damage rolls and a +3 morale bonus on saving throws against mind-affecting spells and effects. This ability is instantly activated an lasts a number of rounds equal to her class level plus her Charisma modifier.<br>Use: Selected.

Sword Shield<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level, as an instant action, a sword dancer may command her dancing sword to fight in a defensive manner, taking a penalty of -5 on the sword's attacks in order to add the same number to her AC as a shield AC bonus.<br>Use: Selected.

Singing Sword<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 11th level, the sword wielded in the right hand of the sword dancer is infused with the divine power of Eilistraee. It gains the ability to use bardic music to inspire courage as a bard of equal level to the character level of the sword dancer who infused it; neither the sword nor the sword dancer need have any ranks in the Perform skill to use this ability. This ability lasts for 20 rounds and is usable once per day at 11th level, twice a day at 15th level, and three times a day at 19th level. The sword also gains the Thundering ability, gaining a +1d6 sonic damage bonus at all times.<br>Use: Selected.

Silverhair Armor<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 13th level the sword dancer's hair becomes infused with divine energy and glows softly with silver light. As long as she is not wearing armor or using a shield, she gains a +2 dodge AC bonus. This bonus increases to +4 at 17th level and to +6 at 21st level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Dissonant Chord Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Dissonant Chord level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, a dissonant chord gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Bard level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Improved Counterspell<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: The dissonant chord's ability to counterspell has improved.  Instead of protecting against a single spell, it now lasts a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier, the duration is extended from 10 rounds to 10 rounds plus your Charisma modifier (along with any other song duration modifiers), and the spell resistance granted gains a bonus equal to half your Charisma modifier.<br>Use: Automatic.

Break Concentration<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, a dissonant chord becomes expert at distracting spellcasters, her discordant ways interfering with spellcasting. Any enemies within 30' of the dissonant chord suffer a penalty to concentration equal to the dissonant chord's class level plus Charisma modifier.<br>Use: Automatic.

Anti-Magic Melody<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 5th level, a dissonant chord can use her Perform skill to negate magic. This works as Mordenkainen's Disjunctions with a cap equal to her bard level (no prestige classes count and this has a +25 max cap). This potent ability is quite exhausting to use as each time it is activated it uses three of her bardic music uses for the day.<br>Use: Selected.

Empower Reserve Feats<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Minimum Spell Level 2, Empower Metamagic.<br>Specifics: Reserve feats that deal damage are now empowered and deal 50% more damage. This feat will work together with Maximize Reserve Feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Maximize Reserve Feats<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Minimum Spell Level 3, Maximized Metamagic.<br>Specifics: Reserve feats that deal damage are now maximized and deal maximum damage. This feat will work together with Empower Reserve Feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Child of Night Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level except 2nd, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in a casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a child of night, you must decide which class to progress in. This is a 9/10 progression class in either Divine, Arcane, or Invocation magic. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Cloak of Shadows<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Your flesh takes on a hazy, almost translucent hue, granting a bonus equal to your child of night level on Hide checks. At 6th level you become immune to poisons and diseases. At 8th level, your form permanently becomes hazy and you continually have a blur effect (20% concealment).<br>Use: Automatic.

Cold Resistance<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 1st level, the shaow in your essence provides cold resistance 5. This resistance increases to 10 at 5th level and 15 at 9th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Dancing Shadows 1/day<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, you can grant yourself 50% concealment for one round per class level. At 7th level and above, you can use dancing shadows twice per day. At 10th level, you can use dancing shadows three times per day. This is an instant feat. <br>Use: Selected.

Dancing Shadows 2/day<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, you can grant yourself 50% concealment for one round per class level. At 7th level and above, you can use dancing shadows twice per day. At 10th level, you can use dancing shadows three times per day. This is an instant feat. <br>Use: Selected.

Dancing Shadows 3/day<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, you can grant yourself 50% concealment for one round per class level. At 7th level and above, you can use dancing shadows twice per day. At 10th level, you can use dancing shadows three times per day. This is an instant feat. <br>Use: Selected.

Night Form<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: For 1 minute per day, you can take the form of a Nightwalker while retaining your ability to cast spells. This is an instant feat.<br>Use: Selected.

Resistance to Energy<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A dragon warrior gains resistance 5 against attacks involving the same energy types as the character's breath weapon (for example, acid for a green dragon warrior). At 5th level, the resistance improves to 10. At 7th level it improves to 15, and at 10th level it becomes 20.<br>Use: Automatic.

Immune to Fear<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 3rd level, a dragon warrior is immune to fear effects due their interaction with the great wyrms.<br>Use: Automatic.

Elemental weapon<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 4th level, a dragon warrior can endow her weapons with elemental energy while she wields them in battle. This energy is of the same type as her breath weapon. At 4th level, this bonus deals an extra 1d6 points of energy damage, and this extra damage increases to 2d6 at 9th level. This ability lasts for one hour per class level at a time (no limit on uses).<br>Use: Selected.

Dragon Breath<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A dragon warrior can use a breath weapon once per day as a standard action. The type of breath weapon depends on the chosen dragon type. The breath weapon deals 1d8 points of damage per class level in a 30' cone. The Reflex save DC for the breath weapon is 10 + the dragon warrior's class level + the dragon warrior's Con modifier. This can be used once per day at 1st level, twice a day at 2nd level, and three times per day at 6th level.<br>Use: Selected.

Divine Spirit<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Benefit: Upon reaching 5th level, you can call upon your deity for aid in the form of a celestial spirit. Using this ability is a standard action that does not provoke attacks of opportunity. See below for detailed spirit descriptions and rules.<br><br>Your paladin level determines which kinds of celestial spirits answer your summons. You can summon the spirit available at your current level as well as any that became available at lower levels.<br><br>Paladin Level<br>5th: Spirit of Healing<br>11th: Spirit of Combat 1/day<br>16th: Spirit of Heroism 1/day<br>20th: Spirit of the Fallen 1/day<br>25th: Spirit of Heroism 2/day<br>30th: Spirit of the Fallen 2/day<br><br>Each spirit available to you can be summoned once per day. A spirit remains for a number of rounds equal to your paladin level (except for Spirit of Healing which provides a passive benefit).<br><br>Spirit of Healing: This spirit increases your ability heal damage to you or your allies. This grants the Paladin the equivalent of two additional uses of Lay on Hands (healing version).<br><br>Spirit of Combat: This spirit enhances combat ability. You and your allies within 10' when the spirit is summoned gain holy fervor. Holy fervor grants a +1 sacred bonus on attack and damage rolls for every four paladin levels you possess (up to a maximum of +7 at 28th level). This is an instantly activated ability. <br><br>Spirit of Heroism: You gain DR 10/-. In addition, you gain bonus hit points equal to your character level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br><br>Spirit of the Fallen: You summon a celestial spirit to watch over you and your allies within 30 feet. You and your allies present when the spirit is summoned gain fast healing 10 and a ward against death. If those warded against death drop to 0 or fewer hps the spirit revives that character. The character revived heals an amount equal to twice your paladin level. This is an instantly activated ability.

Spirit of Healing<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This spirit increases your ability heal damage to you or your allies. This grants the Paladin the equivalent of two additional uses of Lay on Hands (healing version).<br>Use: Selected.

Spirit of Combat<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: This spirit enhances combat ability. You and your allies within 10' when the spirit is summoned gain holy fervor. Holy fervor grants a +1 sacred bonus on attack and damage rolls for every four paladin levels you possess (up to a maximum of +7 at 28th level). This is an instantly activated ability. <br>Use: Selected.

Spirit of Heroism<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You gain DR 10/-. In addition, you gain bonus hit points equal to your character level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Spirit of the Fallen<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You summon a celestial spirit to watch over you and your allies within 30 feet. You and your allies present when the spirit is summoned gain fast healing 10 and a ward against death. If those warded against death drop to 0 or fewer hps the spirit revives that character. The character revived heals an amount equal to twice your paladin level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

AC Bonus<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 1st level, a dervish gains a +1 dodge bonus to AC. This bonus improves to +2 at 5th level and +3 at 9th level.<br><br>A dervish gains this bonus to Armor Class as long as she is wearing no armor or light armor and not carrying a shield. She loses this bonus when she is immobilized or helpless, when she wears any armor heavier than light, when she carries a shield, or when she carries a medium or heavy load.<br>Use: Automatic.

Dervish Dance<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A dervish can become a whirling dancer of death a certain number of times per day. She gains a bonus on her attack and damage rolls; +1 at 1st level, and it increases by an extra +1 at every odd-numbered level thereafter.<br><br>A dervish may only perform a dervish dance while wielding a slashing weapon (she may use multiple weapons only if both weapons are of the slashing type). She cannot perform a dervish dance in any armor heavier than light or if she is using a shield.<br><br>A dervish dance lasts 1 round for every 2 ranks of Perform that the character has. At the end of a dervish dance, the character becomes fatigued for 1 minute (unless she is a 9th-level dervish, at which point this limitation no longer applies).<br><br>Fatigue causes a -2 penalty to Strength and Dexterity as well as a 10% movement speed reduction.<br>Use: Selected.

Dance of Death<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 4th level, a dervish gains the benefit of the Cleave feat while performing a dervish dance, even if she does not meet the prerequisites for the feat. At 10th level this improves to Great Cleave.<br>Use: Automatic.

Defensive Parry<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When she attains 7th level, a dervish gains an extra +4 bonus to Dodge Armor Class when she chooses to fight defensively (Combat Expertise or Improved Combat Expertise is activated).<br>Use: Automatic.

Tireless Dance<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a dervish reaches 9th level, the character no longer becomes fatigued at the end of a dervish dance.<br>Use: Automatic.

A Thousand Cuts<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a dervish reaches 10th level, once per day she gains a +2d6 slashing damage bonus on her attacks for 60 seconds.<br>Use: Selected.

AC Bonus<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A ninja is highly trained at dodging blows, and she has a sixth sense that lets her avoid even unanticipated attacks. When unarmored and not using a shield, a ninja adds her Wisdom bonus (if any) to her Armor Class. This ability does not stack with the monk's AC bonus ability and the bonus can not exceed the number of levels you have in the Ninja class. In addition, a ninja gains a +1 bonus (Dodge) to AC at 5th level. This bonus increases by 1 for every five ninja levels thereafter (+2 at 10th, +3 at 15th, +4 at 20th, +5 at 25th, and +6 at 30th).<br>Use: Automatic.

Ki Power<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A ninja can channel her <i>ki</i> to manifest special powers of stealth and mobility. She can use her <i>ki</i> powers a number of times per day equal to her class level (up to 30 uses/day). <i>Ki</i> powers can be used only if a ninja is wearing no armor and is unencumbered.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ghost Step<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 2nd level, a ninja can spend one daily use of her ki power to become invisible for 3 rounds. At 10th level, a ninja can become ethereal when using ghost step instead of becoming invisible. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Acrobatics<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 6th level, a ninja gains a +2 bonus to Tumble checks. This bonus increases to +4 at 12th level, +6 at 18th level, +8 at 24th level, and +10 at 30th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ki Dodge<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 6th level and higher, a ninja can spend one daily use of her <i>ki</i> power to cause an attack against her to miss when it might otherwise hit. When a ninja activates this ability, her outline shifts and wavers, granting her concealment (20% miss chance) against all attacks for 3 rounds. This is an instantly activated ability.  Starting at 18th level, a ninja's <i>ki</i> dodge ability grants total concealment (50% miss chance).<br>Use: Selected.

Ghost Strike<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 8th level and higher, a ninja can spend one daily use of her <i>ki</i> power to strike spirits as if they were corporeal (this ignores their concealment bonus like the Spirit Shaman's Ghost Warrior ability) for three rounds. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Ghost Sight<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 16th level and higher, a ninja can see invisible and ethereal creatures as easily as she sees material creatures and objects.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ghost Walk<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A 20th-level ninja can spend two daily uses of her <i>ki</i> power to enter the Ethereal Plane for an extended period of time. This ability functions as the ethereal jaunt spell with a caster level equal to the ninja's class level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Ghost Step<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A ghost-faced killer can become invisible for 3 rounds. At 6th level, a ghost-faced killer can become ethereal when using ghost step instead of becoming invisible. This is an instantly activated ability. The ghost-faced killer can use this ability once per day at 1st level, 2/day at 4th level, 3/day at 7th level, and 4/day at 10th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Frightful Attack<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Beginning at 3rd level, a ghost-faced killer can panic onlookers and even frighten his victim to death by making a sudden strike attack. The ghost-faced killer makes a single melee attack and the victim must make a Will save (DC 10 + ghost-faced killer's class level + Cha modifier). <br><br>If the victim succeeds, she is shaken for 1 round per class level; if she fails, she dies instantly of fear. Creatures immune to mind-affecting effects, immune to fear, or with hit dice that exceed the ghost-faced killer's character level are immune to this frightful attack.<br><br>In additional, all creatures within 30 feet who see the frightful attack become shaken for 1 round per class level. A successful Will save (DC 10 + ghost-faced killer's class level + Cha modifier) negates this effect.<br>Use: Selected.

Moonfire<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics:<br>Components: V,S<br>Area: 60' Cone<br>Duration: Instantaneous (see text)<br>Saving Throw: Reflex half (see text)<br>Spell Resistance: Yes<br><br>A cone of fiery, white moonlight springs from your hand. Living creatures in the area feel an unnatural chill and take 1d8 points of divine damage per two caster levels, to a maximum of 10d8. Undead and shapechangers take double damage. This application of the spell allows a Reflex save for half damage.<br><br>Shapechanged or polymorphed creatures and objects in the spell's area at the time the spell is cast must make Will saves or immediately return to their normal forms.<br><br>Use: Selected.

Sword Dance<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, a sword dancer causes a ghostly sword (identical to the one in her right hand) to come to life and begin attacking the enemy. It has a BAB equal to the sword dancer's and cannot be killed. The sword remains for four rounds and can be used a number of times per day equal to her class level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Fearsome Reputation<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: By the time he attains 2nd level, a dread pirate has developed a reputation on the high seas and gains a +2 bonus on Intimidate checks. This bonus increases to +4 at 6th level and to +6 at 10th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Rally the Crew<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Starting at 3rd level, a dread pirate can inspire his allies (including himself) to great bravery in combat once per day. This inspiration grants them a +1 bonus on saving throws against charm and fear effects, a +1 attack bonus, and a +1 bonus on weapon damage rolls. This is an instantly activated ability and lasts for 1 minute per class level. At 7th level, a dread pirate can use this ability twice per day, and the bonus increases to +2. Rally the Crew can not be used while Inspire Courage is active.<br><br>If a dread pirate has the inspire courage bardic music ability, he adds this bonus automatically whenever he uses the inspire courage ability. For example, an 8th-level bard/3rd-level dread pirate would provide a +3 bonus on attack rolls and weapon damage rolls. <br>Use: Selected.

Fight to the Death<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Fight to the Death: At 9th level and higher, a dread pirate can inspire his allies to carry on against tremendous odds. Each ally within range gains temporary hit points equal to 10 + the dread pirate's Cha bonus (minimum 10), gains a dodge bonus to Armor Class equal to the dread pirate's Cha bonus (minimum +1), and gains a number of bonus hit points equal to their level. These bonuses last for 1 round per dread pirate level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ki Power<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A ninja can channel her <i>ki</i> to manifest special powers of stealth and mobility. She can use her <i>ki</i> powers a number of times per day equal to her class level (up to 30 uses/day). <i>Ki</i> powers can be used only if a ninja is wearing no armor and is unencumbered.<br>Use: Automatic.

Expanded Ki Pool<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Ninja 1.<br>Benefit: You gain three extra daily uses of your ki power.<br>Use: Automatic.

Spirit of Heroism<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You gain DR 10/-. In addition, you gain bonus hit points equal to your character level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Spirit of the Fallen<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You summon a celestial spirit to watch over you and your allies within 30 feet. You and your allies present when the spirit is summoned gain fast healing 10 and a ward against death. If those warded against death drop to 0 or fewer hps the spirit revives that character. The character revived heals an amount equal to twice your paladin level. This is an instantly activated ability.<br>Use: Selected.

Ascetic Stalker<br>
Type: General<br>Prerequisite: Ki Power, Ki Strike (Magic)<br>Benefit: Your monk and ninja levels stack for the purpose of determining the size of your ki pool. For example, a 4th-level monk/2nd-level ninja with this feat could use her ki powers 6 times per day.<br>

Martial Stalker<br>
Type: General<br>Prerequisite: Ki Power, +1 BAB.<br>Benefit: Your fighter, thug, and ninja levels stack for the purpose of determining the size of your ki pool, as well as your AC bonus. <br>Special: A fighter can select Martial Stalker as one of his fighter bonus feats.<br>

Devoted Tracker<br>
Type: General<br>Prerequisite: Paladin level 1, Tracking, and Animal Companion or Telthor Companion.<br>Benefit: Your Paladin levels count for the purpose of determining your animal companion's level (at Paladin level - 4). Your companion also gains Dash, Improved Evasion, and a +2 dodge AC bonus. If you have 15 levels or more of Paladin then your companion gains Spell Resistance equal to 5 + it's hit dice.  Your effective Druid companion level is equal to any druid levels plus Ranger - 3, Paladin - 4, and any Lion of Talisid levels. It can be further modified with the Natural Bond and Epic Animal Companion feats. This feat will also work for the Telthor Companion feat (stacking full Spirit Shaman levels and Paladin - 4 levels).

Extra Spirit Form<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Form 1/day<br>Benefit: You gain two extra daily uses of your spirit form ability.

Extra Spirit Journey<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Journey 1/day<br>Benefit: You gain two extra daily uses of your spirit journey ability.

Spirit Form (6x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Shaman Level 9.<br>Specifics: A spirit shaman learns how to temporarily transform herself into a spirit.  As a standard action, she can grant herself a 50% concealment bonus, a Deflection AC bonus equal to her Charisma modifer, and Spell Resistance equal to 11 + her character level for 10 rounds. This ability is usable 1 time per day at 9th level, 2 times per day at 15th level, and 1 additional time per day every 5 levels thereafter.<br>Use: Selected.

Spirit Form (7x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Shaman Level 9.<br>Specifics: A spirit shaman learns how to temporarily transform herself into a spirit.  As a standard action, she can grant herself a 50% concealment bonus, a Deflection AC bonus equal to her Charisma modifer, and Spell Resistance equal to 11 + her character level for 10 rounds. This ability is usable 1 time per day at 9th level, 2 times per day at 15th level, and 1 additional time per day every 5 levels thereafter.<br>Use: Selected.

Telthor Companion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spirit Shaman 4.<br>Specifics: A Telthor wolf companion may be summoned by the character. Note that a spirit shaman's effective level is level - 3 for determining their telthor companion's abilities and hit dice and only Spirit Shaman levels count. Natural Bond and Epic Animal Companion can increase your companion's hit dice. Warning: Taking Telthor Companion will prevent any other companion type (such as Dinosaur, Magical Beast, or Dragon) from being used.<br>Use: Selected.

Improved Natural Bond<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Telthor Companion or Devoted Tracker, Natural Bond.<br>Specifics: The character's level for determining their animal or telthor companion increases by 6, up to the character's total level. This bonus replaces the benefit gained by Natural Bond and stacks with Devoted Tracker and Epic Animal Companion. For example, a 10th level spirit shaman/8th level paladin would now have an telthor companion as if they were an 13th level druid (17th if they had Devoted Tracker).<br>Use: Automatic.

Battle Fortitude<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 2nd level, a scout gains a +1 bonus on Fortitude saves. This bonus increases to +2 at 11th, +3 at 20th, and +4 at 29th. <br>Use: Automatic.

Battlefield Signals<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 15th level, a Scout can communicate with other scouts on the battlefield by arranging terrain and leaving special marks which only scouts understand. This allows them to safely communicate large distances with other scouts.<br>Use: Automatic.

Free Movement<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 18th level and higher, a scout can slip out of bonds, grapples, and even the effects of confining spells easily. This ability duplicates the effect of a freedom of movement spell, except that it is always active.<br>Use: Automatic.

Skirmish AC Bonus<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics:  At 3rd level, scouts gain a +1 Dodge AC bonus. For each additional 4 levels of Scout, another +1 AC is gained. This bonus is +2 at 7th, +3 at 11th, +4 at 15th, +5 at 19th, +6 at 23rd, and +7 at 27th. Wilderness Stalkers gain +1 Dodge AC at levels 4 and 8 which will stack with the Scout bonus. Stalkers of the Depth gain +1 AC at 4th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Swift Ambusher<br>
You combine your scout training with the stealth of a rogue or ninja to open up new methods of ambushing enemies.<br>Type: General<br>Prerequisite: Scout Level 3, Sneak Attack +1d6<br>Benefit: Your scout levels are treated as rogue levels for the purpose of determining your total number of sneak dice (ie, the progression is faster). Rogue and Ninja levels count as Scout for determining Scout dodge AC. <br><br>For example, a 4th-level scout/7th-level rogue would have a +6d6 Sneak Attack (as a level 11 rogue).

Swift Hunter<br>
You have applied the hit-and-run tactics learned from scouting to your strong hunting abilities.<br>Type: General<br>Prerequisite: Scout level 3, Ranger level 2<br>Benefit: Your ranger and scout levels stack for the purpose of determining how many dice of sneak attack you have. Ranger levels count as Scout for determining Scout dodge AC.<br><br>For example, a 3rd-level scout/4th-level ranger would have +2d6 dice of sneak attack.

Daggercast<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Daggerspell mages seamlessly blend the use of their twin daggers with powerful spellcasting abilities. A daggerspell mage delivers melee touch attack spells with a dagger attack (adding the base dagger damage plus any enhance bonus from the right hand).<br>Use: Automatic.

Double Daggercast<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: As a daggerspell mage advances in level, the connection between his spellcasting abilities and his two-dagger fighting style strengthens. At 5th level or higher, a daggerspell mage delivers melee touch attack spells with both of his daggers (adding the base dagger damage plus any enhance bonus from each dagger).<br>Use: Automatic.

Arcane Infusion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Arcane Infusion: At 7th level and higher, a daggerspell mage can infuse arcane spell power into his daggers, temporarily enabling them to deal extra energy damage. The daggers deal an extra 1d6 points of magic damage for a number of rounds equal to their class level and has a 5 minute cooldown.<br>Use: Selected.

Arcane Throw<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 8th level and higher, a daggerspell mage can imbue arcane spell power into his thrown daggers. The mage can deliver a ranged touch attack spell with one of his daggers, adding it's damage to that of the spell (adding the base dagger damage plus any enhance bonus from the right hand). The dagger returns to the mage automatically.<br>Use: Automatic.

Daggerspell Mage Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level except 2nd, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane or invocation based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained. If you had more than one arcane or invocation spellcasting class before becoming a daggerspell mage, you must decide which class to progress in. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level except 2nd, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane or invocation based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained. Daggercast<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When making a melee touch attack spell the shaper uses her dagger to deliver the spell, adding her right hand's dagger damage to the spells (base dagger damage plus any enhance bonus). Supported spells are Cause Light Wounds, Cause Moderate Wounds, Cause Serious Wounds, Harm, and the reserve feat Clap of Thunder.<br>Use: Automatic.

Dagger Claws<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a daggerspell shaper of 2nd level or higher uses wild shape, she adds the enhance bonus from the dagger she is holding in her right hand into the natural attacks of her new form.  <br>Use: Automatic.

Daggerspell Flurry<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A 10th-level daggerspell shaper can blend spellcasting with a flurry of dagger attacks. When delivering a touch attack spell and wielding daggers, the daggerspell shaper adds each of her dagger's damage (base damage plus any enhance bonus) to the touch spell's damage. This replaces the Daggercast ability by adding both daggers damage instead of the main hand's damage. Supported spells are Cause Light Wounds, Cause Moderate Wounds, Cause Serious Wounds, Harm, and the reserve feat Clap of Thunder. Each dagger's damage is applied separately of the touch spell's affect.<br>Use: Selected.

Daggerspell Shaper Spellcasting Class (Ranger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level except 2nd, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in a divine based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. Plant Shape (Verdant Guardian)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Beginning at 6th level, the verdant guardian can use wild shape to take the form of a treant once per day (with a druid level equal to his verdant guardian level). The verdant guardian can cast spells normally while in plant shape.<br>Use: Selected.
