Epic Fiendish Resilience II<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Epic Fiendish Resilience.<br>Specifics: The Fiendish Resilience ability now grants fast healing 30 for 5 minutes and has 2 uses per day.<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Fiendish Resilience III<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Epic Fiendish Resilience II.<br>Specifics: The Fiendish Resilience ability now grants fast healing 35 for 5 minutes and 3 uses per day.<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Fiendish Resilience IV<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Epic Fiendish Resilience III.<br>Specifics: The Fiendish Resilience ability now grants fast healing 40 for 5 minutes and 4 uses per day.<br>Use: Automatic.

Guttural Invocations<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Warlock level 5, 13 Wisdom.<br>Specifics: You may now use invocations while polymorphed using Hellspawned Grace or Word of Changing. NOTE: The basic eldritch blast does not work in these forms. You MUST apply an essence or blast shape to be able to use eldritch blast in these forms.<br>Use: Automatic.

Empower Eldritch Blast<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Warlock level 5.<br>Specifics: Three times per day the Warlock can empower (+50%) the damage of their next Eldritch Blast. This is an instantly activated ability. This ability will stack with Maximize Eldritch Blast. Only the first target of the blast is affected by this boost (regardless of shape).<br>Use: Selected.

Maximize Eldritch Blast<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Warlock level 5.<br>Specifics: Three times per day the Warlock can maximize the damage of their next Eldritch Blast. This is an instantly activated ability. This ability will stack with Empower Eldritch Blast. Only the first target of the blast is affected by this boost (regardless of shape).<br>Use: Selected.

Dark Transient<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: Warlock level 5.<br>Specifics: The duration of your Flee the Scene invocation is now doubled and provides you with a +1 Attack Bonus.<br>Use: Automatic.

Paragon Visionary<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Warlock level 5, Spellcraft 24, 8d6 Eldritch Blast<br>Specifics: When cast, the warlock gains true seeing as well as a bonus to Listen and Spot for 24 hours. The bonus is twice your Wisdom modifier or +6, whichever is higher.<br>Use: Selected.

Practiced Invoker<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 4 ranks, Warlock 1.<br>Specifics: Your caster level for invocations increases by +4. This can not increase your caster level beyond your HD. Your eldritch blast damage will be increased as though you had gained the appropriate amount of levels in warlock.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>

Extra Wild Shape<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Ability to use wild shape.<br>Specifics: You use your wild shape ability two more times per day than you otherwise could. If you are able to use wild shape to become an elemental, you also gain one additional elemental wild shape use per day. These extra uses are real uses and will help qualify for feats such as Dragon Shape.<br>Use: Automatic.

Wild Shape (10x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an animal at will. It may be used once per day at fifth level, twice per day at sixth level, three times per day at seventh level, four times per day at 10th level, five times per day at 14th level, and six times per day at 18th level. The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level. Available shapes include bear, wolf, boar and badger. At 12th level, the druid may assume the dire form of any of these animals.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose an animal form to take.

Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an animal at will. The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose one of four elemental types (earth, air, fire or water) to become.

Elemental Shape (10x day)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Druid level 16.<br>Specifics: The character with this ability can take the shape of an elemental (a huge elemental at 16th level, and an elder elemental at 20th level). The ability may be used once per day at level 16, twice per day at level 18, three times per day at level 20, and one additional use for each additional two levels (to 8/day at 30th). The transformation has a duration of 1 hour per class level.<br>Use: Selected. After choosing the ability, the character must choose one of four elemental types (earth, air, fire or water) to become.

Way of the Tome<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd, 4th, and 5th level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in the wizard class.<br>Use: Automatic.

Way of the Slayer<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An Elite Commando who chooses the way of the slayer seeks to master the art of war with a minor emphasis on stealth and gains +1 Dodge AC at 1st level, a +1 Attack Bonus at 3rd level, and +2 magic damage at 5th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Way of the Shadow<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: An Elite Commando who chooses the way of the shadow seeks to master the arts of war and stealth and in doing so gains +1d6 dice of sneak attack at 1st, 3rd, and 5th level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Hexblade's Curse<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Once per day, a hexblade can unleash a curse upon a foe as an instant action. The target of a hexblade's curse takes a -2 penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls for 1 hour. A successful Will save (DC 10 + 1/2 hexblade's class level + hexblade's Cha modifier) negates the effect and does not consume a use of the curse. This ability shares a cooldown with Curse of Lethargy and Curse of Silence.<br><br>At every odd level after 1st a hexblade gains the ability to use her curse one additional time per day. Multiple hexblade's curses do not stack and can be removed with a Remove Curse spell.<br>When a hexblade attains 7th level, the penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls incurred by a target of the hexblade's curse becomes -4 instead of -2.<br>When a hexblade attains 19th level, the penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls incurred by a target of the hexblade's curse becomes -6 instead of -4.<br>Use: Selected<br>

Armored Caster<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: As a martial arcane caster using spell-like abilities, the hexblade does not suffer from arcane spell failure, her spells cannot be interrupted, her spells bypass spell resistance, and she cannot use metamagics.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Arcane Resistance<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 2nd level, a hexblade gains a bonus equal to her base Charisma bonus (minimum +1) on saving throws. This bonus can not exceed a hexblades level and does not stack with Dark Blessing or Dark One's Own Luck.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Mettle<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level and higher, a hexblade can resist magical and unusual attacks with great willpower and gains a +4 bonus on Fort and Will saving throws. This bonus is only applied if at least half your hit dice are Hexblade levels.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Dark Companion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: In combat, a dark companion functions like a floating hex that you place upon your foes. By weakening the defenses of enemies, your dark companion makes your spells and attacks (and those of your allies) more powerful. Any enemy within 20' of you takes a -2 penalty on its saves and to its AC. This penalty stacks with the hexblades curse abilities.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Hexblade's Spellbook, Level 1<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Level 1 spells: Entropic Shield, Expeditious Retreat, Ghostly Visage, Protection from Evil, and Shield <br>Use: Selected<br>

Hexblade's Spellbook, Level 2<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Level 2 spells: Eagle's Splendor, Faerie Fire, Invisibility, Mirror Image, and See Invisibility<br>Use: Selected<br>

Hexblade's Spellbook, Level 3<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Level 3 spells: Greater Magic Weapon, Haste, Protection from Energy, Spiderskin, and Vampiric Touch<br>Use: Selected<br>

Hexblade's Spellbook, Level 4<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Level 4 spells: Enervation, Greater Invisibility, Polymorph Self (War Troll), Remove Curse, and Shadow Form<br>Use: Selected<br>

Greater Hexblade's Curse<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a hexblade attains 7th level, the penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls incurred by a target of the hexblade's curse becomes -4 instead of -2.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Aura of Unluck<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Once per day, a hexblade of 12th level or higher can create a baleful aura of misfortune. Any melee or ranged attack made against the hexblade while this aura of unluck is active has a 20% miss chance (similar to the effect of concealment). The aura lasts for a number of rounds equal to 4 + the hexblade's Charisma bonus (if any). This ability is instant.<br><br>At 16th level and every four levels after that (20th, 24th, and 28th) the hexblade gains an additional use of the aura.<br>Use: Selected<br>

Dire Hexblade's Curse<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a hexblade attains 19th level, the penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls incurred by a target of the hexblade's curse becomes -6 instead of -4.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Hexblade's Curse<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Once per day, a hexblade can unleash a curse upon a foe as an instant action. The target of a hexblade's curse takes a -2 penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls for 1 hour. A successful Will save (DC 10 + 1/2 hexblade's class level + hexblade's Cha modifier) negates the effect and does not consume a use of the curse. This ability shares a cooldown with Curse of Lethargy and Curse of Silence.<br><br>At every odd level after 1st a hexblade gains the ability to use her curse one additional time per day. Multiple hexblade's curses do not stack and can be removed with a Remove Curse spell.<br>When a hexblade attains 7th level, the penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls incurred by a target of the hexblade's curse becomes -4 instead of -2.<br>When a hexblade attains 19th level, the penalty on attacks, saves, skill checks, and armor class rolls incurred by a target of the hexblade's curse becomes -6 instead of -4.<br>Use: Selected<br>

Craft Rod<br>
Type of Feat: Item Creation<br>Prerequisite: Spellcaster Level 9th+.<br>Specifics: You can create a rod of any spell of 6th level or less that you know. To create a rod, cast an appropriate spell on a bone rod in your inventory. Creating a rod costs gold pieces based on the relative power of the spell. Rods containing first level spells can cost a few thousand gold pieces while those made with higher level spells can cost tens of thousands. Warlock invocations cannot be used in the creation of rods. Newly crafted rods have 50 charges.<br>Use: Cast a spell on a bone rod in your inventory.

Craft Staff<br>
Type of Feat: Item Creation<br>Prerequisite: Spellcaster Level  12th+.<br>Specifics: You can create a staff of any spell of 9th level or less that you know. To create a staff, cast an appropriate spell on a bone staff in your inventory. Creating a staff costs gold pieces based on the relative power of the spell. Staves containing first level spells can cost a few thousand gold pieces while those made with higher level spells can cost tens of thousands. Warlock invocations cannot be used in the creation of staves. Newly crafted staves have 50 charges.<br>Use: Cast a spell on a bone staff in your inventory.

Forge Ring<br>
Type of Feat: Item Creation<br>Prerequisite: Spellcaster Level  12th+.<br>Specifics: You can create magic rings, the cost of which is based on the relative power of the item.<br>Use: Cast a spell on an electrum ring in your inventory.

Tireless<br>
Type of Feat: Background Trait<br>Prerequisite: Can only take this feat at 1st-level.<br>Specifics: Character does not become tired when using rage, dervish dance, or frenzy.<br>Use: Automatic.

Extend Rage II<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Rage, frenzy, or feral stance ability.<br>Specifics: Each use of rage, frenzy, or feral stance lasts 5 rounds longer. Extend Rage II through IV each add an additional 5 rounds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Extend Rage III<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Rage, frenzy, or feral stance ability.<br>Specifics: Each use of rage, frenzy, or feral stance lasts 5 rounds longer. Extend Rage II through IV each add an additional 5 rounds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Extend Rage IV<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisite: Rage, frenzy, or feral stance ability.<br>Specifics: Each use of rage, frenzy, or feral stance lasts 5 rounds longer. Extend Rage II through IV each add an additional 5 rounds.<br>Use: Automatic.

Lord of All Essences<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Warlock level 5, Spellcraft 24.<br>Specifics: A warlock with this feat gains a +4 bonus to caster level checks to beat a creature's spell resistance (which stacks with the Spell Penetration feat line) with all blasts and invocations. In addition, the DC of all eldritch blasts and invocations you use increases by 2.<br>Use: Automatic.

Ability Focus (Hexblade's Curse)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Your chosen ability becomes more potent than normal.<br>Prerequisite: Hexblade level 1.<br>Benefit: Add +2 to the DC for all saving throws against the chosen ability. Your Curses now debuff your target's Will save by 4 points even when you fail to curse them.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Extra Hexblade's Curse I<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You use your Hexblade's Curse ability three more times per day than you otherwise could for each Extra Curse feat you have. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Extra Hexblade's Curse II<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You use your Hexblade's Curse ability three more times per day than you otherwise could for each Extra Curse feat you have. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Extra Hexblade's Curse III<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You use your Hexblade's Curse ability three more times per day than you otherwise could for each Extra Curse feat you have. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Extra Aura of Unluck<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You use your Aura of Unluck ability three more times per day than you otherwise could. The duration of your Aura is also increased by two rounds.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Practiced Caster (Hexblade)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Spellcraft 4 ranks, Hexblade 4.<br>Specifics: Your caster level for spells increases by +4. This can increase your caster level beyond your HD. Your Mettle ability now provides 10 + Hexblade level Spell Resistance at level 13. Spellcasting Prodigy improves this by +2.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Boon of the Unlucky<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Aura of Unluck.<br>Specifics: You trade a single use of your Aura of Unluck ability for a use of Hexblade's Curse. There is a 5% chance you will regain two uses of Hexblade's Curse and a 10% chance you will not consume a use of Aura of Unluck. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses. You add 10% to the concealment granted by your Aura of Unluck.<br>Use: Selected.<br>

Epic Dark Companion<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Character level 21+, Hexblade 26.<br>Specifics: Your dark companion inflicts a -4 penalty instead of -2.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Arcane Strike<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Hexblade 5, 15 Charisma.<br>Specifics: You gain a +2 magic damage bonus with your attacks. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses. You deals 1 additional point of magic damage for every 5 levels past 5th (3 at 10th, 4 at 15th, 5 at 20th, 6 at 25th, and 7 at 30th) if Hexblade is the only class the character has.<br>Use: Automatic.<br>

Epic Hexblade's Curse<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Character level 21+, Hexblade 26.<br>Specifics: Your Hexblade's Curse penalty increases by 2.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Epic Misfortune<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Character level 21+, Hexblade 20.<br>Specifics: Your Hexblade's Curse ability now affects all enemies within 30' of your target. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Epic Improved Aura of Unluck<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Character level 21+, Hexblade 20.<br>Specifics: Your Aura of Unluck ability now provides 50% concealment instead of 20%.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Rhyme of Misfortune<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Hexblade 1, Bardsongs, Curse Song.<br>Specifics: Your Bard and Hexblade levels stack for determining the power of your Curse Song. You gain a bonus to your bardic level equal to half your Hexblade level (capped by your bardic level).<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Lingering Hexblade's Curse<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Hexblade 1.<br>Specifics: Your Hexblade's Curse, Curse of Lethargy, and Curse of Silence last two hours instead of one. You also gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses.<br>Use: Automatic<br>

Arcane Mastery<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Hexblade 5<br>Specifics: The duration of your spells, auras, and curses are doubled (as well as the Hexblade's Minor Shapeshift feat). You gain a +1 DC with all Hexblade abilities (Curses, Demoralize Opponent).<br>

Minor Shapeshift (Hexblade)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: Your mastery of shapeshifting magic allows you to reshape your flesh in small but significant ways.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 4th-level Hexblade spells.<br>Benefit: You gain a +1 DC bonus on your curses. As long as you have at least one use of your level 4 spells remaining, you can instantly activate this feat to grant yourself one of the following benefits:<br><br>Might: +2 bonus on melee damage rolls.<br>Speed: +5-foot enhancement bonus to movement.<br>Vigor: Temporary hit points equal to your HD.<br><br>The chosen benefit lasts for a number of rounds equal to your hexblade caster level. If you activate this feat a second time while a previous feat is still in effect, the first benefit ends immediately.<br>Use: Selected.<br>

Curse of Lethargy<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Hexblade 1.<br>Specifics: You can spend one use of your Hexblade's Curse ability to inflict the Slow spell on any enemies within 30' of your target. If you have the Epic Hexblade's Curse feat, you will also decrease the physical damage dealt by any opponents you affect by 5 points. A successful Will save (DC 10 + 1/2 hexblade's class level + hexblade's Cha modifier) negates the effect and does not consume a use of the curse if no targets are affected. Ability Focus (Hexblade's Curse) also affects this ability. This ability shares a cooldown with Curse of Silence and Hexblade's Curse.<br>Use: Selected.<br>

Curse of Silence<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Hexblade 1.<br>Specifics: You can spend one use of your Hexblade's Curse ability to Silence any enemies within 30' of your target. If you have the Epic Hexblade's Curse feat, you will also knockdown any opponents you affect.  A successful Will save (DC 10 + 1/2 hexblade's class level + hexblade's Cha modifier) negates the effect and does not consume a use of the curse if no targets are affected. Ability Focus (Hexblade's Curse) also affects this ability. This ability shares a cooldown with Hexblade's Curse and Curse of Lethargy.<br>Use: Selected.<br>

Dreadful Wrath<br>
Type: Background<br>You are terrible to behold in battle, and few foes have the heart to face you without quailing.<br>Benefit: Each enemy within a 20-foot radius of you must succeed on a Will save (DC 10 + 1/2 your character level + your Cha modifier) or be shaken (-2 Saves, -2 AC, -2 AB) for 1 minute. This ability does not affect those immune to fear.

Demoralize Opponent<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You try to intimidate your opponent and demoralize them. Failure to make a Will save (DC equal to d20 + your Intimidate skill) causes the enemy to suffer a 2 point penalty to attack rolls, saving throws, and skill checks. This ability does not affect those immune to fear, those with an intelligence less than 4, or those that are constructs or undead.<br>Use: Selected.<br>

Epic Heavy Armor Optimization<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>You have mastered the use of heavy armor, maximizing its protective qualities while moving more easily in it.<br>Prerequisite: Greater Heavy Armor Optimization, Base Attack Bonus +20, Character level 21+<br>Benefit: When you are wearing heavy armor, lessen the armor check penalty by 3 and increase the armor bonus by 3. This is done as a +skill bonus to (Hide, Move Silently, Parry, Set Trap, Sleight of Hand, Tumble) and a +3 Dodge AC bonus. This bonus replaces the one from Greater Heavy Armor Optimization.<br>Special: A fighter can choose Epic Heavy Armor Optimization as one of his bonus fighter feats.<br>Use: Automatic.

Two-Handed Weapon Combat Style<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Ranger 2<br>Specifics: You have chosen to train in the art of fighting with a two-handed weapon. As your ranger level increases, you gain the following bonus feats: Power Attack at 2nd level, Improved Power Attack at 6th level, Enhanced Power Attack at 11th level, and Supreme Power Attack at 21st level. You gain the benefit of these feats even if you do not meet their prerequisites. You suffer a -4 AB penalty when using armor heavier than Light Armor.<br>Use: Automatic.

Templar Spellcasting Class (Paladin)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Templar level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, a templar gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Paladin level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Fervor<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: A templar gains a +1 bonus to both Fortitude and Willpower saves at levels 2, 4, 6, 8, and 10.<br>Use: Automatic.

Marksman Spellcasting Class (Ranger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Marksman level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, a marksman gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Ranger level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Child of Night Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level except 2nd, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in a casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a child of night, you must decide which class to progress in. This is a 9/10 progression class in either Divine, Arcane, or Invocation magic. Eldritch Blast progresses for Warlocks when a level granting spellcasting progression is gained if they chose the Warlock progression.<br>Use: Automatic.

Child of Night Spellcasting Class (Cleric)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level except 2nd, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in a casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. Rune of Flame<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel magical energy into runes of fire.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a fire spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can throw a fire rune. The rune affects a 10-foot-radius burst and deals 2d6 points of damage per level of that fire spell. A successful Reflex save halves the damage. The rune lasts 30 seconds if no targets enter it's area of effect and this feat has a 30 second cooldown. Only one rune can be active (including runes from the other Rune of X feats).<br><br>Trigger Spells: Deadly Lahar, Delayed Blast Fireball, Elemental Shield, Fire Storm, Fireball, Flame Arrow, Flame Strike, Incendiary Cloud, Meteor Swarm, Shades, Wall of Fire, Firebrand, Inferno, Combust, Flame Weapon, Fireburst, Greater Fireburst, Shroud of Flame, Body of the Sun, Scorching Ray, Lesser Energized Shield, Energized Shield, Flame of Faith, Weapon of Energy, Orb of Fire, Heartfire<br>

Rune of Ice<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel magical energy into runes of ice.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a cold spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can throw a cold rune. The rune affects a 10-foot-radius burst and deals 2d6 points of damage per level of that cold spell. A successful Reflex save halves the damage. The rune lasts 30 seconds if no targets enter it's area of effect and this feat has a 30 second cooldown. Only one rune can be active (including runes from the other Rune of X feats).<br><br>Trigger Spells: Cone of Cold, Elemental Shield, Frost Breath, Shades, Ice Storm, Polar Ray, Hypothermia, Creeping Cold, Greater Creeping Cold, Glacial Wrath, Lesser Energized Shield, Energized Shield, Weapon of Energy, Orb Cold<br>

Rune of Storms<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel magical energy into runes of lightning.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a lightning spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can throw a lightning rune. The rune affects a 10-foot-radius burst and deals 2d6 points of damage per level of that lightning spell. A successful Reflex save halves the damage. The rune lasts 30 seconds if no targets enter it's area of effect and this feat has a 30 second cooldown. Only one rune can be active (including runes from the other Rune of X feats).<br><br>Trigger Spells: Arc of Lightning, Call Lightning, Chain Lightning, Lightning Bolt, Storm of Vengeance, Scintillating Sphere, Call Lightning Storm, Energized Shield, Weapon of Energy, Orb of Electricty, Lightning Blast<br>

Rune of Thunder<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel magical energy into runes of thunder.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have a sonic spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can throw a sonic rune. The rune affects a 10-foot-radius burst and deals 2d4 points of damage per level of that sonic spell. A successful Reflex save halves the damage. The rune lasts 30 seconds if no targets enter it's area of effect and this feat has a 30 second cooldown. Only one rune can be active (including runes from the other Rune of X feats).<br><br>Trigger Spells: Cacophonic Burst, Shout, Greater Shout, Castigate, Energized Shield, Sonic Shield, Lion's Roar, Resonating Bolt, Sound Blast, Orb of Sound, Song of Discord, War Cry, Dirge, and Wounding Whispers<br>

Rune of Vitriol<br>
Type of Feat: Reserve<br>You can channel magical energy into runes of acid.<br>Prerequisite: Ability to cast 3rd-level spells.<br>Benefit: As long as you have an acid spell of 3rd level or higher available to cast, you can throw an acid rune. The rune affects a 10-foot-radius burst and deals 2d6 points of damage per level of that acid spell. A successful Reflex save halves the damage. The rune lasts 30 seconds if no targets enter it's area of effect and this feat has a 30 second cooldown. Only one rune can be active (including runes from the other Rune of X feats).<br><br>Trigger Spells: Acid Fog, Greater Shadow Conjuration, Melfs Acid Arrow, Storm of Vengeance, Mestils Acid Breath, Vitriolic Sphere, Lesser Energized Shield, Energized Shield, Weapon of Energy, Orb of Acid<br>

Raging Vigor<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Barbarian 5.<br>Specifics: You expend a use of rage in order to heal twice the total of your hit dice and your Constitution modifier.<br>Use: Selected<br>

Epic Spell: Epic Mass Heal<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 25, the ability to cast 9th level spells.<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Epic Mass Heal.<br> <br>All allies within the area of effect regain 300 hit points and are cured of the following status effects: blinded, confused, dazzled, deafened, diseased, feebleminded, insane, nauseated, poisoned, and stunned. Healing spells have a reverse effect on undead, harming instead of healing them (allowing a will save for half damage).<br><br>Usable twice per day. <br><br>Use: Selected.

Epic Spell: Epic Call Lightning Storm<br>
Type of Feat: Epic<br>Prerequisites: 21st level, Spellcraft 27, the ability to cast 9th level spells.<br>Specifics: The character can cast the Epic Spell Call Lightning Storm.<br> <br>This spell smites an area around the caster with bolts of lightning which strike all enemies. The bolts do 35d6 points of electrical damage, and the caster gets a +9 bonus to the spell's DC when targets attempt to save against it. <br><br>Use: Selected.

Hide in Plain Sight<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Rogue 13+.<br>Specifics: The rogue or shadowdancer is able to use the Hide skill even while being observed. Note: This ability will not always be successful in the frenzy of combat -- but disengaging from the enemy before attempting to hide in plain sight will succeed.<br>Use: Automatic.

Chosen Form<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Feral Shifter 1.<br>Specifics: You choose the shape you always assume from the following list: Worg, Telthor Bear, White Tiger, Stag Beetle, Phase Spider. This form is the one you believe to be your true shape, unshackled by the race you born unto. Your physical stats in feral form are 14 plus half your level (improves on even levels). Base damage is d6. Base Natural AC is 2 + half your level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Animal Companion Combat Style<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Ranger 2<br>Specifics: You have chosen to train in the art of fighting with your animal companion. As your ranger level increases, you gain the following bonus feats: Natural Bond at 2nd level, Protective Ward at 6th level, Exalted Companion at 11th level, and Epic Animal Companion at 21st level. You gain the benefit of these feats even if you do not meet their prerequisites.<br>Use: Automatic.

Blade of the Shadow<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Prerequisites: Shadowdancer, 2d6 Sneak Attack, BAB +6<br>Benefit: You gain +1d6 dice of Sneak Attack at levels 4, 7, and 10 of Shadowdancer. Your Shadow Daze ability restores a use of Shadow Evade.<br>NOTE: Frantic Reactions does not qualify for the 2d6 Sneak Attack for this feat.

Wildfire Blade<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Ranger 6, 14 Wisdom<br>Benefit: When you take levels in Invisible Blade, you gain ranger spellcasting progression at each level. Your Blades of Fire spell adds 1d10 fire damage instead of 1d8. If you have the Protective Ward feat, your Invisible Blade levels count as Ranger levels.

Shadow Lyrist<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Bard level 8, 16 Charisma<br>Benefit: When you take levels in Shadowdancer, you gain bard spellcasting progression at each odd level. This feat should be taken before your first level in shadowdancer. Each time you cast Darkness, Invisibility, or Shadow Conjuration as a bard you regain a use of Shadow Evade.

Warband Arcanist<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Sorcerer level 8, 16 Charisma<br>Benefit: When you take levels in the Arcane Archer class, you gain sorcerer spellcasting progression at each odd level. This feat should be taken before your first level in arcane archer. Your Imbue Arrow abilitity will also add your Sorcerer level to your Arcane Archer level to determine damage. Casting Fireball as a sorcerer will cause you to regain a use of Imbue Arrow.

Extra Recall Spirit I<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisite: Recall Spirit<br>Benefit: You gain three extra daily uses of your recall spirit ability.

Extra Recall Spirit II<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisite: Recall Spirit<br>Benefit: You gain six extra daily uses of your recall spirit ability. This feat replaces Extra Recall Spirit I.

Holy Icon of War<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Warpriest level 10, Holy Warrior<br>Benefit: Your Holy Warrior ability provides an additional +2 damage.

Reward of Life<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>When you lay on hands, you are also healed.<br>Prerequisite: Lay on hands class feature.<br>Benefit: Each time you use your lay on hands ability to heal a creature other than yourself, you are healed as well. This ability has no effect if you use lay on hands to harm undead.

Companion Barding<br>
You train your companion to wear a chain barding.<br>Prerequisites: Natural Bond.<br>Benefit: Your companion (telthor, animal, dino, elemental, dragon) gains a +5 armor enhance bonus.

Wild Hunter<br><br>
Your bow is not your only companion.<br>Prerequisites: Marksman 1 or Arbalest 1, Natural Bond<br>Benefit: Your Marksman level (-3) or Arbalest level (-3) is added to your Ranger levels for determining your companions effectiveness.

Battlefield Surveyor<br>
Your skill at studying the shifting tides of battle have improved your ability to hear or spot enemies.<br>Prerequisites: Tracking, Scout 5<br>Benefit: You gain a +1 bonus to Listen and Spot for every 3 levels of Scout and Wilderness Stalker.

Epic Empty Body<br>
Prerequisites: Empty Body<br>Benefit: A monk gains haste while using Empty Body. The duration of Empty Body is extended by his Wisdom modifier. Sacred Fist levels are treated as Monk levels.

Nature's Gift<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Beast Heart Adept 2<br>Specifics: The Beast Heart Adepts animal companion gains a passive regeneration of 1 point. This improves to 2 points at level 3 and 3 points at level 5.

Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level (and spells known, if applicable) as if you had also gained a level in an arcane based spellcasting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained. <br>Use: Automatic.

Knight of the Wild Spellcasting Class (Paladin)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, a knight of the wild gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Paladin and a Ranger level. Unlike normal paladin or ranger levels, these are full spellcasting levels (not cut in half) so that a 4 Paladin/4 Ranger/22 Knight of the Wild has a caster level of 24 (28 with practiced caster) in each class.<br>Use: Automatic.

Knight of the Wild Spellcasting Class (Ranger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, a knight of the wild gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Paladin and a Ranger level. Unlike normal paladin or ranger levels, these are full spellcasting levels (not cut in half) so that a 4 Paladin/4 Ranger/22 Knight of the Wild has a caster level of 24 (28 with practiced caster) in each class.<br>Use: Automatic.

Bloodline Exemplar Spellcasting Class (Sorcerer)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, bloodline exemplar gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Sorcerer level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Inspire Heroics (Show of Arms)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Skald level 1<br>Specifics: A skald can use song or poetics to inspire heroics in her allies (including herself), improving their combat abilities. Your inspiration grants a +2 enhance bonus to Strength and Dexterity with an additional +1 for every 5 skald levels (maximum +6 at 20th).<br>Use: Activated. Once turned on, this Inspiration will turn off any other Inspiration this character was previously using (including Marshal or Bard inspirations). Inspire Heroics will not stack with Inspire Defense from a bard. Inspire Heroics counts as singing a bard song and will stop any other inspirations that are active when started (and will be stopped if a bardic inspiration is started). Your inspire heroics ability also provides 1 point of regeneration every round in addition to the previous effects. This improves to 2 points at 11th level and 3 points at 21st level.

Inspire Heroics (Aggression)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Skald level 6<br>Specifics: Your inspire heroics ability now also provides 2 point of bonus magic damage on attacks. This improves to 3 points at 11th and 4 points at 21st level.

Inspire Heroics (Protection)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Skald level 11<br>Specifics: Your inspire heroics ability now also provides 1 point of dodge AC in addition to the previous effects. This improves to +2 AC at 18th and +3 AC at 25th level.

Inspire Heroics (Clarity)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Skald level 16<br>Specifics: Your inspire heroics ability now also provides +1 enhance bonus to Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma for every 5 skald levels (maximum +6 at 30th) in addition to the previous effects.

Inspire Heroics (Cover)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Skald level 21<br>Specifics: Your inspire heroics ability now also provides 5% concealment in addition to the previous effects. This improves to 10% at 28th level.

Inspire Heroics (Excellence)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Skald level 26<br>Specifics: Your inspire heroics ability now also provides +2 to all skills in addition to the previous effects. This improves to +3 at 30th level.

Adrenaline Boost (3 minute cooldown)<br>
You can exhort your allies to discover a reservoir of energy they didn't think they had. As a standard action, you can grant temporary hit points equal to 10 plus your skald level to all of your allies within 30 feet (including yourself). If the ally's current hit point total is no more than half his full normal hit points, the number of temporary hit points granted to that character is doubled. The temporary hit points last for up to 1 minute per skald level (3 minute minimum). This ability has a 3 minute cooldown. The cooldown becomes 2 minutes at 11th level and 1 minute at 21st level. If the timer becomes confused, right click your character and use Adrenaline Boost from that GUI and it will reset the timer to work correctly.

Adrenaline Boost (2 minute cooldown)<br>
You can exhort your allies to discover a reservoir of energy they didn't think they had. As a standard action, you can grant temporary hit points equal to 10 plus your skald level to all of your allies within 30 feet (including yourself). If the ally's current hit point total is no more than half his full normal hit points, the number of temporary hit points granted to that character is doubled. The temporary hit points last for up to 1 minute per skald level (3 minute minimum). This ability has a 3 minute cooldown. The cooldown becomes 2 minutes at 11th level and 1 minute at 21st level. If the timer becomes confused, right click your character and use Adrenaline Boost from that GUI and it will reset the timer to work correctly.

As a standard action, you can grant temporary hit points equal to 10 plus your skald level to all of your allies within 30 feet (including yourself). Arcane Dilettante<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Factotum level 2<br>Specifics: At 2nd level, you acquire a vague understanding of magic. You know that with a few weird hand gestures and an array of grunts and bizarre words, you can conjure up something that looks like a spell. The spell is cast as though you were a wizard with a caster level equal to your class level immediately after resting. <color=Red>Arcane Dilettante will not provide any bonus if you have the Able Learner feat.</color><br><br>Spells<br>Level 2: Mage Armor<br>Level 4: Ghostly Visage<br>Level 6: Heroism<br>Level 7: Greater Magic Weapon<br>Level 9: Greater Resistance<br>Level 10: Improved Mage Armor<br>Level 12: Lesser Mind Blank<br>Level 14: Greater Heroism<br>Level 17: True Seeing<br>Level 18: Superior Resistance<br>Level 20: Shadow Shield<br>Level 24: Greater Stoneskin<br>Level 27: Premonition<br>Level 28: Chasing Perfection

Brains over Brawn<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Factotum level 3<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, you gain your Intelligence bonus as a modifier on checks involving skills based on Strength or Dexterity. This bonus is capped by half your class level (maximum +15 at 30th). The affected skills are: Hide, Move Silently, Open Lock, Parry, Set Trap, Sleight of Hand, and Tumble.

Opportunistic Piety (Heal Wounds)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Factotum level 5<br>Specifics: Factotums are legendary for the number of holy symbols, lucky trinkets, and blessed items they keep handy. As the saying goes, there are no atheists in the dungeon. You can channel divine energy (expend a turn undead use) to heal injuries or harm undead. If you use this ability to heal injuries, you channel positive energy to heal a living creature of a number of points of damage equal to twice your factotum level + your Int modifier. The energy will also deal the same amount of damage to undead targets.

Cunning Defense<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Factotum level 16<br>Specifics: At 16th level, you gain your Intelligence bonus as a dodge bonus to Armor Class. You do not gain this benefit when wearing medium or heavy armor. <color=Red>If you have levels in any class other than Factotum or Swashbuckler, this bonus is cut in half and will cap at +5.</color>

Mystic Reservist Spellcasting Class (Bard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in a casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a mystic reservist, you must decide which class to progress in.

If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a mystic reservist, you must decide which class to progress in. 

Mystic Reservist Spellcasting Class (Spirit Shaman)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in a casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a mystic reservist, you must decide which class to progress in. 

Mystic Reservist Spellcasting Class (Wizard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in a casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one spellcasting class before becoming a mystic reservist, you must decide which class to progress in. 

Bloodline Exemplar Spellcasting Class (Sorcerer)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in sorcerer. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats.

Ancient Bloodline (Abyssal)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: One of your ancestors had ties to a powerful arcane source of magic which has passed down your bloodline to you. Your powers of sorcerery are largely based on this bloodline and as such is leaves a faint signature on all the magic you perform. This feat provides no benefit but clearly defines the source of your power for roleplay.

Force of Personality<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: You use Charisma to determine your Will saves, not Wisdom.

Force Mage Spellcasting Class (Sorcerer)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in an arcane casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one arcane spellcasting class before becoming a force mage, you must decide which class to progress in. 

Force Mage Spellcasting Class (Wizard)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At each level, you gain new spells per day and an increase in caster level and spells known as if you had also attained a level in an arcane casting class to which you belonged before adding the prestige class level. You do not, however, gain any other benefit a character of that class would have gained such as bonus feats. If you had more than one arcane spellcasting class before becoming a force mage, you must decide which class to progress in. 

Force Protection<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Forge Mage 1<br>Specifics: You gain an additional +1 bonus from the Shield spell at levels 1, 3, and 5. Any arcane spell failure you suffer from armor or shields is doubled.<br>

Force Missiles<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Force Mage 1<br>Specifics: You may cast the magic missile spell at will as though it were a reserve feat. Each use launches one missile plus one missile per class level which deal 1d3+3 damage. You also gain an additional missile for every 10 HD (maximum +3 for 9 missiles at 30 HD with 5 Force Mage). Your Force Missiles ability will strip the shield spell from your target. Force Missiles can gain the benefit of the Empower and Maximize Reserve Feats abilities like other reserve feats.

Arcane Heroism<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Your skill at mixing the arcane arts with martial skill are such that you inspire others with your heroics.<br>Prerequisites: Hexblade 5<br>Benefit: You gain the benefits of the Heroism spell at all times. These benefits improve to those of Greater Heroism once you obtain 13 levels in Hexblade. This bonus will not stack with the Heroism or Greater Heroism spells.

Siphon the Weave<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Arcane Mastery<br>Specifics: Once per minute you can tap the weave to restore a use of your curse and aura of unluck abilities. You take damage equal to your character level as magic damage (unresistable) as a result of the strain. You gain a +1 DC bonus on curses.<br>Use: 60s Cooldown, Selected.

Fighter Weapon Training<br>
<b>Weapon Training:</b> Starting at 5th level, a fighter gains a +1 bonus on attack and damage rolls. This bonus improves by +1 at 9th, 13th, 17th, and 20th level. This bonus is lost if the character has levels in any class other than Fighter, Divine Champion, or Weapon Master.<br>

Fighter Armor Training<br>
<b>Armor Training:</b> Starting at 3rd level, a fighter learns to be more maneuverable while wearing armor. Whenever he is wearing armor, he reduces the armor check penalty by 1 (to a minimum of 0) and increases the maximum Dexterity bonus allowed by his armor by 1. Every four levels thereafter (7th, 11th, and 15th), these bonuses increase by +1 each time, to a maximum –4 reduction of the armor check penalty and a +4 increase of the maximum Dexterity bonus allowed. This bonus is lost if the character has levels in any class other than Fighter, Divine Champion, or Weapon Master.<br><br><b>Armor Mastery:</b> At 19th level, a fighter gains DR 5/— whenever he is wearing armor or using a shield. This bonus is lost if the character has levels in any class other than Fighter, Divine Champion, or Weapon Master. This damage reduction does not stack with any other source of DR.<br>

Wholeness of Body<br>
Type of feat: Class<br>Specifics: Restores a number of hit points equal to twice the number of the monk's class levels as well as providing 1 point of regeneration for 3 minutes if the character only has Monk levels. Wholeness of Body can be used 1 time for every 3 monks levels.<br>Use: Selected 

Reserve Feat Mastery<br>
Your mastery over Reserve feats improves.<br>Prerequisites: Acidic Splatter, Clap of Thunder, Fiery Burst, Invisible Needle, Storm Bolt, or Winter's Blast.<br>Benefit: You add an additional half of the spells level to the DC, rounded up (maximum +5).

Epic Reserve Feat Mastery<br>
Your mastery over Reserve feats becomes truly epic.<br>Prerequisites: Reserve Feat Mastery.<br>Benefit: You add an additional bonus to the DC equal to the spells level (maximum +9). This replaces the bonus from Reserve Feat Mastery.

Reserve Feat Enhancement<br>
You learn to add secondary effects to some of your reserve feats.<br>Prerequisites: Reserve Feat Mastery.<br>Benefit: Winter's Blast can inflict -2 Saves, Fiery Burst can inflict a -1 AB/-1AC penalty, Storm Bolt can inflict slow, Clap of Thunder can deafen with a +2 DC, Acidic Splatter can inflict a -2 AB penalty, and Invisible Needle can reduce the dodge AC of the target by 2. A Fortitude save negates the secondary effect. Secondary effects do not stack and last for one round.

Improved Reserve Feats<br>
Your ability to channel the elements allows you to sometimes bypass elemental resists.<br>Prerequisites: Reserve Feat Mastery<br>Benefit: You bypass any elemental resists the enemy has when using reserve feats.

Heroic Ki<br>
Your skill at mixing the arcane arts with martial skill are such that you inspire others with your heroics.<br>Prerequisites: Monk 5<br>Benefit: You gain the benefits of the Heroism spell at all times. These benefits improve to those of Greater Heroism once you obtain 11 levels in Monk. This bonus will not stack with the Heroism or Greater Heroism spells. This feat has no effect if you take any non-Monk levels.

Epic Diamond Soul<br>
Prerequisites: Diamond Soul, Heroic Ki<br>Benefit: A monk gains spell resistance equal to his current monk level + 14 as long as he does not have levels in any other class.

Fist of the Heavens<br>
Your favor with the Gods has gifted you with the ability to wreathe your fists or quarterstaff with sacred flames.<br>Prerequisites: Ki strike (Adamantine), Intuitive Attack<br>Benefit: You add your Wisdom modifier as divine damage to your unarmed and quarterstaff attacks (total bonus may not exceed 1/2 your Monk level or +6, whichever is lower). This bonus caps at +2 if you multiclass. Unarmed Combat Mastery now deals divine damage.

Lightning Strikes Twice<br>
Prerequisites: Monk 5<br>You gain an attack bonus with unarmed and quarterstaff attacks, starting at +1. This bonus improves by +1 for every 5 additional monk levels (to a maximum of +4 at 20th level). You also deal an additional 2 points of lightning damage on your unarmed or quarterstaff attacks. This bonus is valid when Unarmed Combat Mastery is valid. If you take levels in any class except monk you will only gain the lightning damage bonus.

Channel Positive Energy<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: When a paladin reaches 4th level, she gains the supernatural ability to channel positive energy. Using this ability consumes two uses of her turn undead ability. Channeling energy causes a burst that affects all creatures in a 30-foot radius centered on the cleric. The amount of damage dealt or healed is equal to 1d6 points of damage plus 1d6 points of damage for every two paladin levels beyond 1st (2d6 at 3rd, 3d6 at 5th, and so on). At 20th level the Paladin always heals or harms for the maximum value. Undead creatures will take damage from the channeled energy and will receive a Will save to halve the damage. The DC of this save is equal to 10 + 1/2 the paladin's level + the paladin's Charisma modifier. Classes which stack for Turn Undead count as Paladin for this ability.

Hero's Call<br>
You drive your allies even harder with tales of the greatest heroes in the land.<br>Prerequisites: Skald 1<br>Benefit: Your adrenaline boost ability now always provides twice the bonus as though you and your allies were always below 50% health. In addition, your allies (but not yourself) gain a +1 bonus to Dodge AC. When you use the Adrenaline Boost ability, you become immune to silence for the duration.

Improved Hero's Call<br>
You personalize your encouragement to your allies, driving them on as though they were the great heroes of lore.<br>Prerequisites: Skald 1, Hero's Call<br>Benefit: Your adrenaline boost ability now always provides twice the bonus as though you and your allies were always below 50% health. If they actually are below 50% health they instead gain three times the bonus. In addition, your allies (but not yourself) gain a +1 bonus to Dodge AC and attack bonus.

Improved Inspire Heroics<br>
Prerequisites: Skald 5<br>Benefit: Your Inspire Heroics (Health) ability now provides an additional point of regeneration. Your Inspire Heroics (Aggression) ability now provides an additional point of damage. Your Inspire Heroics (Protection) ability now provides an additional point of Dodge AC.

Greater Inspire Heroics<br>
Prerequisites: Skald 11, Improved Inspire Heroics<br>Benefit: Your Inspire Heroics (Show of Arms) ability now provides an additional +2 enhance bonus to Strength and Dexterity. Your Inspire Heroics ability now provides +1 to Will saves for every five levels of Skald (maximum +6 at 30th).

Inspire Heroics (Toughness)<br>
Prerequisites: Skald 1<br>Benefit: Your inspire heroics ability now also provides 1% physical damage immunity for every 2 levels of Skald (maximum +15% to blunt, piercing, and slashing at 30th level). When you use the Adrenaline Boost ability, you become immune to being stunned for the duration.

Curse of Shadows<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 1st level, a Vengeance Taker can expend a use of their Hexblade Curse to cloud their targets vision with shadows. The shadows cause the target to suffer a 5% miss chance when attacking. This penalty improves to 10% if the Hexblade has Greater Hexblade's Curse, 15% if they have Dire Hexblade's Curse, and 20% if they have Epic Hexblade's Curse.

Curse of Blood<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 4th level, a Vengeance Taker learns to inflict a terrible curse that causes the target to bleed profusely from their skin. By expending a use of Hexblade's Curse, the target suffers bleeding that deals 1 point of damage for every Vengeance Taker level with the curse lasting for 1 round per level of Vengeance Taker. This curse is potent enough that only the lingering curse ability can extend the duration and even then it only extends the duration by 50%.

Curse of Shattering<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 7th level, a Vengeance Taker learns to master the Curse of Lethargy and now adds her Vengeance Taker level to the DC.

Empowered Curses<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 9th level, a Vengeance Taker completes her mastery of curses and learns to a new phrase to any curse she uses. She gains a +2 DC bonus as well as +2 rounds to the duration of any curses she uses. In addition, any curse she uses causes her enemies to suffer 2 negative levels. This additional penalty will not stack regardless of how many curses are used.

Curse of Life Draining<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 10th level, a Vengeance Taker learns how to recover from her own wounds when using her Harm Touch ability. As long as she is not empowering the Harm Touch, she heals an amount equal to the damage dealt.

Harm Touch<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 2nd level, a Vengeance Taker gains the ability to deliver a retributive Harm Touch against her enemies. This ability has a 30s cooldown and deals 5 points of negative energy damage per level of Vengeance Taker. The Vegeance Taker gains a number of temporary hitpoints equal to the damage dealt.

Vampiric Blades<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 3rd level, a Vengeance Taker learns to channel her unending fury into her weapon, allowing her blows in combat to drive her further and further along the path to vengeance. She gains a +1 vampiric bonus for every 3 Vengeance Taker levels which lasts for 10 minutes.

Empower Harm Touch<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Specifics: At 6th level, a Vengeance Taker learns how to empower her Harm Touch with more negative energy but the strain of channeling this power comes with a cost. The damage and number of temporary hitpoints done by Harm Touch is doubled although the Vengeance Taker suffers 2 points per class level in hit point damage before the temporary hitpoints are applied.

Arcane Servant of Darkness<br>
Type of Feat: Spellcasting<br>Prerequisites: Hexblade 5, Blackguard 1<br>Benefit: Your Blackguard levels count as Hexblade levels for determining the Hexblade caster level, curse uses per day, curses known, and hexblade level for curse DC. If you have the Practiced Caster (Hexblade) feat then Blackguard levels will count as Hexblade levels for spells known as well as providing a +1 DC bonus for curses and the demoralize opponent ability.

Arbalest Spellcasting Class (Ranger)<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Arbalest level 1.<br>Specifics: At each level, an arbalest gains new spells per day (and spells known, if applicable) as if she had also gained a Ranger level.<br>Use: Automatic.

Crossbow Combat Style<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Ranger 2<br>Specifics: You have chosen to train in the art of the crossbow. As your ranger level increases, you gain the following bonus feats: Rapid Reload at 2nd level, Crossbow Sniper at 6th level (full bonus), Crossbow Mastery at 11th level, and Epic Rapid Reload at 21st. You gain the benefit of these feats even if you do not meet their prerequisites.<br>Use: Automatic.

Crossbow Mastery<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Requirements: Dex 15, Point-Blank Shot, Rapid Reload, Rapid Shot.<br>Benefit: When using a crossbow you gain a bonus attack. This bonus attack stacks with Epic Rapid Reload.<br>Use: Automatic.

Epic Rapid Reload<br>
Type of Feat: General<br>Requirements: Rapid Reload, Level 21+<br>Benefit: When using a crossbow you gain a bonus attack. This bonus attack stacks with Crossbow Mastery.<br>Use: Automatic.

Shadow Combat Style<br>
Type of Feat: Class<br>Prerequisite: Ranger 2<br>Specifics: You have chosen to train in the art of the shadows. As your ranger level increases, you gain the following bonus feats: Sneak Attack 1d6 at 2nd level, Sneak Attack 2d6 at 6th level, Sneak Attack 3d6 at 11th level, and Bane of Enemies at 21st. You gain the benefit of these feats even if you do not meet their prerequisites. Sneak Attack stacks with other sources of Sneak Attack. <br>Use: Automatic.
